`FEATURES.md`:

```md
# Lenrete 1.1.8

A lightweight client-side utility mod for **Minecraft 26.2** (Fabric, Java 25).
Everything runs on the client — no server-side component, no extra dependencies beyond Fabric API.

---

## Highlights

- **43 modules** across 7 categories, all individually configurable and bindable
- **Two complete GUI themes** — a modern panel interface and a Classic draggable-tab menu, switchable live
- **Fully customizable HUD** — watermark, module list, and info readouts with text/color animations
- **Config profiles** with save/load/export, plus chat commands and Tab-completion

---

## GUI

Open with **Right Shift**.

### Themes
Switch instantly from the ClickGUI settings — no restart, no reopen. The choice persists in the config.

**Modern** — the default. Rounded panels, category rail, search bar, favorites, per-module setting expanders, drag-to-move and edge-resize, color pickers, staggered open animations.

**Classic** — an entirely different menu in the old-school style: one draggable tab panel per category, left-click to toggle, right-click to fold settings open, collapsible panels with saved positions. Includes its own search (just start typing) that flattens every category into one results list.

### Themeable
Colors, corner radius, opacity, gradients, text shadow, separators and animation speed all come from the active theme. Both themes expose their own settings, and the irrelevant set hides automatically when you switch.

### Background animations
10 animated backdrops shared by both menus: Particles, Waves, Starfield, Rain, Grid, Snow, Matrix, Aurora, Bubbles, Fireflies.

### Other
- Search with live enabled-state indicators
- Favorites with drag-to-reorder
- Per-module keybinding directly in the GUI
- Notification toasts (position, duration and fade configurable)
- Selectable client logo (static or animated), with a five-click easter egg
- Works while standing in a nether portal

---

## Modules

### Combat (7)
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| KillAura | Attacks entities in range automatically, respecting the vanilla attack cooldown |
| AimAssist | Smoothly pulls aim toward the nearest valid target |
| Criticals | Sends micro-hop packets so ground attacks register as critical hits |
| Reach | Extends block and entity interaction range |
| TargetTP | Teleports onto an enemy the moment you hit it — multiple strafe modes, optional camera lock |
| MaceHit | Spikes up before a mace smash so fall damage one-shots the target |
| SpearHit | Dashes at the target at top speed with a spear and strikes on arrival |

### Movement (14)
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| Flight | Creative-style flight, with optional auto-disable on damage |
| Speed | Multiplies horizontal movement velocity |
| Sprint | Automatically keeps sprinting when moving forward |
| Step | Walk up blocks taller than the vanilla step height |
| Spider | Climb walls by holding into them |
| NoFall | Cancels fall damage |
| AirJump | Jump again in mid-air (limited or unlimited) |
| AutoWalk | Holds the movement key so you keep walking automatically |
| InventoryMove | Walk around while an inventory screen is open |
| Freecam | Detaches the camera to fly around while your real body stays put and renders in place |
| ClickTP | Press a key to blink where you are aiming — optional unlimited reach and move-packet mode |
| EntityControl | Steer rideable entities (horses, boats, minecarts) with your movement keys |
| EntitySpeed | Increases the movement speed of the mount you are riding |
| EntityFly | Lets your mount fly freely with your movement keys |

### Render (6)
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| ESP | 2D boxes around entities through walls — Box / Corners / Filled styles |
| StorageESP | Boxes around chests, barrels, shulkers and ender chests (double chests merged) |
| ItemESP | Boxes and tags on dropped items — name, count, enchants, durability, distance |
| Nametags | Name and health tags above entities |
| Fullbright | Overrides brightness/gamma for maximum visibility |
| FovChanger | Overrides field of view, including past the vanilla 110 limit |

### Player (7)
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| AutoEat | Eats from the hotbar when hunger or health drops low |
| AutoTool | Switches to the best hotbar tool before breaking a block |
| AutoRespawn | Respawns automatically after dying |
| FastBreak | Removes the pause between breaking blocks |
| FastPlace | Removes the delay between placing blocks and using items |
| InventoryCleaner | Drops configured junk items from the hotbar |
| AntiHunger | Reduces exhaustion drain by a configurable percentage |

### World (2)
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| ChunkBorders | Draws the boundaries of the chunk you are standing in |
| GhostHand | Open chests and other containers through walls by aiming at them |

### Misc (5)
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| ClickGUI | Position, size, colors, themes and notifications for the menu |
| Nuker | Breaks nearby blocks within a radius |
| Blinker | Holds back your movement from the server, then releases it all at once |
| AutoMessage | Sends a configured message in chat when toggled on |
| AutoSign | Fills and confirms sign edit screens with configured text |

### HUD (1)
Watermark, enabled-module list and info readouts, each placeable in any screen corner.

- **Watermark**: custom text with `{text} {name} {version} {fps} {ping}` tokens in any order
- **Text animations**: Static, Fade, Typewriter, Blink, Scroll, Pulse — speed, delay, loop, direction
- **Color animations**: Static, Rainbow, Gradient, Fade, Pulse — speed, saturation, brightness, gradient colors
- **Selectable image**, static or animated
- **Info lines**: FPS, coordinates, facing, speed, ping, armor, effects, clock, biome, session time
- Rainbow module list, text shadow toggle, background panel, scale and per-corner placement

---

## Config & commands

- Settings save automatically to `config/lenrete.json` (with an automatic `.bak`)
- **Profiles**: create, switch, save, load, delete, plus import/export
- **Chat commands** (prefix `.`), with Tab-completion:
  - `.help` — list commands
  - `.toggle <module>` — toggle a module
  - `.bind <module> <key>` — bind a module
  - `.config list|save|load` — manage profiles
- Offline account manager for usernames (offline-mode servers only — **no passwords, tokens, or Microsoft login are handled or stored**)

---

## Technical notes

- Minecraft **26.2**, Fabric Loader 0.16+, Fabric API, **Java 25**
- Built against Mojang's official mappings — no obfuscated or intermediary names in the source or jar
- Mixins kept to a minimum (6 total) and each injection is fail-soft, so a mapping change degrades a feature instead of crashing the game
- Modules are discovered by annotation and their settings by reflection, so adding one is a single class
- Themes are pluggable: a new skin is one class and one registration line

---

## Notes

Client-side utility features like these are detectable and against the rules on most public servers.
Use them on your own server, in singleplayer, or where the server rules allow it.
Modules that rely on lenient server-side validation (GhostHand, ClickTP packet mode, Reach) will be
limited or blocked outright by strict anti-cheat.

```

`README.md`:

```md
# Lenrete

A client-side **Fabric utility/cheat client** for **Minecraft 26.2** (Java 25, unobfuscated Mojang mappings). Lenrete provides a modular in-game menu with configurable modules across combat, movement, render, world, player, and HUD categories, plus a fully themeable click GUI.

> **For singleplayer and private/own servers.** Most modules are trivially detected by server-side anti-cheat and will get you banned on public servers. Test in singleplayer first.

- **Mod ID:** `lenrete`
- **Package:** `dev.dd.lenrete`
- **Minecraft:** 26.2 · **Loader:** Fabric · **Java:** 25
- **Menu key:** `Right Shift` (rebindable per-module inside the GUI)

---

## Interface

### Click GUI
- Draggable, resizable panel (drag the top bar to move, bottom-right corner to resize) — position and size persist across restarts.
- Left category rail: **Favorites**, the six module categories, **Config**, and **Accounts**.
- Two-column module grid with inline, expandable settings; long descriptions wrap.
- **Mouse-wheel scrolling** when a category overflows the panel.
- **Per-module keybinds:** each card has a bind badge — left-click to bind a key, right-click to unbind (shown as readable key names).
- **Fully customizable colors** (background, panel, border, text, accent) with a real **HSV color picker** and preset swatches; separate opacity sliders.
- **Search:** start typing anywhere to fuzzy-search module names, descriptions, and settings.
- **Background animations:** None / Particles / Waves / Starfield / Rain / Grid, plus delta-time panel/hover/toggle animations and a Reduce Animations option.
- Remembers your last-open tab, and opens during nether-portal transitions.

### In-game HUD
Watermark (with icon), enabled-module list, and an info block with toggles for **coordinates, FPS, facing direction, speed, ping, armor, and potion effects**. Each of the three groups can be placed in any screen corner; adjustable scale, text color, and an optional rainbow module list. Auto-hides with the F3 debug screen.

### Config & Accounts
- **Config profiles:** create, switch, delete, import/export (via clipboard); auto-saved to JSON.
- **Account manager:** stores a list of **offline usernames** for offline-mode servers. **No passwords, tokens, or Microsoft login are ever handled or stored** — it only tracks plain usernames and shows the current session name.

### Chat commands
Prefix `.` with Tab autocomplete: `.help`, `.toggle <module>`, `.bind <module> <key>`, `.config <save|load|list> <name>`. Toast notifications for toggles, config saves, and errors.

---

## Modules

### Combat
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| **KillAura** | Auto-attacks entities in range, respecting the vanilla attack cooldown. Player/Hostile/Passive filters, range, delay, max targets. |
| **AimAssist** | Smoothly pulls your aim toward the nearest target within a configurable FOV/range. |
| **Criticals** | Packet/mini-jump micro-hops so ground attacks land as critical hits. |
| **Reach** | Extends block and entity interaction range (works in singleplayer; servers validate reach). |
| **TargetTP** | Blinks you onto an enemy the instant you hit it; optional **strafe-around** mode circles the target so it can't track you. Drives off KillAura hits too. |
| **MaceHit** | Spikes you up to a safe air position before a mace smash so fall damage one-shots the target. |
| **SpearHit** | Dashes at the target at top speed with a spear/trident and strikes on arrival. |

### Movement
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| **Sprint** | Auto-sprints while moving forward. |
| **Speed** | Multiplies horizontal movement velocity. |
| **Flight** | Creative-style flight with anti-kick and disable-on-damage. |
| **NoFall** | Cancels fall damage via ground-status packets. |
| **Step** | Walk up blocks taller than the vanilla step height. |
| **Spider** | Climb walls by holding into them. |
| **AutoWalk** | Holds forward/backward so you keep moving. |
| **AirJump** | Jump again in mid-air — limited count or unlimited. |
| **ClickTP** | Press the teleport key to blink to where you're aiming; optional unlimited reach. |
| **InventoryMove** | Walk around while an inventory/container screen is open. *(mixin)* |
| **Freecam** | Detaches the camera to fly around while your body stays put. *(experimental)* |

### Render
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| **ESP** | 2D boxes around entities through walls — Box / CS:GO-style Corners / Filled, per-type colors, distance. Smooth interpolated tracking. |
| **StorageESP** | Boxes around chests, barrels, shulkers, ender chests; double chests merge into one box. |
| **Nametags** | Name + health + distance tags above entities. |
| **Fullbright** | Overrides gamma for full brightness. |
| **FovChanger** | Overrides field of view, including past the vanilla 110 cap. |

### World
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| **ChunkBorders** | Draws the boundaries of the chunk you're standing in. |

### Player
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| **AutoTool** | Switches to the best hotbar tool before breaking a block. |
| **AutoEat** | Eats from the hotbar when hunger/health drops low. |
| **InventoryCleaner** | Drops configured junk items from the hotbar. |
| **AutoRespawn** | Respawns automatically after dying. |
| **FastPlace** | Removes the block-place / item-use delay. |
| **FastBreak** | Removes the pause between breaking blocks. |

### Misc
| Module | Description |
|---|---|
| **Nuker** | Breaks nearby blocks within a radius (nearest/all, respect-tool). |
| **AutoMessage** | Sends a configured chat message when toggled on. |
| **AutoSign** | Fills and confirms sign edit screens with configured text. |
| **Blinker** | Withholds your movement packets from the server, then releases them at once when disabled. *(mixin)* |
| **ClickGUI** | Position, size, colors, and notification settings for the menu. |

*(**HUD** lives in its own HUD category.)*

---

## Architecture notes
- **Module system:** annotation-driven discovery (`@ModuleMetadata`), a generic `Setting<T>` system (boolean, int, double, enum, color, keybind, String) with automatic JSON persistence and per-module keybinds. A guarded tick/render loop disables a misbehaving module instead of crashing the game.
- **Rendering:** ESP/overlays are 2D screen-projected onto the HUD layer using the camera's view-projection matrix (no dependence on the 3D world-render pipeline), with off-screen clipping to stay safe.
- **Mixins:** minimal, `require = 0` (a missed injection is skipped rather than crashing launch) — used for InventoryMove (`ClientInput`) and Blinker (packet send).
- **Mappings:** official Mojang names only (Minecraft 26.2 is unobfuscated) — no Yarn/Intermediary, no refmap.

## Building
```bash
gradlew build
```
The jar is produced at `build/libs/Lenrete-<version>.jar`.

---

*Educational project. Use responsibly and only where you have permission.*

```

`build.gradle`:

```gradle
plugins {
	id 'net.fabricmc.fabric-loom' version "${loom_version}"
	id 'maven-publish'
}

version = project.mod_version
group = project.maven_group

base {
	archivesName = 'Lenrete'
}

repositories {
	mavenCentral()
}

dependencies {
	minecraft "com.mojang:minecraft:${project.minecraft_version}"
	implementation "net.fabricmc:fabric-loader:${project.loader_version}"
	implementation "net.fabricmc.fabric-api:fabric-api:${project.fabric_api_version}"
}

processResources {
	inputs.property "version", project.version

	filesMatching("fabric.mod.json") {
		expand "version": project.version
	}
}

tasks.withType(JavaCompile).configureEach {
	it.options.release = 25
}

java {
	toolchain {
		languageVersion = JavaLanguageVersion.of(25)
	}
	sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_25
	targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_25
}

jar {
	from("LICENSE") {
		rename { "${it}_${project.name}" }
	}
}

```

`gradle.properties`:

```properties
# Memory settings
org.gradle.jvmargs=-Xmx2G
org.gradle.parallel=true
org.gradle.configuration-cache=false
org.gradle.java.home=C:/Users/Anwender/AppData/Roaming/PrismLauncher/java/java-runtime-epsilon

# Fabric Properties
minecraft_version=26.2
loader_version=0.19.3
loom_version=1.17-SNAPSHOT

# Mod Properties

`gradlew`:

```
#!/bin/sh

#
# Copyright © 2015 the original authors.
#
# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
# You may obtain a copy of the License at
#
#      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
#
# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
# limitations under the License.
#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#

##############################################################################
#
#   Gradle start up script for POSIX generated by Gradle.
#
#   Important for running:
#
#   (1) You need a POSIX-compliant shell to run this script. If your /bin/sh is
#       noncompliant, but you have some other compliant shell such as ksh or
#       bash, then to run this script, type that shell name before the whole
#       command line, like:
#
#           ksh Gradle
#
#       Busybox and similar reduced shells will NOT work, because this script
#       requires all of these POSIX shell features:
#         * functions;
#         * expansions «$var», «${var}», «${var:-default}», «${var+SET}»,
#           «${var#prefix}», «${var%suffix}», and «$( cmd )»;
#         * compound commands having a testable exit status, especially «case»;
#         * various built-in commands including «command», «set», and «ulimit».
#
#   Important for patching:
#
#   (2) This script targets any POSIX shell, so it avoids extensions provided
#       by Bash, Ksh, etc; in particular arrays are avoided.
#
#       The "traditional" practice of packing multiple parameters into a
#       space-separated string is a well documented source of bugs and security
#       problems, so this is (mostly) avoided, by progressively accumulating
#       options in "$@", and eventually passing that to Java.
#
#       Where the inherited environment variables (DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS,
#       and GRADLE_OPTS) rely on word-splitting, this is performed explicitly;
#       see the in-line comments for details.
#
#       There are tweaks for specific operating systems such as AIX, CygWin,
#       Darwin, MinGW, and NonStop.
#
#   (3) This script is generated from the Groovy template
#       https://github.com/gradle/gradle/blob/3d91ce3b8caaf77ad09f381f43615b715b53f72c/platforms/jvm/plugins-application/src/main/resources/org/gradle/api/internal/plugins/unixStartScript.txt
#       within the Gradle project.
#
#       You can find Gradle at https://github.com/gradle/gradle/.
#
##############################################################################

# Attempt to set APP_HOME

# Resolve links: $0 may be a link
app_path=$0

# Need this for daisy-chained symlinks.
while
    APP_HOME=${app_path%"${app_path##*/}"}  # leaves a trailing /; empty if no leading path
    [ -h "$app_path" ]
do
    ls=$( ls -ld "$app_path" )
    link=${ls#*' -> '}
    case $link in             #(
      /*)   app_path=$link ;; #(
      *)    app_path=$APP_HOME$link ;;
    esac
done

# This is normally unused
# shellcheck disable=SC2034
APP_BASE_NAME=${0##*/}
# Discard cd standard output in case $CDPATH is set (https://github.com/gradle/gradle/issues/25036)
APP_HOME=$( cd -P "${APP_HOME:-./}" > /dev/null && printf '%s\n' "$PWD" ) || exit

# Use the maximum available, or set MAX_FD != -1 to use that value.
MAX_FD=maximum

warn () {
    echo "$*"
} >&2

die () {
    echo
    echo "$*"
    echo
    exit 1
} >&2

# OS specific support (must be 'true' or 'false').
cygwin=false
msys=false
darwin=false
nonstop=false
case "$( uname )" in                #(
  CYGWIN* )         cygwin=true  ;; #(
  Darwin* )         darwin=true  ;; #(
  MSYS* | MINGW* )  msys=true    ;; #(
  NONSTOP* )        nonstop=true ;;
esac



# Determine the Java command to use to start the JVM.
if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
    if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
        # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java
    else
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/bin/java
    fi
    if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: $JAVA_HOME

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
else
    JAVACMD=java
    if ! command -v java >/dev/null 2>&1
    then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH.

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
fi

# Increase the maximum file descriptors if we can.
if ! "$cygwin" && ! "$darwin" && ! "$nonstop" ; then
    case $MAX_FD in #(
      max*)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -H is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        MAX_FD=$( ulimit -H -n ) ||
            warn "Could not query maximum file descriptor limit"
    esac
    case $MAX_FD in  #(
      '' | soft) :;; #(
      *)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -n is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        ulimit -n "$MAX_FD" ||
            warn "Could not set maximum file descriptor limit to $MAX_FD"
    esac
fi

# Collect all arguments for the java command, stacking in reverse order:
#   * args from the command line
#   * the main class name
#   * -classpath
#   * -D...appname settings
#   * --module-path (only if needed)
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and GRADLE_OPTS environment variables.

# For Cygwin or MSYS, switch paths to Windows format before running java
if "$cygwin" || "$msys" ; then
    APP_HOME=$( cygpath --path --mixed "$APP_HOME" )

    JAVACMD=$( cygpath --unix "$JAVACMD" )

    # Now convert the arguments - kludge to limit ourselves to /bin/sh
    for arg do
        if
            case $arg in                                #(
              -*)   false ;;                            # don't mess with options #(
              /?*)  t=${arg#/} t=/${t%%/*}              # looks like a POSIX filepath
                    [ -e "$t" ] ;;                      #(
              *)    false ;;
            esac
        then
            arg=$( cygpath --path --ignore --mixed "$arg" )
        fi
        # Roll the args list around exactly as many times as the number of
        # args, so each arg winds up back in the position where it started, but
        # possibly modified.
        #
        # NB: a `for` loop captures its iteration list before it begins, so
        # changing the positional parameters here affects neither the number of
        # iterations, nor the values presented in `arg`.
        shift                   # remove old arg
        set -- "$@" "$arg"      # push replacement arg
    done
fi


# Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS='"-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"'

# Collect all arguments for the java command:
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and optsEnvironmentVar are not allowed to contain shell fragments,
#     and any embedded shellness will be escaped.
#   * For example: A user cannot expect ${Hostname} to be expanded, as it is an environment variable and will be
#     treated as '${Hostname}' itself on the command line.

set -- \
        "-Dorg.gradle.appname=$APP_BASE_NAME" \
        -jar "$APP_HOME/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar" \
        "$@"

# Stop when "xargs" is not available.
if ! command -v xargs >/dev/null 2>&1
then
    die "xargs is not available"
fi

# Use "xargs" to parse quoted args.
#
# With -n1 it outputs one arg per line, with the quotes and backslashes removed.
#
# In Bash we could simply go:
#
#   readarray ARGS < <( xargs -n1 <<<"$var" ) &&
#   set -- "${ARGS[@]}" "$@"
#
# but POSIX shell has neither arrays nor command substitution, so instead we
# post-process each arg (as a line of input to sed) to backslash-escape any
# character that might be a shell metacharacter, then use eval to reverse
# that process (while maintaining the separation between arguments), and wrap
# the whole thing up as a single "set" statement.
#
# This will of course break if any of these variables contains a newline or
# an unmatched quote.
#

eval "set -- $(
        printf '%s\n' "$DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS $JAVA_OPTS $GRADLE_OPTS" |
        xargs -n1 |
        sed ' s~[^-[:alnum:]+,./:=@_]~\\&~g; ' |
        tr '\n' ' '
    )" '"$@"'

exec "$JAVACMD" "$@"

```

`gradlew.bat`:

```bat
@rem
@rem Copyright 2015 the original author or authors.

@rem Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
@rem you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
@rem You may obtain a copy of the License at
@rem
@rem      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
@rem
@rem Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
@rem distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
@rem WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
@rem See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
@rem limitations under the License.
@rem
@rem SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
@rem

@if "%DEBUG%"=="" @echo off
@rem ##########################################################################
@rem
@rem  Gradle startup script for Windows
@rem
@rem ##########################################################################

@rem Set local scope for the variables, and ensure extensions are enabled
setlocal EnableExtensions

set DIRNAME=%~dp0
if "%DIRNAME%"=="" set DIRNAME=.
@rem This is normally unused
set APP_BASE_NAME=%~n0
set APP_HOME=%DIRNAME%

@rem Resolve any "." and ".." in APP_HOME to make it shorter.
for %%i in ("%APP_HOME%") do set APP_HOME=%%~fi

@rem Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
set DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS="-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"

@rem Find java.exe
if defined JAVA_HOME goto findJavaFromJavaHome

set JAVA_EXE=java.exe
%JAVA_EXE% -version >NUL 2>&1
if %ERRORLEVEL% equ 0 goto execute

echo. 1>&2
echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH. 1>&2
echo. 1>&2
echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the 1>&2
echo location of your Java installation. 1>&2

"%COMSPEC%" /c exit 1

:findJavaFromJavaHome
set JAVA_HOME=%JAVA_HOME:"=%
set JAVA_EXE=%JAVA_HOME%/bin/java.exe

if exist "%JAVA_EXE%" goto execute

echo. 1>&2
echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: %JAVA_HOME% 1>&2
echo. 1>&2
echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the 1>&2
echo location of your Java installation. 1>&2

"%COMSPEC%" /c exit 1

:execute
@rem Setup the command line



@rem Execute Gradle
@rem endlocal doesn't take effect until after the line is parsed and variables are expanded
@rem which allows us to clear the local environment before executing the java command
endlocal & "%JAVA_EXE%" %DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS% %JAVA_OPTS% %GRADLE_OPTS% "-Dorg.gradle.appname=%APP_BASE_NAME%" -jar "%APP_HOME%\gradle\wrapper\gradle-wrapper.jar" %* & call :exitWithErrorLevel

:exitWithErrorLevel
@rem Use "%COMSPEC%" /c exit to allow operators to work properly in scripts
"%COMSPEC%" /c exit %ERRORLEVEL%

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete;

import dev.dd.lenrete.command.CommandAutocomplete;
import dev.dd.lenrete.command.CommandManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.account.AccountManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.config.ConfigManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.gui.ClickGuiScreen;
import dev.dd.lenrete.hud.HudRenderer;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.render.EspRenderer;
import net.fabricmc.api.ClientModInitializer;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

public class Lenrete implements ClientModInitializer {
    public static final String MOD_ID = "lenrete";
    public static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(MOD_ID);

    @Override
    public void onInitializeClient() {
        LOGGER.info("Initializing Lenrete utility client...");

        // Initialize ModuleManager (registers modules and Fabric API tick/render events)
        ModuleManager.getInstance();

        // Register GUI themes (Modern is the default)
        dev.dd.lenrete.gui.theme.ThemeManager.init();

        // Load saved configuration
        ConfigManager.loadConfig();

        // Load the offline account list
        AccountManager.load();

        // Register the HUD: watermark, enabled module list, coordinates/FPS
        HudRenderer.init();

        // Register the click GUI keybind (Right Shift)
        ClickGuiScreen.register();

        // Register toast notifications, bottom-right by default
        ToastManager.init();

        // Register the 2D ESP/Nametags/world overlay renderer
        EspRenderer.init();

        // Register the "." chat command system and its Tab autocomplete
        CommandManager.register();
        CommandAutocomplete.register();

        LOGGER.info("Lenrete initialized successfully!");
    }
}

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/account/AccountManager.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.account;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import com.google.gson.JsonArray;
import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;
import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.User;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileReader;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Manages a list of offline (cracked) usernames. This deliberately handles no
 * passwords, tokens, or Microsoft authentication — it only stores plain usernames
 * and best-effort applies the selected one to the client's offline session for
 * offline-mode servers. Selecting an account never signs in anywhere.
 */
public final class AccountManager {
    private static final Gson GSON = new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create();
    private static final File FILE = FabricLoader.getInstance().getConfigDir().resolve("lenrete_accounts.json").toFile();

    private static final List<String> ACCOUNTS = new ArrayList<>();
    private static String selected = "";

    private AccountManager() {
    }

    public static void load() {
        if (!FILE.exists()) {
            return;
        }
        try (FileReader reader = new FileReader(FILE, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
            JsonObject root = JsonParser.parseReader(reader).getAsJsonObject();
            ACCOUNTS.clear();
            if (root.has("accounts")) {
                for (JsonElement element : root.getAsJsonArray("accounts")) {
                    ACCOUNTS.add(element.getAsString());
                }
            }
            if (root.has("selected")) {
                selected = root.get("selected").getAsString();
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Failed to load Lenrete accounts", e);
        }
    }

    public static void save() {
        try {
            JsonObject root = new JsonObject();
            JsonArray array = new JsonArray();
            for (String account : ACCOUNTS) {
                array.add(account);
            }
            root.add("accounts", array);
            root.addProperty("selected", selected);

            File parent = FILE.getParentFile();
            if (parent != null && !parent.exists()) {
                parent.mkdirs();
            }
            try (FileWriter writer = new FileWriter(FILE, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
                GSON.toJson(root, writer);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Failed to save Lenrete accounts", e);
        }
    }

    public static List<String> getAccounts() {
        return new ArrayList<>(ACCOUNTS);
    }

    /** The username of the currently active session. Never exposes tokens or credentials. */
    public static String getCurrentUsername() {
        try {
            return Minecraft.getInstance().getUser().getName();
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            return "unknown";
        }
    }

    public static String getSelected() {
        return selected;
    }

    public static void add(String name) {
        String trimmed = name.trim();
        if (!trimmed.isEmpty() && !ACCOUNTS.contains(trimmed)) {
            ACCOUNTS.add(trimmed);
            save();
        }
    }

    public static void remove(String name) {
        if (ACCOUNTS.remove(name)) {
            if (name.equals(selected)) {
                selected = "";
            }
            save();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Best-effort swap of the client's offline session name. Only affects offline-mode
     * servers and only takes effect on the next connection; may silently no-op if the
     * runtime forbids the reflective field write.
     *
     * @return true if the offline name was applied.
     */
    public static boolean select(String name) {
        selected = name;
        save();

        try {
            UUID offlineId = UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes(("OfflinePlayer:" + name).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            User current = Minecraft.getInstance().getUser();
            User replacement = new User(name, offlineId, "0", Optional.empty(), Optional.empty());

            Field userField = findUserField();
            if (userField == null) {
                return false;
            }
            userField.setAccessible(true);
            userField.set(Minecraft.getInstance(), replacement);
            return true;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.warn("Offline account name could not be applied at runtime", e);
            return false;
        }
    }

    private static Field findUserField() {
        for (Field field : Minecraft.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
            if (field.getType() == User.class) {
                return field;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/command/CommandAutocomplete.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.command;

import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import dev.dd.lenrete.config.ConfigManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.screen.v1.ScreenEvents;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.screen.v1.ScreenKeyboardEvents;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.components.EditBox;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.ChatScreen;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

public final class CommandAutocomplete {
    private static final String[] COMMANDS = {"help", "toggle", "bind", "config"};
    private static final String[] CONFIG_ACTIONS = {"save", "load", "list"};

    private static Field inputField;

    private CommandAutocomplete() {
    }

    public static void register() {
        try {
            inputField = ChatScreen.class.getDeclaredField("input");
            inputField.setAccessible(true);
        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.warn("Command autocomplete unavailable: could not access ChatScreen input field", e);
            inputField = null;
            return;
        }

        ScreenEvents.AFTER_INIT.register((client, screen, width, height) -> {
            if (screen instanceof ChatScreen chatScreen) {
                ScreenKeyboardEvents.afterKeyPress(screen).register((s, keyEvent) -> {
                    if (keyEvent.key() == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_TAB) {
                        applyCompletion(chatScreen);
                    }
                });
            }
        });
    }

    private static void applyCompletion(ChatScreen chatScreen) {
        if (inputField == null) {
            return;
        }

        EditBox editBox = getEditBox(chatScreen);
        if (editBox == null) {
            return;
        }

        String value = editBox.getValue();
        if (!value.startsWith(".")) {
            return;
        }

        boolean trailingSpace = value.endsWith(" ");
        String[] tokens = value.substring(1).trim().split("\\s+", -1);
        int tokenIndex = trailingSpace ? tokens.length : tokens.length - 1;
        String partial = trailingSpace || tokens.length == 0 ? "" : tokens[tokens.length - 1];

        List<String> candidates = candidatesFor(tokens, tokenIndex);
        String match = firstPrefixMatch(candidates, partial);
        if (match == null) {
            return;
        }

        int completeCount = trailingSpace ? tokens.length : tokens.length - 1;
        StringBuilder rebuilt = new StringBuilder(".");
        for (int i = 0; i < completeCount; i++) {
            rebuilt.append(tokens[i]).append(' ');
        }
        rebuilt.append(match).append(' ');

        editBox.setValue(rebuilt.toString());
        editBox.setCursorPosition(rebuilt.length());
    }

    private static List<String> candidatesFor(String[] tokens, int tokenIndex) {
        List<String> candidates = new ArrayList<>();

        if (tokenIndex == 0) {
            candidates.addAll(List.of(COMMANDS));
            return candidates;
        }

        String commandName = tokens[0].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (tokenIndex == 1) {
            if (commandName.equals("toggle") || commandName.equals("bind")) {
                for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
                    candidates.add(module.getName());
                }
            } else if (commandName.equals("config")) {
                candidates.addAll(List.of(CONFIG_ACTIONS));
            }
            return candidates;
        }

        if (tokenIndex == 2 && commandName.equals("config") && tokens.length > 1) {
            String action = tokens[1].toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
            if (action.equals("save") || action.equals("load")) {
                candidates.addAll(ConfigManager.listProfiles());
            }
        }
        return candidates;
    }

    private static String firstPrefixMatch(List<String> candidates, String partial) {
        String lowerPartial = partial.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        for (String candidate : candidates) {
            if (candidate.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).startsWith(lowerPartial)) {
                return candidate;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static EditBox getEditBox(ChatScreen chatScreen) {
        try {
            return (EditBox) inputField.get(chatScreen);
        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/command/CommandManager.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.command;

import dev.dd.lenrete.config.ConfigManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.message.v1.ClientSendMessageEvents;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

public final class CommandManager {
    private static final String PREFIX = ".";

    private CommandManager() {
    }

    public static void register() {
        ClientSendMessageEvents.ALLOW_CHAT.register(CommandManager::onChat);
    }

    private static boolean onChat(String message) {
        if (!message.startsWith(PREFIX)) {
            return true;
        }

        List<String> tokens = tokenize(message.substring(PREFIX.length()));
        if (tokens.isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }

        String name = tokens.get(0).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        List<String> args = tokens.subList(1, tokens.size());

        switch (name) {
            case "help" -> handleHelp();
            case "toggle" -> handleToggle(args);
            case "bind" -> handleBind(args);
            case "config" -> handleConfig(args);
            default -> feedback("Unknown command: ." + name + " (try .help)");
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static void handleHelp() {
        feedback(".help");
        feedback(".toggle <module>");
        feedback(".bind <module> <key>");
        feedback(".config <save|load|list> <name>");
    }

    private static void handleToggle(List<String> args) {
        if (args.isEmpty()) {
            ToastManager.show("Usage: .toggle <module>");
            return;
        }
        Module module = findModule(args.get(0));
        if (module == null) {
            ToastManager.show("Unknown module: " + args.get(0));
            return;
        }
        ModuleManager.getInstance().toggleModule(module);
    }

    private static void handleBind(List<String> args) {
        if (args.size() < 2) {
            ToastManager.show("Usage: .bind <module> <key>");
            return;
        }
        Module module = findModule(args.get(0));
        if (module == null) {
            ToastManager.show("Unknown module: " + args.get(0));
            return;
        }
        int key = resolveKey(args.get(1));
        if (key < 0) {
            ToastManager.show("Unknown key: " + args.get(1));
            return;
        }
        module.setKey(key);
        ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
        ToastManager.show(module.getName() + " bound to " + args.get(1).toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
    }

    private static void handleConfig(List<String> args) {
        if (args.isEmpty()) {
            ToastManager.show("Usage: .config <save|load|list> <name>");
            return;
        }

        String action = args.get(0).toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        switch (action) {
            case "list" -> {
                for (String profileName : ConfigManager.listProfiles()) {
                    boolean active = profileName.equals(ConfigManager.getActiveProfile());
                    feedback((active ? "* " : "  ") + profileName);
                }
            }
            case "save" -> {
                if (args.size() < 2) {
                    ToastManager.show("Usage: .config save <name>");
                    return;
                }
                ConfigManager.saveAs(args.get(1));
                ToastManager.show("Saved profile '" + args.get(1) + "'");
            }
            case "load" -> {
                if (args.size() < 2) {
                    ToastManager.show("Usage: .config load <name>");
                    return;
                }
                ConfigManager.switchProfile(args.get(1));
                ToastManager.show("Loaded profile '" + args.get(1) + "'");
            }
            default -> ToastManager.show("Usage: .config <save|load|list> <name>");
        }
    }

    private static Module findModule(String name) {
        Module prefixMatch = null;
        for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
            if (module.getName().equalsIgnoreCase(name)) {
                return module;
            }
            if (prefixMatch == null && module.getName().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).startsWith(name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT))) {
                prefixMatch = module;
            }
        }
        return prefixMatch;
    }

    private static int resolveKey(String name) {
        try {
            Field field = GLFW.class.getField("GLFW_KEY_" + name.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT));
            return field.getInt(null);
        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
            return -1;
        }
    }

    private static List<String> tokenize(String text) {
        List<String> tokens = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String token : text.trim().split("\\s+")) {
            if (!token.isEmpty()) {
                tokens.add(token);
            }
        }
        return tokens;
    }

    private static void feedback(String message) {
        LocalPlayer player = Minecraft.getInstance().player;
        if (player != null) {
            player.sendSystemMessage(Component.literal("[Lenrete] > " + message));
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/config/ConfigManager.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.config;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;
import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.ColorValue;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Keybind;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.FileReader;
import java.io.FileWriter;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

public class ConfigManager {
    private static final Gson GSON = new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create();
    private static final File CONFIG_FILE = FabricLoader.getInstance().getConfigDir().resolve("lenrete.json").toFile();
    private static final long SAVE_DELAY_MS = 500L;
    private static String activeProfile = "default";
    private static long saveAfterMs = -1L;

    public static void loadConfig() {
        if (!CONFIG_FILE.exists()) {
            saveConfig();
            return;
        }

        String savedTheme = null;
        try (FileReader reader = new FileReader(CONFIG_FILE)) {
            JsonObject root = JsonParser.parseReader(reader).getAsJsonObject();
            if (root.has("activeProfile")) {
                activeProfile = root.get("activeProfile").getAsString();
            }
            if (root.has("theme")) {
                savedTheme = root.get("theme").getAsString();
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Failed to read active Lenrete profile", e);
        }

        loadActiveProfileModules();
        applyTheme(savedTheme);
    }

    /** Activates the saved theme; unknown or missing names fall back to Modern. */
    private static void applyTheme(String savedTheme) {
        dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc.ClickGuiModule gui =
                ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc.ClickGuiModule.class);

        String name = savedTheme;
        if (name == null && gui != null) {
            // Older configs without the top-level key: honor the module setting if present.
            name = gui.getThemeName();
        }
        if (name == null || !dev.dd.lenrete.gui.theme.ThemeManager.setActive(name)) {
            dev.dd.lenrete.gui.theme.ThemeManager.setActive("Modern");
        }
        if (gui != null) {
            gui.setThemeName(dev.dd.lenrete.gui.theme.ThemeManager.getActive().name());
        }
    }

    public static void saveConfigDebounced() {
        saveAfterMs = System.currentTimeMillis() + SAVE_DELAY_MS;
    }

    public static void flushPendingSave() {
        if (saveAfterMs > 0L && System.currentTimeMillis() >= saveAfterMs) {
            saveAfterMs = -1L;
            saveConfig();
        }
    }

    public static void saveConfig() {
        JsonObject profiles = readProfilesObject();
        JsonObject profile = new JsonObject();
        JsonObject modules = new JsonObject();

        for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
            modules.add(module.getName(), writeModule(module));
        }
        profile.add("modules", modules);
        profiles.add(activeProfile, profile);

        persistProfilesObject(profiles);
        Lenrete.LOGGER.info("Saved Lenrete profile {}", activeProfile);
    }

    public static void saveAs(String name) {
        JsonObject profiles = readProfilesObject();
        JsonObject profile = new JsonObject();
        JsonObject modules = new JsonObject();

        for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
            modules.add(module.getName(), writeModule(module));
        }
        profile.add("modules", modules);
        profiles.add(name, profile);

        persistProfilesObject(profiles);
    }

    public static String getActiveProfile() {
        return activeProfile;
    }

    public static List<String> listProfiles() {
        JsonObject profiles = readProfilesObject();
        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>(profiles.keySet());
        if (!names.contains(activeProfile)) {
            names.add(activeProfile);
        }
        Collections.sort(names);
        return names;
    }

    public static void switchProfile(String name) {
        if (name.equals(activeProfile)) {
            return;
        }
        saveConfig();
        activeProfile = name;
        loadActiveProfileModules();
    }

    public static void deleteProfile(String name) {
        if (name.equals(activeProfile)) {
            return;
        }
        JsonObject profiles = readProfilesObject();
        if (!profiles.has(name)) {
            return;
        }
        profiles.remove(name);
        persistProfilesObject(profiles);
    }

    private static void loadActiveProfileModules() {
        if (!CONFIG_FILE.exists()) {
            resetModulesToDefaults();
            saveConfig();
            return;
        }

        try (FileReader reader = new FileReader(CONFIG_FILE)) {
            JsonObject root = JsonParser.parseReader(reader).getAsJsonObject();
            JsonObject profiles = root.getAsJsonObject("profiles");
            if (profiles == null || !profiles.has(activeProfile)) {
                resetModulesToDefaults();
                saveConfig();
                return;
            }

            JsonObject modules = profiles.getAsJsonObject(activeProfile).getAsJsonObject("modules");
            if (modules == null) {
                resetModulesToDefaults();
                return;
            }

            for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
                try {
                    loadModule(modules, module);
                } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                    // One module failing to restore must not abort loading the rest.
                    Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Failed to load module {} from config", module.getName(), e);
                }
            }

            Lenrete.LOGGER.info("Loaded Lenrete profile {}", activeProfile);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Failed to load Lenrete config", e);
        }
    }

    private static void resetModulesToDefaults() {
        for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
            module.setEnabled(false);
            module.setFavorite(false);
            module.setFavoriteOrder(0);
            for (Setting<?> setting : module.getSettings()) {
                resetSetting(setting);
            }
        }
    }

    private static <T> void resetSetting(Setting<T> setting) {
        setting.set(setting.getDefaultValue());
    }

    private static JsonObject readProfilesObject() {
        if (!CONFIG_FILE.exists()) {
            return new JsonObject();
        }

        try (FileReader reader = new FileReader(CONFIG_FILE)) {
            JsonObject root = JsonParser.parseReader(reader).getAsJsonObject();
            JsonObject profiles = root.getAsJsonObject("profiles");
            return profiles != null ? profiles : new JsonObject();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Failed to read existing Lenrete profiles", e);
            return new JsonObject();
        }
    }

    private static void persistProfilesObject(JsonObject profiles) {
        try {
            backupExistingConfig();

            JsonObject root = new JsonObject();
            root.addProperty("activeProfile", activeProfile);
            root.addProperty("theme", dev.dd.lenrete.gui.theme.ThemeManager.getActive().name());
            root.add("profiles", profiles);

            File parent = CONFIG_FILE.getParentFile();
            if (parent != null && !parent.exists() && !parent.mkdirs()) {
                throw new IOException("Could not create " + parent);
            }

            try (FileWriter writer = new FileWriter(CONFIG_FILE)) {
                GSON.toJson(root, writer);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Failed to persist Lenrete profiles", e);
        }
    }

    public static String exportActiveProfile() {
        JsonObject exported = new JsonObject();
        JsonObject modules = new JsonObject();
        for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
            modules.add(module.getName(), writeModule(module));
        }
        exported.addProperty("profile", activeProfile);
        exported.add("modules", modules);
        return GSON.toJson(exported);
    }

    public static void importActiveProfile(String json) {
        JsonObject root = JsonParser.parseString(json).getAsJsonObject();
        JsonObject modules = root.getAsJsonObject("modules");
        if (modules == null) {
            return;
        }

        for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
            loadModule(modules, module);
        }
        saveConfigDebounced();
    }

    private static void loadModule(JsonObject modules, Module module) {
        if (!modules.has(module.getName())) {
            return;
        }

        JsonObject object = modules.getAsJsonObject(module.getName());
        if (object.has("enabled")) {
            module.setEnabled(object.get("enabled").getAsBoolean());
        }
        if (object.has("key")) {
            module.setKey(object.get("key").getAsInt());
        }
        if (object.has("favorite")) {
            module.setFavorite(object.get("favorite").getAsBoolean());
        }
        if (object.has("favoriteOrder")) {
            module.setFavoriteOrder(object.get("favoriteOrder").getAsInt());
        }
        if (object.has("settings")) {
            JsonObject settings = object.getAsJsonObject("settings");
            for (Setting<?> setting : module.getSettings()) {
                loadSetting(settings, setting);
            }
        }
    }

    private static JsonObject writeModule(Module module) {
        JsonObject object = new JsonObject();
        JsonObject settings = new JsonObject();
        object.addProperty("enabled", module.isEnabled());
        object.addProperty("key", module.getKey());
        object.addProperty("favorite", module.isFavorite());
        object.addProperty("favoriteOrder", module.getFavoriteOrder());
        for (Setting<?> setting : module.getSettings()) {
            writeSetting(settings, setting);
        }
        object.add("settings", settings);
        return object;
    }

    private static <T> void writeSetting(JsonObject settings, Setting<T> setting) {
        T value = setting.get();
        if (value instanceof Boolean booleanValue) {
            settings.addProperty(setting.getName(), booleanValue);
        } else if (value instanceof Number numberValue) {
            settings.addProperty(setting.getName(), numberValue);
        } else if (value instanceof Character characterValue) {
            settings.addProperty(setting.getName(), characterValue);
        } else if (value instanceof ColorValue colorValue) {
            settings.addProperty(setting.getName(), colorValue.argb());
        } else if (value instanceof Keybind keybind) {
            settings.addProperty(setting.getName(), keybind.code());
        } else if (value != null) {
            settings.addProperty(setting.getName(), value.toString());
        }
    }

    private static <T> void loadSetting(JsonObject settings, Setting<T> setting) {
        if (!settings.has(setting.getName())) {
            return;
        }

        JsonElement value = settings.get(setting.getName());
        Class<T> type = setting.getType();
        if (type == Boolean.class) {
            setting.set(type.cast(value.getAsBoolean()));
        } else if (type == Integer.class) {
            setting.set(type.cast(value.getAsInt()));
        } else if (type == Double.class) {
            setting.set(type.cast(value.getAsDouble()));
        } else if (type == String.class) {
            setting.set(type.cast(value.getAsString()));
        } else if (type == ColorValue.class) {
            setting.set(type.cast(new ColorValue(value.getAsInt())));
        } else if (type == Keybind.class) {
            setting.set(type.cast(new Keybind(value.getAsInt())));
        } else if (type.isEnum()) {
            loadEnumSetting(setting, value.getAsString());
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
    private static <T> void loadEnumSetting(Setting<T> setting, String value) {
        Class<T> type = setting.getType();
        try {
            setting.set((T) Enum.valueOf((Class<? extends Enum>) type.asSubclass(Enum.class), value));
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            // A constant that no longer exists (renamed option) falls back to the default
            // rather than aborting the rest of the module's settings.
            setting.set(setting.getDefaultValue());
        }
    }

    private static void backupExistingConfig() throws IOException {
        if (!CONFIG_FILE.exists()) {
            return;
        }

        File backup = new File(CONFIG_FILE.getParentFile(), "lenrete.json.bak");
        Files.copy(CONFIG_FILE.toPath(), backup.toPath(), StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
    }
}

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/event/EventBus.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.event;

import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class EventBus {
    private final Map<Class<?>, List<Subscriber>> subscribers = new HashMap<>();

    public void register(Object listener) {
        for (Method method : listener.getClass().getDeclaredMethods()) {
            Subscribe subscribe = method.getAnnotation(Subscribe.class);
            if (subscribe == null || method.getParameterCount() != 1) {
                continue;
            }

            method.setAccessible(true);
            Class<?> eventType = method.getParameterTypes()[0];
            subscribers.computeIfAbsent(eventType, ignored -> new ArrayList<>())
                    .add(new Subscriber(listener, method, subscribe.priority()));
            subscribers.get(eventType).sort(Comparator.comparingInt(Subscriber::priority).reversed());
        }
    }

    public void post(Object event) {
        List<Subscriber> listeners = subscribers.get(event.getClass());
        if (listeners == null) {
            return;
        }

        for (Subscriber subscriber : listeners) {
            try {
                subscriber.method.invoke(subscriber.owner, event);
            } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
                Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Event subscriber failed for {}", event.getClass().getSimpleName(), e);
            }
        }
    }

    private record Subscriber(Object owner, Method method, int priority) {
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/event/Subscribe.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.event;

import java.lang.annotation.ElementType;
import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target(ElementType.METHOD)
public @interface Subscribe {
    int priority() default 0;
}

```
