`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/gui/ClassicGuiScreen.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.gui;

import dev.dd.lenrete.config.ConfigManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.gui.theme.Theme;
import dev.dd.lenrete.gui.theme.ThemeManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.ColorValue;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Keybind;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen;
import net.minecraft.client.input.KeyEvent;
import net.minecraft.client.input.MouseButtonEvent;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.EnumMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * The Classic theme's menu: one draggable tab panel per category, in the style of the
 * old-school cheat clients. Left-click a module to toggle it, right-click to fold its
 * settings open underneath, drag a title bar to move the panel, and click the title bar's
 * marker to collapse it.
 *
 * <p>Colors, edges and text shadow all come from the active {@link Theme}, so this layout
 * follows whatever theme opened it.
 */
public class ClassicGuiScreen extends Screen implements LenreteScreen {
    private static final int TITLE_HEIGHT = 14;
    private static final int SETTING_HEIGHT = 11;
    private static final int PANEL_GAP = 6;
    private static final int MARGIN = 6;
    private static final double OPEN_MS = 160;
    private static final double CLOSE_MS = 120;

    // Metrics come from the Classic settings, refreshed each frame.
    private int PANEL_WIDTH = 108;
    private int ROW_HEIGHT = 12;

    private double openProgress;
    private double screenOpenElapsedMs;
    private long lastFrameNanos = -1;
    private boolean closing;
    private double frameAlpha = 1.0;

    // Search: typing filters every category into one results panel.
    private static final int SEARCH_WIDTH = 150;
    private static final int SEARCH_HEIGHT = 14;
    private final StringBuilder searchQuery = new StringBuilder();
    private boolean searchFocused;

    private final ClickGuiModuleAccess gui = new ClickGuiModuleAccess();
    private final Map<Category, Panel> panels = new EnumMap<>(Category.class);
    private final List<Module> expanded = new ArrayList<>();

    private Panel draggingPanel;
    private int dragOffsetX;
    private int dragOffsetY;

    private Setting<?> draggingSlider;
    private Rect draggingSliderRect;
    private Setting<Keybind> capturingKeybind;
    private Module capturingModuleBind;

    // Theme values, refreshed every frame so a theme switch applies instantly.
    private int cBackground;
    private int cPanel;
    private int cHeader;
    private int cHeaderEnd;
    private int cAccent;
    private int cTextPrimary;
    private int cTextSecondary;
    private int cTextDisabled;
    private int cOutline;
    private int cOutlineHover;
    private int cToggleOff;
    private boolean cShadow;
    private boolean cGradientHeader;
    private boolean cSeparator;

    public ClassicGuiScreen() {
        super(Component.literal("Lenrete"));
        buildPanels();
        ToastManager.show("Lenrete opened");
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ layout state

    private void buildPanels() {
        Category[] categories = Category.values();
        for (int i = 0; i < categories.length; i++) {
            Category category = categories[i];
            // Default: a row of panels across the top, wrapping is handled on first render.
            panels.put(category, new Panel(MARGIN + i * (PANEL_WIDTH + PANEL_GAP), MARGIN));
        }
        loadPanelLayout();
    }

    private void loadPanelLayout() {
        String raw = gui.classicPanels();
        if (raw.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        for (String entry : raw.split(";")) {
            String[] parts = entry.split(",");
            if (parts.length < 4) {
                continue;
            }
            try {
                Category category = Category.valueOf(parts[0]);
                Panel panel = panels.get(category);
                if (panel != null) {
                    panel.x = Integer.parseInt(parts[1]);
                    panel.y = Integer.parseInt(parts[2]);
                    panel.collapsed = Boolean.parseBoolean(parts[3]);
                }
            } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
                // A renamed or removed category simply keeps its default position.
            }
        }
    }

    private void savePanelLayout() {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (Map.Entry<Category, Panel> entry : panels.entrySet()) {
            if (sb.length() > 0) {
                sb.append(';');
            }
            Panel panel = entry.getValue();
            sb.append(entry.getKey().name()).append(',')
                    .append(panel.x).append(',')
                    .append(panel.y).append(',')
                    .append(panel.collapsed);
        }
        gui.setClassicPanels(sb.toString());
        ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
    }

    @Override
    protected void init() {
        super.init();
        // Keep every panel reachable if the window shrank since they were positioned.
        for (Panel panel : panels.values()) {
            panel.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(panel.x, Math.max(0, this.width - PANEL_WIDTH)));
            panel.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(panel.y, Math.max(0, this.height - TITLE_HEIGHT)));
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void tick() {
        super.tick();
        // While the menu is open, keep a nether portal from teleporting you out of it.
        if (this.minecraft != null && this.minecraft.player != null) {
            this.minecraft.player.portalProcess = null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isPauseScreen() {
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public void requestClose() {
        if (closing) {
            return;
        }
        closing = true;
        savePanelLayout();
    }

    @Override
    public void onClose() {
        savePanelLayout();
        super.onClose();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ rendering

    private void refreshTheme() {
        Theme theme = ThemeManager.getActive();
        if (theme == null) {
            return;
        }
        PANEL_WIDTH = gui.panelWidth();
        ROW_HEIGHT = Math.max(this.font.lineHeight + 2, theme.itemHeight());
        cBackground = theme.background();
        cPanel = scaleAlpha(theme.panelBackground(), theme.panelOpacity());
        cHeader = theme.header();
        cHeaderEnd = theme.headerGradientEnd();
        cAccent = theme.accent();
        cTextPrimary = theme.textPrimary();
        cTextSecondary = theme.textSecondary();
        cTextDisabled = theme.textDisabled();
        cOutline = theme.outline();
        cOutlineHover = theme.outlineHover();
        cToggleOff = theme.disabledToggle();
        cShadow = theme.useTextShadow();
        cGradientHeader = theme.useGradientHeader();
        cSeparator = theme.useItemSeparator();
    }

    @Override
    public void extractRenderState(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTick) {
        refreshTheme();

        long now = System.nanoTime();
        double deltaMs = lastFrameNanos < 0 ? 0 : Math.min((now - lastFrameNanos) / 1_000_000.0, 100);
        lastFrameNanos = now;
        screenOpenElapsedMs += deltaMs;

        // Same open/close feel as the modern menu, scaled by the theme's animation speed.
        Theme theme = ThemeManager.getActive();
        double speed = theme != null ? Math.max(0.01, theme.animationSpeedMultiplier()) : 1.0;
        double step = deltaMs / ((closing ? CLOSE_MS : OPEN_MS) * speed);
        openProgress = closing
                ? Math.max(0, openProgress - step)
                : Math.min(1, openProgress + step);
        if (closing && openProgress <= 0.001) {
            if (this.minecraft != null) {
                this.minecraft.setScreenAndShow(null);
            }
            return;
        }
        frameAlpha = openProgress;

        g.fill(0, 0, this.width, this.height, applyAlpha(cBackground, frameAlpha));
        GuiBackground.render(g, gui.backgroundAnimation(), this.width, this.height,
                screenOpenElapsedMs, cAccent, frameAlpha);

        // Panels scale up from 92% as they fade in, mirroring the modern pop-open.
        var pose = g.pose();
        pose.pushMatrix();
        float scale = (float) (0.92 + 0.08 * openProgress);
        float cx = this.width / 2f;
        float cy = this.height / 2f;
        pose.translate(cx, cy);
        pose.scale(scale);
        pose.translate(-cx, -cy);

        if (hasQuery()) {
            renderSearchResults(g, mouseX, mouseY);
        } else {
            for (Category category : Category.values()) {
                renderPanel(g, category, panels.get(category), mouseX, mouseY);
            }
        }

        renderSearchBar(g);

        String hint = "Left-click: toggle   Right-click: settings   Drag title: move   RightShift: close";
        text(g, hint, MARGIN, this.height - 10, applyAlpha(cTextSecondary, frameAlpha));

        pose.popMatrix();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ search

    private boolean hasQuery() {
        return searchQuery.length() > 0;
    }

    private Rect searchBarRect() {
        return new Rect((this.width - SEARCH_WIDTH) / 2, MARGIN, SEARCH_WIDTH, SEARCH_HEIGHT);
    }

    private Rect searchPanelRect() {
        int width = Math.max(PANEL_WIDTH, SEARCH_WIDTH);
        return new Rect((this.width - width) / 2, MARGIN + SEARCH_HEIGHT + PANEL_GAP, width, 0);
    }

    private void renderSearchBar(GuiGraphicsExtractor g) {
        Rect bar = searchBarRect();
        g.fill(bar.x, bar.y, bar.x + bar.w, bar.y + bar.h, applyAlpha(cPanel, frameAlpha));
        outline(g, bar.x, bar.y, bar.w, bar.h,
                applyAlpha(searchFocused ? cAccent : cOutline, frameAlpha));

        String label = hasQuery() || searchFocused
                ? searchQuery + (searchFocused ? "_" : "")
                : "Search...";
        int color = hasQuery() ? cTextPrimary : cTextSecondary;
        textClipped(g, label, bar.x + 4, bar.y + (bar.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2,
                bar.w - 8, applyAlpha(color, frameAlpha));
    }

    /** Modules from every category whose name matches the query, in one flat panel. */
    private List<Module> searchMatches() {
        List<Module> matches = new ArrayList<>();
        String query = searchQuery.toString().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
            if (module.getName().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(query)) {
                matches.add(module);
            }
        }
        return matches;
    }

    private void renderSearchResults(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        Rect base = searchPanelRect();
        List<Module> matches = searchMatches();

        int x = base.x;
        int y = base.y;
        int width = base.w;

        if (cGradientHeader) {
            g.fillGradient(x, y, x + width, y + TITLE_HEIGHT,
                    applyAlpha(cHeader, frameAlpha), applyAlpha(cHeaderEnd, frameAlpha));
        } else {
            g.fill(x, y, x + width, y + TITLE_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(cHeader, frameAlpha));
        }
        outline(g, x, y, width, TITLE_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(cOutline, frameAlpha));
        textClipped(g, "Results (" + matches.size() + ")", x + 4,
                y + (TITLE_HEIGHT - this.font.lineHeight) / 2 + 1, width - 8,
                applyAlpha(0xFF0A0A0E, frameAlpha));

        int bodyHeight = Math.max(ROW_HEIGHT, matches.size() * ROW_HEIGHT);
        int bodyY = y + TITLE_HEIGHT;
        g.fill(x, bodyY, x + width, bodyY + bodyHeight, applyAlpha(cPanel, frameAlpha));
        outline(g, x, bodyY, width, bodyHeight, applyAlpha(cOutline, frameAlpha));

        if (matches.isEmpty()) {
            textClipped(g, "no matches", x + 4, bodyY + (ROW_HEIGHT - this.font.lineHeight) / 2,
                    width - 8, applyAlpha(cTextDisabled, frameAlpha));
            return;
        }

        int rowY = bodyY;
        for (Module module : matches) {
            boolean hovered = mouseX >= x && mouseX < x + width && mouseY >= rowY && mouseY < rowY + ROW_HEIGHT;
            if (hovered) {
                g.fill(x + 1, rowY, x + width - 1, rowY + ROW_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(cOutlineHover, frameAlpha));
            }

            int color = module.isUnavailable()
                    ? cTextDisabled
                    : (module.isEnabled() ? cAccent : cTextSecondary);
            int textY = rowY + (ROW_HEIGHT - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;

            // Category tag on the right so results stay identifiable across categories.
            String tag = capitalize(module.getCategory().name());
            int tagWidth = this.font.width(tag);
            textClipped(g, module.getName(), x + 4, textY, width - tagWidth - 12, applyAlpha(color, frameAlpha));
            text(g, tag, x + width - 4 - tagWidth, textY, applyAlpha(cTextDisabled, frameAlpha));

            rowY += ROW_HEIGHT;
            if (cSeparator) {
                g.fill(x + 1, rowY - 1, x + width - 1, rowY, applyAlpha(cOutline, frameAlpha));
            }
        }
    }

    /** Click handling for the search bar and results list; true if the click was consumed. */
    private boolean handleSearchClick(double mx, double my, int button) {
        if (searchBarRect().contains(mx, my)) {
            searchFocused = true;
            if (button == 1) {
                searchQuery.setLength(0);
            }
            return true;
        }

        if (!hasQuery()) {
            return false;
        }

        Rect base = searchPanelRect();
        int rowY = base.y + TITLE_HEIGHT;
        for (Module module : searchMatches()) {
            if (mx >= base.x && mx < base.x + base.w && my >= rowY && my < rowY + ROW_HEIGHT) {
                if (button == 0) {
                    ModuleManager.getInstance().toggleModule(module);
                }
                return true;
            }
            rowY += ROW_HEIGHT;
        }

        // Clicking the results panel's chrome still counts as inside the search UI.
        return mx >= base.x && mx < base.x + base.w
                && my >= base.y && my < rowY;
    }

    private static int applyAlpha(int color, double alpha) {
        int baseAlpha = (color >>> 24) == 0 ? 0xFF : (color >>> 24);
        int scaled = (int) Math.round(baseAlpha * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha)));
        return (scaled << 24) | (color & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }

    private void renderPanel(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Category category, Panel panel, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        int x = panel.x;
        int y = panel.y;

        // Title bar: gradient when the theme wants one, otherwise flat accent.
        if (cGradientHeader) {
            g.fillGradient(x, y, x + PANEL_WIDTH, y + TITLE_HEIGHT,
                    applyAlpha(cHeader, frameAlpha), applyAlpha(cHeaderEnd, frameAlpha));
        } else {
            g.fill(x, y, x + PANEL_WIDTH, y + TITLE_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(cHeader, frameAlpha));
        }
        outline(g, x, y, PANEL_WIDTH, TITLE_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(cOutline, frameAlpha));

        String title = capitalize(category.name());
        int titleTextColor = applyAlpha(0xFF0A0A0E, frameAlpha);
        textClipped(g, title, x + 4, y + (TITLE_HEIGHT - this.font.lineHeight) / 2 + 1,
                PANEL_WIDTH - 16, titleTextColor);
        String marker = panel.collapsed ? "+" : "-";
        text(g, marker, x + PANEL_WIDTH - 9, y + (TITLE_HEIGHT - this.font.lineHeight) / 2 + 1, titleTextColor);

        if (panel.collapsed) {
            return;
        }

        List<Module> modules = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModulesByCategory(category);
        int bodyHeight = panelBodyHeight(modules);
        int bodyY = y + TITLE_HEIGHT;
        g.fill(x, bodyY, x + PANEL_WIDTH, bodyY + bodyHeight, applyAlpha(cPanel, frameAlpha));
        outline(g, x, bodyY, PANEL_WIDTH, bodyHeight, applyAlpha(cOutline, frameAlpha));

        int rowY = bodyY;
        for (Module module : modules) {
            boolean hovered = mouseX >= x && mouseX < x + PANEL_WIDTH && mouseY >= rowY && mouseY < rowY + ROW_HEIGHT;
            if (hovered) {
                // Classic hover: a solid full-width bar, no fade.
                g.fill(x + 1, rowY, x + PANEL_WIDTH - 1, rowY + ROW_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(cOutlineHover, frameAlpha));
            }

            int color;
            if (module.isUnavailable()) {
                color = cTextDisabled;
            } else {
                color = module.isEnabled() ? cAccent : cTextSecondary;
            }
            String label = module.isUnavailable() ? module.getName() + " (off)" : module.getName();
            int textY = rowY + (ROW_HEIGHT - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
            textClipped(g, label, x + 4, textY, PANEL_WIDTH - 14, applyAlpha(color, frameAlpha));

            if (isExpanded(module)) {
                text(g, "-", x + PANEL_WIDTH - 8, textY, applyAlpha(cTextSecondary, frameAlpha));
            }
            rowY += ROW_HEIGHT;

            if (cSeparator) {
                g.fill(x + 1, rowY - 1, x + PANEL_WIDTH - 1, rowY, applyAlpha(cOutline, frameAlpha));
            }

            if (isExpanded(module)) {
                rowY = renderSettings(g, module, x, rowY, mouseX, mouseY);
            }
        }
    }

    private int renderSettings(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Module module, int x, int startY, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        int rowY = startY;

        // Module keybind first, matching the old clients' "bind" row.
        boolean capturing = capturingModuleBind == module;
        String bindLabel = "Bind: " + (capturing ? "..." : ClickGuiScreen.keyName(module.getKey()));
        g.fill(x + 1, rowY, x + PANEL_WIDTH - 1, rowY + SETTING_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(cPanel, frameAlpha));
        textClipped(g, bindLabel, x + 10, rowY + 2, PANEL_WIDTH - 16,
                applyAlpha(capturing ? cAccent : cTextSecondary, frameAlpha));
        rowY += SETTING_HEIGHT;

        for (Setting<?> setting : module.getSettings()) {
            if (!setting.isVisible()) {
                continue;
            }
            g.fill(x + 1, rowY, x + PANEL_WIDTH - 1, rowY + SETTING_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(cPanel, frameAlpha));
            renderSetting(g, setting, x, rowY);
            rowY += SETTING_HEIGHT;
        }
        return rowY;
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private void renderSetting(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Setting<?> setting, int x, int rowY) {
        Class<?> type = setting.getType();
        String name = setting.getName();
        int textY = rowY + 2;

        // Every label is clipped to the space left inside the panel, so nothing overflows.
        int labelX = x + 10;
        int fullWidth = PANEL_WIDTH - 16;

        if (isThemeSetting(setting)) {
            Theme active = ThemeManager.getActive();
            textClipped(g, "Theme: " + (active != null ? active.name() : "Modern"),
                    labelX, textY, fullWidth, applyAlpha(cAccent, frameAlpha));
            return;
        }

        if (type == Boolean.class) {
            boolean value = Boolean.TRUE.equals(((Setting<Boolean>) setting).get());
            int color = applyAlpha(value ? cAccent : cTextSecondary, frameAlpha);
            String mark = value ? "[x]" : "[ ]";
            int markWidth = this.font.width(mark);
            textClipped(g, name, labelX, textY, fullWidth - markWidth - 4, color);
            text(g, mark, x + PANEL_WIDTH - 6 - markWidth, textY, color);
        } else if (type == ColorValue.class) {
            ColorValue value = ((Setting<ColorValue>) setting).get();
            textClipped(g, name, labelX, textY, fullWidth - 18, applyAlpha(cTextSecondary, frameAlpha));
            int swatchX = x + PANEL_WIDTH - 20;
            g.fill(swatchX, rowY + 2, swatchX + 12, rowY + 9,
                    applyAlpha(value != null ? value.argb() : 0xFFFFFFFF, frameAlpha));
            outline(g, swatchX, rowY + 2, 12, 7, applyAlpha(cOutline, frameAlpha));
        } else if (type == Keybind.class) {
            Keybind value = ((Setting<Keybind>) setting).get();
            boolean capturing = capturingKeybind == setting;
            String label = capturing ? "..." : ClickGuiScreen.keyName(value != null ? value.code() : -1);
            label = truncate(label, fullWidth / 2);
            int labelWidth = this.font.width(label);
            textClipped(g, name, labelX, textY, fullWidth - labelWidth - 4, applyAlpha(cTextSecondary, frameAlpha));
            text(g, label, x + PANEL_WIDTH - 6 - labelWidth, textY,
                    applyAlpha(capturing ? cAccent : cTextPrimary, frameAlpha));
        } else if (isSlider(setting)) {
            double min = ((Number) setting.getMin()).doubleValue();
            double max = ((Number) setting.getMax()).doubleValue();
            double value = ((Number) setting.get()).doubleValue();
            double ratio = max > min ? (value - min) / (max - min) : 0;
            ratio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ratio));

            String label = type == Integer.class
                    ? name + ": " + (int) value
                    : name + ": " + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.2f", value);
            textClipped(g, label, labelX, textY, fullWidth, applyAlpha(cTextSecondary, frameAlpha));

            // Thin fill bar along the bottom of the row.
            int barX = labelX;
            int barW = fullWidth;
            int barY = rowY + SETTING_HEIGHT - 2;
            g.fill(barX, barY, barX + barW, barY + 1, applyAlpha(cToggleOff, frameAlpha));
            g.fill(barX, barY, barX + (int) Math.round(barW * ratio), barY + 1, applyAlpha(cAccent, frameAlpha));
        } else if (type.isEnum()) {
            Object value = setting.get();
            String label = value == null ? "" : ((Enum<?>) value).name();
            textClipped(g, name + ": " + label, labelX, textY, fullWidth, applyAlpha(cTextSecondary, frameAlpha));
        } else {
            Object value = setting.get();
            textClipped(g, name + ": " + (value == null ? "" : value), labelX, textY, fullWidth,
                    applyAlpha(cTextSecondary, frameAlpha));
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ input

    @Override
    public boolean mouseClicked(MouseButtonEvent event, boolean doubled) {
        if (closing) {
            return true;
        }
        double mx = event.x();
        double my = event.y();
        int button = event.button();

        capturingKeybind = null;
        capturingModuleBind = null;

        if (handleSearchClick(mx, my, button)) {
            return true;
        }
        searchFocused = false;

        // While searching, the category panels are hidden and must not receive clicks.
        if (hasQuery()) {
            return true;
        }

        for (Category category : Category.values()) {
            Panel panel = panels.get(category);
            int x = panel.x;
            int y = panel.y;

            // Title bar: collapse marker, or start dragging.
            if (mx >= x && mx < x + PANEL_WIDTH && my >= y && my < y + TITLE_HEIGHT) {
                if (mx >= x + PANEL_WIDTH - 12 || button == 1) {
                    panel.collapsed = !panel.collapsed;
                    savePanelLayout();
                } else {
                    draggingPanel = panel;
                    dragOffsetX = (int) (mx - x);
                    dragOffsetY = (int) (my - y);
                }
                return true;
            }

            if (panel.collapsed) {
                continue;
            }

            List<Module> modules = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModulesByCategory(category);
            int rowY = y + TITLE_HEIGHT;
            for (Module module : modules) {
                if (mx >= x && mx < x + PANEL_WIDTH && my >= rowY && my < rowY + ROW_HEIGHT) {
                    if (button == 1) {
                        toggleExpanded(module);
                    } else if (button == 0) {
                        ModuleManager.getInstance().toggleModule(module);
                    }
                    return true;
                }
                rowY += ROW_HEIGHT;

                if (isExpanded(module)) {
                    // Bind row.
                    if (mx >= x && mx < x + PANEL_WIDTH && my >= rowY && my < rowY + SETTING_HEIGHT) {
                        if (button == 1) {
                            module.setKey(-1);
                            ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
                        } else {
                            capturingModuleBind = module;
                        }
                        return true;
                    }
                    rowY += SETTING_HEIGHT;

                    for (Setting<?> setting : module.getSettings()) {
                        if (!setting.isVisible()) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        if (mx >= x && mx < x + PANEL_WIDTH && my >= rowY && my < rowY + SETTING_HEIGHT) {
                            handleSettingClick(setting, x, rowY, mx);
                            return true;
                        }
                        rowY += SETTING_HEIGHT;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return super.mouseClicked(event, doubled);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
    private void handleSettingClick(Setting<?> setting, int panelX, int rowY, double mx) {
        Class<?> type = setting.getType();

        if (isThemeSetting(setting)) {
            cycleTheme();
            return;
        }

        if (type == Boolean.class) {
            Setting<Boolean> boolSetting = (Setting<Boolean>) setting;
            boolSetting.set(!Boolean.TRUE.equals(boolSetting.get()));
        } else if (type == Keybind.class) {
            capturingKeybind = (Setting<Keybind>) setting;
            return;
        } else if (type == ColorValue.class) {
            cycleColor((Setting<ColorValue>) setting);
        } else if (isSlider(setting)) {
            draggingSlider = setting;
            draggingSliderRect = new Rect(panelX + 10, rowY, PANEL_WIDTH - 16, SETTING_HEIGHT);
            applySlider(setting, draggingSliderRect, mx);
        } else if (type.isEnum()) {
            Object[] constants = type.getEnumConstants();
            if (constants.length > 0) {
                Object current = setting.get();
                int index = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < constants.length; i++) {
                    if (constants[i] == current) {
                        index = i;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                ((Setting) setting).set(constants[(index + 1) % constants.length]);
            }
        } else {
            // Text settings are edited in the Modern layout; nothing to do here.
            return;
        }
        ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
    }

    /** Steps a color through the shared preset palette — the classic layout has no picker. */
    private void cycleColor(Setting<ColorValue> setting) {
        int[] presets = {0xFF4CE3D0, 0xFFE85C5C, 0xFFE8C15C, 0xFF5CE87A,
                0xFF5C9EE8, 0xFFB05CE8, 0xFFE85CAF, 0xFFE8EAED, 0xFF00CFE8, 0xFF8B5CF6};
        ColorValue current = setting.get();
        int argb = current != null ? current.argb() : presets[0];
        int index = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < presets.length; i++) {
            if (presets[i] == argb) {
                index = i;
                break;
            }
        }
        setting.set(new ColorValue(presets[(index + 1) % presets.length]));
    }

    private void cycleTheme() {
        List<String> names = ThemeManager.names();
        if (names.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        Theme active = ThemeManager.getActive();
        int index = active != null ? names.indexOf(active.name()) : -1;
        String next = names.get((index + 1) % names.size());
        ThemeManager.setActive(next);
        gui.setThemeName(next);
        savePanelLayout();
        ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
        ToastManager.show("Theme: " + next);

        // The new theme may use a different layout entirely — reopen through its factory.
        Screen replacement = ClickGuiScreen.openScreen();
        if (this.minecraft != null && !replacement.getClass().equals(getClass())) {
            this.minecraft.setScreenAndShow(replacement);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseDragged(MouseButtonEvent event, double dragX, double dragY) {
        if (draggingPanel != null) {
            draggingPanel.x = (int) (event.x() - dragOffsetX);
            draggingPanel.y = (int) (event.y() - dragOffsetY);
            return true;
        }
        if (draggingSlider != null && draggingSliderRect != null) {
            applySlider(draggingSlider, draggingSliderRect, event.x());
            return true;
        }
        return super.mouseDragged(event, dragX, dragY);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseReleased(MouseButtonEvent event) {
        if (draggingPanel != null) {
            draggingPanel = null;
            savePanelLayout();
            return true;
        }
        if (draggingSlider != null) {
            draggingSlider = null;
            draggingSliderRect = null;
            ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
            return true;
        }
        return super.mouseReleased(event);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean keyPressed(KeyEvent event) {
        int key = event.key();

        if (capturingKeybind != null) {
            capturingKeybind.set(key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE ? new Keybind(-1) : new Keybind(key));
            ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
            capturingKeybind = null;
            return true;
        }
        if (capturingModuleBind != null) {
            capturingModuleBind.setKey(key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE ? -1 : key);
            ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
            capturingModuleBind = null;
            return true;
        }
        if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
            // Escape clears an active search first, and only then closes the menu.
            if (hasQuery() || searchFocused) {
                searchQuery.setLength(0);
                searchFocused = false;
                return true;
            }
            requestClose();
            return true;
        }
        if (closing) {
            return true;
        }
        if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE && (searchFocused || hasQuery())) {
            if (searchQuery.length() > 0) {
                searchQuery.setLength(searchQuery.length() - 1);
            }
            return true;
        }
        return super.keyPressed(event);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean charTyped(net.minecraft.client.input.CharacterEvent event) {
        int typed = event.codepoint();
        // Typing anywhere starts a search — no need to click the bar first.
        if (typed >= ' ' && typed != 127 && !closing) {
            searchFocused = true;
            searchQuery.appendCodePoint(typed);
            return true;
        }
        return super.charTyped(event);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ helpers

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private void applySlider(Setting<?> setting, Rect track, double mouseX) {
        double min = ((Number) setting.getMin()).doubleValue();
        double max = ((Number) setting.getMax()).doubleValue();
        double ratio = track.w <= 0 ? 0 : (mouseX - track.x) / track.w;
        ratio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ratio));
        double value = min + ratio * (max - min);

        if (setting.getType() == Integer.class) {
            ((Setting<Integer>) setting).set((int) Math.round(value));
        } else {
            ((Setting<Double>) setting).set(value);
        }
    }

    private static boolean isSlider(Setting<?> setting) {
        Class<?> type = setting.getType();
        return (type == Integer.class || type == Double.class)
                && setting.getMin() != null && setting.getMax() != null;
    }

    private boolean isThemeSetting(Setting<?> setting) {
        return gui.themeSetting() == setting;
    }

    private int panelBodyHeight(List<Module> modules) {
        int height = 0;
        for (Module module : modules) {
            height += ROW_HEIGHT;
            if (isExpanded(module)) {
                height += SETTING_HEIGHT;
                for (Setting<?> setting : module.getSettings()) {
                    if (setting.isVisible()) {
                        height += SETTING_HEIGHT;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return Math.max(ROW_HEIGHT, height);
    }

    private boolean isExpanded(Module module) {
        return expanded.contains(module);
    }

    private void toggleExpanded(Module module) {
        if (!expanded.remove(module)) {
            expanded.add(module);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Draws text truncated to {@code maxWidth} with a trailing ellipsis, so nothing ever
     * spills past the edge of a tab panel however narrow it is or however long the label.
     */
    private void textClipped(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, String value, int x, int y, int maxWidth, int color) {
        if (maxWidth <= 0) {
            return;
        }
        text(g, truncate(value, maxWidth), x, y, color);
    }

    private String truncate(String value, int maxWidth) {
        if (this.font.width(value) <= maxWidth) {
            return value;
        }
        int ellipsis = this.font.width("..");
        if (ellipsis > maxWidth) {
            return "";
        }
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        int width = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {
            int charWidth = this.font.width(String.valueOf(value.charAt(i)));
            if (width + charWidth + ellipsis > maxWidth) {
                break;
            }
            sb.append(value.charAt(i));
            width += charWidth;
        }
        return sb + "..";
    }

    private void text(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, String value, int x, int y, int color) {
        g.text(this.font, value, x, y, color, cShadow);
    }

    private void outline(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int x, int y, int w, int h, int color) {
        g.fill(x, y, x + w, y + 1, color);
        g.fill(x, y + h - 1, x + w, y + h, color);
        g.fill(x, y, x + 1, y + h, color);
        g.fill(x + w - 1, y, x + w, y + h, color);
    }

    private static int scaleAlpha(int color, double factor) {
        factor = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, factor));
        int alpha = (int) Math.round((color >>> 24) * factor);
        return (alpha << 24) | (color & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }

    private static String capitalize(String value) {
        String lower = value.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        return lower.isEmpty() ? lower : Character.toUpperCase(lower.charAt(0)) + lower.substring(1);
    }

    private static final class Panel {
        private int x;
        private int y;
        private boolean collapsed;

        private Panel(int x, int y) {
            this.x = x;
            this.y = y;
        }
    }

    private record Rect(int x, int y, int w, int h) {
        boolean contains(double px, double py) {
            return px >= x && px < x + w && py >= y && py < y + h;
        }
    }

    /** Thin wrapper so a missing ClickGUI module can never NPE the screen. */
    private static final class ClickGuiModuleAccess {
        private dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc.ClickGuiModule module() {
            return ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc.ClickGuiModule.class);
        }

        private String classicPanels() {
            var m = module();
            return m != null ? m.getClassicPanels() : "";
        }

        private void setClassicPanels(String value) {
            var m = module();
            if (m != null) {
                m.setClassicPanels(value);
            }
        }

        private void setThemeName(String value) {
            var m = module();
            if (m != null) {
                m.setThemeName(value);
            }
        }

        private Setting<String> themeSetting() {
            var m = module();
            return m != null ? m.getThemeSetting() : null;
        }

        private int panelWidth() {
            var m = module();
            return m != null ? m.getClassicPanelWidth() : 108;
        }

        private dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc.ClickGuiModule.BackgroundAnimation backgroundAnimation() {
            var m = module();
            if (m == null || m.isReduceAnimations()) {
                return null;
            }
            return m.getBackgroundAnimation();
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/gui/ClickGuiScreen.java`:

package dev.dd.lenrete.gui;

public class ClickGuiScreen extends Screen implements LenreteScreen {
    private static final int DEFAULT_WIDTH = 720;

    private static final int MIN_WIDTH = 360;
    private static final int MIN_HEIGHT = 240;

    private static final int RESIZE_LEFT = 1;
    private static final int RESIZE_RIGHT = 2;
    private static final int RESIZE_TOP = 4;
    private static final int RESIZE_BOTTOM = 8;

    private static final int RAIL_WIDTH = 160;
    private static final int GRID_GUTTER = 12;
    private static final int CARD_ROW_SPACING = 8;
    private static final int CLOSE_BUTTON_SIZE = 24;
    private static final int TOGGLE_WIDTH = 28;
    private static final int TOGGLE_HEIGHT = 14;
    private static final int RESIZE_HANDLE_SIZE = 12;

    // Shape values sourced from the active Theme each frame (see refreshTheme).
    private int TOP_BAR_HEIGHT = 48;
    private int PANEL_PADDING = 16;
    private int CARD_HEIGHT = 64;
    private int RAIL_ITEM_HEIGHT = 32;
    private int PANEL_RADIUS = 4;
    private int CONTROL_RADIUS = 2;

    private static final int SETTINGS_ROW_HEIGHT = 20;
    private static final int SETTINGS_TOP_PADDING = 8;
    private static final int SETTINGS_BOTTOM_PADDING = 8;
    private static final int CONTROL_WIDTH = 120;
    private static final int CONTROL_HEIGHT = 14;
    private static final int CHECKBOX_SIZE = 14;
    private static final int SLIDER_TRACK_WIDTH = 70;
    private static final int SLIDER_TRACK_HEIGHT = 6;
    private static final int COLOR_SWATCH_WIDTH = 24;
    private static final int COLOR_SWATCH_HEIGHT = 14;
    private static final int SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT = 24;
    private static final int SEARCH_BOX_HEIGHT = 20;

    private static final double PANEL_OPEN_MS = 160;
    private static final double PANEL_CLOSE_MS = 120;
    private static final double HOVER_MS = 80;
    private static final double TOGGLE_MS = 120;
    private static final double EXPAND_MS = 180;
    private static final double STAGGER_STEP_MS = 15;
    private static final double STAGGER_CAP_MS = 120;
    private static final double STAGGER_REVEAL_MS = 120;

    private static final Identifier ICON_TEXTURE = Identifier.fromNamespaceAndPath(Lenrete.MOD_ID, "textures/gui/perona.png");
    private static final int ICON_SIZE = 24;

    private static final int[] COLOR_PRESETS = {
            0xFF4CE3D0, 0xFFE85C5C, 0xFFE8C15C, 0xFF5CE87A,
            0xFF5C9EE8, 0xFFB05CE8, 0xFFE85CAF, 0xFFE8EAED
    };

    private static boolean keyWasDown;

    // Palette sourced from the active Theme each frame (see refreshTheme). The Modern theme
    // delegates to ClickGuiModule's color settings, so user customization still applies.
    private int COLOR_OVERLAY = 0xF00A0B0D;
    private int COLOR_PANEL = 0xFF121417;
    private int COLOR_BORDER = 0x14FFFFFF;
    private int COLOR_BORDER_HOVER = 0x28FFFFFF;
    private int COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY = 0xFFE8EAED;
    private int COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY = 0xFF8A9099;
    private int COLOR_TEXT_DISABLED = 0xFF8A9099;
    private int COLOR_ACCENT = 0xFF4CE3D0;
    private int COLOR_ACCENT_FILL = 0xFF4CE3D0;
    private int COLOR_ACCENT_TINT = 0x144CE3D0;
    private int COLOR_TOGGLE_ON = 0xFF4CE3D0;
    private int COLOR_TOGGLE_OFF = 0x14FFFFFF;
    private int COLOR_HEADER = 0xFF121417;
    private int COLOR_HEADER_END = 0xFF121417;
    private boolean themeTextShadow;
    private boolean themeGradientHeader;
    private boolean themeItemSeparator;
    private boolean themeAccentEnabledNames;

    private final Set<Module> expandedModules = new HashSet<>();
    private final Animator panelAnimator = new Animator();
    private final Animator closeHoverAnimator = new Animator();
    private final Animator resizeHoverAnimator = new Animator();
    private final Map<Category, Animator> railHoverAnimators = new EnumMap<>(Category.class);
    private final Map<Module, Animator> cardHoverAnimators = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<Module, Animator> toggleAnimators = new HashMap<>();
    private final Map<Module, Animator> expandAnimators = new HashMap<>();
    private final Animator favoritesHoverAnimator = new Animator();
    private final Animator configHoverAnimator = new Animator();

    private final ClickGuiModule guiModule = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(ClickGuiModule.class);

    private int panelX;
    private int panelY;
    private int panelWidth = DEFAULT_WIDTH;
    private int panelHeight = DEFAULT_HEIGHT;
    private int resizeEdges;
    private boolean movingPanel;
    private boolean closing;
    private long lastFrameTimeNanos = -1;
    private double frameAlpha = 1.0;
    private double screenOpenElapsedMs;
    private Category selectedCategory = Category.values()[0];
    private boolean showingFavorites;
    private boolean showingConfig;
    private boolean showingAccounts;
    private boolean creatingProfile;
    private boolean creatingAccount;
    private StringBuilder newProfileBuffer = new StringBuilder();
    private StringBuilder newAccountBuffer = new StringBuilder();
    private final Animator accountsHoverAnimator = new Animator();

    private Module draggingFavorite;
    private double dragStartX;
    private double dragStartY;
    private boolean dragMoved;

    private Setting<?> draggingSetting;
    private Rect draggingTrackRect;
    private Setting<Keybind> capturingKeybind;
    private Module capturingModuleBind;
    private boolean suppressNextChar;
    private Setting<?> focusedField;
    private StringBuilder textBuffer = new StringBuilder();

    private final StringBuilder searchQuery = new StringBuilder();
    private int selectedSearchIndex;

    private static final double LOGO_ANIM_MS = 700;
    private double logoAnimMs = -1;

    // Icon easter-egg: five quick clicks swap the logo to the animated torof.gif (and back).
    // The chosen icon is persisted via ClickGuiModule, so it stays until toggled again.
    private static final long ICON_MULTI_CLICK_WINDOW_MS = 700;
    private static final double ICON_SWAP_ANIM_MS = 450;
    private int iconClickCount;
    private long lastIconClickMs;
    private double iconSwapAnimMs = -1;

    private double scrollOffset;
    private int contentTotalHeight;

    private Rect pickerRect;
    private double pickerHue;
    private double pickerSat;
    private double pickerVal;
    private int pickerDragMode;

    public ClickGuiScreen() {
        super(Component.literal("Lenrete"));
        panelAnimator.animateTo(1, PANEL_OPEN_MS, Easing.EASE_OUT_BACK);
        dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager.show("Lenrete opened");
        restoreLastTab();
    }

    private void restoreLastTab() {
        if (guiModule == null) {
            return;
        }
        String tab = guiModule.getLastTab();
        if ("FAVORITES".equals(tab)) {
            showingFavorites = true;
        } else if ("CONFIG".equals(tab)) {
            showingConfig = true;
        } else if ("ACCOUNTS".equals(tab)) {
            showingAccounts = true;
        } else if (!tab.isEmpty()) {
            try {
                selectedCategory = Category.valueOf(tab);
            } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) {
                // Stale or unknown saved tab name: keep the default category.
            }
        }
    }

    private void rememberTab(String tab) {
        scrollOffset = 0;
        closeColorPicker();
        if (guiModule == null) {
            return;
        }
        guiModule.setLastTab(tab);
        ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
    }

    public static void register() {
        ClientTickEvents.END_CLIENT_TICK.register(ClickGuiScreen::onTick);
    }

    private static void onTick(Minecraft client) {
        boolean keyDown = InputConstants.isKeyDown(client.getWindow(), GLFW.GLFW_KEY_RIGHT_SHIFT);
        if (keyDown && !keyWasDown) {
            Screen current = client.gui.screen();
            boolean inPortal = client.player != null && client.player.portalProcess != null;
            if (current instanceof LenreteScreen guiScreen) {
                guiScreen.requestClose();
            } else if (current == null || current instanceof net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.LevelLoadingScreen || inPortal) {
                // Allow opening over the dimension-load screen and while standing in a portal,
                // so the GUI works throughout the whole nether-portal transition.
                client.setScreenAndShow(openScreen());
            }
        }
        keyWasDown = keyDown;
    }

    /** The screen for the active theme's layout — themes may ship an entirely different menu. */
    public static Screen openScreen() {
        Theme theme = ThemeManager.getActive();
        return theme != null ? theme.createScreen() : new ClickGuiScreen();
    }

    @Override
    public void tick() {
        super.tick();
        // While the menu is open, keep a nether portal from teleporting you out of it.
        if (this.minecraft != null && this.minecraft.player != null) {
            this.minecraft.player.portalProcess = null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void init() {
        super.init();
        syncBounds();
    }

    /**
     * Pulls panel size/position from the ClickGUI module every frame, making it the single
     * source of truth: changing the width/height sliders updates the open GUI live, and mouse
     * resizing (which writes back to the module) stays in sync. Skipped while actively
     * resizing so the drag math owns the bounds that frame.
     */
    private void syncBounds() {
        if (guiModule == null || resizeEdges != 0 || movingPanel) {
            panelWidth = clamp(panelWidth, MIN_WIDTH, this.width);
            panelHeight = clamp(panelHeight, MIN_HEIGHT, this.height);
            return;
        }

        panelWidth = clamp(guiModule.getPanelWidth(), MIN_WIDTH, this.width);
        panelHeight = clamp(guiModule.getPanelHeight(), MIN_HEIGHT, this.height);

        int savedX = guiModule.getPanelX();
        int savedY = guiModule.getPanelY();
        if (savedX < 0 || savedY < 0) {
            panelX = (this.width - panelWidth) / 2;
            panelY = (this.height - panelHeight) / 2;
        } else {
            panelX = clamp(savedX, 0, Math.max(0, this.width - panelWidth));
            panelY = clamp(savedY, 0, Math.max(0, this.height - panelHeight));
        }
    }

    private int detectResizeEdges(double mx, double my) {
        boolean nearX = mx >= panelX - EDGE_MARGIN && mx <= panelX + panelWidth + EDGE_MARGIN;
        boolean nearY = my >= panelY - EDGE_MARGIN && my <= panelY + panelHeight + EDGE_MARGIN;
        if (!nearX || !nearY) {
            return 0;
        }
        int edges = 0;
        if (Math.abs(mx - panelX) <= EDGE_MARGIN) {
            edges |= RESIZE_LEFT;
        }
        if (Math.abs(mx - (panelX + panelWidth)) <= EDGE_MARGIN) {
            edges |= RESIZE_RIGHT;
        }
        if (Math.abs(my - panelY) <= EDGE_MARGIN) {
            edges |= RESIZE_TOP;
        }
        if (Math.abs(my - (panelY + panelHeight)) <= EDGE_MARGIN) {
            edges |= RESIZE_BOTTOM;
        }
        return edges;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isPauseScreen() {
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean shouldCloseOnEsc() {
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public void requestClose() {
        if (closing) {
            return;
        }
        closing = true;
        panelAnimator.animateTo(0, PANEL_CLOSE_MS, Easing.EASE_IN);
    }

    private boolean isReduceAnimationsEnabled() {
        return guiModule != null && guiModule.isReduceAnimations();
    }

    /** Pulls every color, shape and effect value from the active theme. Runs each frame, so
     * switching themes takes effect immediately without reopening the GUI. */
    private void refreshTheme() {
        Theme theme = ThemeManager.getActive();
        if (theme == null) {
            return;
        }

        COLOR_OVERLAY = theme.background();
        COLOR_PANEL = scaleAlpha(theme.panelBackground(), theme.panelOpacity());
        COLOR_BORDER = theme.outline();
        COLOR_BORDER_HOVER = theme.outlineHover();
        COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY = theme.textPrimary();
        COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY = theme.textSecondary();
        COLOR_TEXT_DISABLED = theme.textDisabled();
        COLOR_ACCENT = theme.accent();
        COLOR_ACCENT_FILL = theme.accent();
        COLOR_ACCENT_TINT = (0x14 << 24) | (COLOR_ACCENT & 0x00FFFFFF);
        COLOR_TOGGLE_ON = theme.enabledToggle();
        COLOR_TOGGLE_OFF = theme.disabledToggle();
        COLOR_HEADER = theme.header();
        COLOR_HEADER_END = theme.headerGradientEnd();

        TOP_BAR_HEIGHT = theme.headerHeight();
        PANEL_PADDING = theme.panelPadding();
        CARD_HEIGHT = theme.cardHeight();
        RAIL_ITEM_HEIGHT = theme.itemHeight();
        PANEL_RADIUS = theme.cornerRadius();
        CONTROL_RADIUS = theme.controlRadius();

        themeTextShadow = theme.useTextShadow();
        themeGradientHeader = theme.useGradientHeader();
        themeItemSeparator = theme.useItemSeparator();
        themeAccentEnabledNames = theme.accentEnabledNames();
        Animator.speedMultiplier = theme.animationSpeedMultiplier();
    }

    private static int scaleAlpha(int color, double factor) {
        factor = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, factor));
        int alpha = (int) Math.round((color >>> 24) * factor);
        return (alpha << 24) | (color & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }

    /** Theme-aware text draw: applies the active theme's drop-shadow preference. */
    private void themedText(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, String text, int x, int y, int color) {
        g.text(this.font, text, x, y, color, themeTextShadow);
    }

    @Override
    public void extractRenderState(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTick) {
        Animator.reduceAnimations = isReduceAnimationsEnabled();
        refreshTheme();
        syncBounds();

        long now = System.nanoTime();
        double deltaMs = lastFrameTimeNanos < 0 ? 0 : Math.min((now - lastFrameTimeNanos) / 1_000_000.0, 100);
        lastFrameTimeNanos = now;

        panelAnimator.tick(deltaMs);
        if (closing && panelAnimator.isSettled() && panelAnimator.value() <= 0.001) {
            this.minecraft.setScreenAndShow(null);
            return;
        }
        screenOpenElapsedMs += deltaMs;
        if (logoAnimMs >= 0) {
            logoAnimMs += deltaMs;
            if (logoAnimMs >= LOGO_ANIM_MS) {
                logoAnimMs = -1;
            }
        }
        if (iconSwapAnimMs >= 0) {
            iconSwapAnimMs += deltaMs;
            if (iconSwapAnimMs >= ICON_SWAP_ANIM_MS) {
                iconSwapAnimMs = -1;
            }
        }

        updateAnimators(deltaMs, mouseX, mouseY);

        double progress = panelAnimator.value();
        frameAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, progress));
        // Pronounced pop-open: the back-easing lets progress overshoot 1 for a subtle bounce.
        float scale = (float) (0.90 + 0.10 * progress);

        g.fill(0, 0, this.width, this.height, applyAlpha(COLOR_OVERLAY, frameAlpha));
        renderBackgroundAnimation(g);

        Matrix3x2fStack pose = g.pose();
        pose.pushMatrix();
        float centerX = panelX + panelWidth / 2f;
        float centerY = panelY + panelHeight / 2f;
        pose.translate(centerX, centerY);
        pose.scale(scale);
        pose.translate(-centerX, -centerY);

        fillRoundedRect(g, panelX, panelY, panelWidth, panelHeight, PANEL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_PANEL, frameAlpha));
        strokeRoundedRect(g, panelX, panelY, panelWidth, panelHeight, PANEL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));

        g.enableScissor(panelX, panelY, panelX + panelWidth, panelY + panelHeight);
        renderTopBar(g, mouseX, mouseY);
        renderRail(g, mouseX, mouseY);
        if (showingConfig) {
            renderConfigPanel(g, mouseX, mouseY);
        } else if (showingAccounts) {
            renderAccountsPanel(g, mouseX, mouseY);
        } else if (searchQuery.length() > 0) {
            renderSearchResults(g, mouseX, mouseY);
        } else {
            renderGrid(g, mouseX, mouseY);
        }
        g.disableScissor();

        renderResizeHandle(g, mouseX, mouseY);
        renderColorPicker(g);

        pose.popMatrix();
    }

    private void updateAnimators(double deltaMs, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
            Animator toggleAnimator = toggleAnimators.computeIfAbsent(module, m -> new Animator());
            boolean enabled = module.isEnabled();
            toggleAnimator.animateTo(enabled ? 1 : 0, TOGGLE_MS, enabled ? Easing.EASE_OUT : Easing.EASE_IN);
            toggleAnimator.tick(deltaMs);

            Animator expandAnimator = expandAnimators.computeIfAbsent(module, m -> new Animator());
            boolean expanded = expandedModules.contains(module);
            expandAnimator.animateTo(expanded ? 1 : 0, EXPAND_MS, expanded ? Easing.EASE_OUT : Easing.EASE_IN);
            expandAnimator.tick(deltaMs);

            Animator cardHover = cardHoverAnimators.computeIfAbsent(module, m -> new Animator());
            cardHover.tick(deltaMs);
        }

        for (Category category : Category.values()) {
            Animator animator = railHoverAnimators.computeIfAbsent(category, c -> new Animator());
            animator.tick(deltaMs);
        }

        for (CategoryRow row : computeCategoryRows()) {
            Animator animator = railHoverAnimators.get(row.category);
            animator.animateTo(row.rect.contains(mouseX, mouseY) ? 1 : 0, HOVER_MS, Easing.LINEAR);
        }

        favoritesHoverAnimator.animateTo(favoritesRowRect().contains(mouseX, mouseY) ? 1 : 0, HOVER_MS, Easing.LINEAR);
        favoritesHoverAnimator.tick(deltaMs);

        configHoverAnimator.animateTo(configRowRect().contains(mouseX, mouseY) ? 1 : 0, HOVER_MS, Easing.LINEAR);
        configHoverAnimator.tick(deltaMs);

        accountsHoverAnimator.animateTo(accountsRowRect().contains(mouseX, mouseY) ? 1 : 0, HOVER_MS, Easing.LINEAR);
        accountsHoverAnimator.tick(deltaMs);

        for (CardEntry entry : computeCardEntries()) {
            Animator animator = cardHoverAnimators.get(entry.module);
            animator.animateTo(entry.rect.contains(mouseX, mouseY) ? 1 : 0, HOVER_MS, Easing.LINEAR);
        }

        closeHoverAnimator.animateTo(closeButtonRect().contains(mouseX, mouseY) ? 1 : 0, HOVER_MS, Easing.LINEAR);
        closeHoverAnimator.tick(deltaMs);

        boolean resizeActive = resizeEdges != 0 || resizeHandleRect().contains(mouseX, mouseY);
        resizeHoverAnimator.animateTo(resizeActive ? 1 : 0, HOVER_MS, Easing.LINEAR);
        resizeHoverAnimator.tick(deltaMs);
    }

    private void renderBackgroundAnimation(GuiGraphicsExtractor g) {
        if (guiModule == null || Animator.reduceAnimations) {
            return;
        }
        GuiBackground.render(g, guiModule.getBackgroundAnimation(),
                this.width, this.height, screenOpenElapsedMs, COLOR_ACCENT, frameAlpha);
    }

    private void renderTopBar(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        // Themed header strip: a vertical gradient across the top bar when the theme asks for it.
        if (themeGradientHeader) {
            g.fillGradient(panelX + 1, panelY + 1, panelX + panelWidth - 1, panelY + TOP_BAR_HEIGHT,
                    applyAlpha(COLOR_HEADER, frameAlpha), applyAlpha(COLOR_HEADER_END, frameAlpha));
        }

        int iconX = panelX + PANEL_PADDING;
        int iconY = panelY + (TOP_BAR_HEIGHT - ICON_SIZE) / 2;

        double logoProgress = logoAnimMs >= 0 ? Math.min(1.0, logoAnimMs / LOGO_ANIM_MS) : -1;

        // The chosen logo; animated images resolve to their current frame.
        Identifier iconTexture = ICON_TEXTURE;
        if (guiModule != null) {
            Identifier chosen = guiModule.getGuiIcon().texture(System.currentTimeMillis());
            if (chosen != null) {
                iconTexture = chosen;
            }
        }

        Matrix3x2fStack pose = g.pose();
        pose.pushMatrix();
        float centerX = iconX + ICON_SIZE / 2f;
        float centerY = iconY + ICON_SIZE / 2f;

        // Replace-pop: a quick shrink-and-grow when the icon swaps between perona and torof.
        if (iconSwapAnimMs >= 0) {
            double p = Math.min(1.0, iconSwapAnimMs / ICON_SWAP_ANIM_MS);
            float swapScale = (float) (0.2 + 0.8 * Easing.EASE_OUT_BACK.apply(p));
            pose.translate(centerX, centerY);
            pose.scale(swapScale);
            pose.translate(-centerX, -centerY);
        }
        if (logoProgress >= 0) {
            float angle = (float) (Easing.EASE_OUT.apply(logoProgress) * Math.PI * 2);
            pose.rotateAbout(angle, centerX, centerY);
        }
        g.blit(RenderPipelines.GUI_TEXTURED, iconTexture, iconX, iconY, 0f, 0f, ICON_SIZE, ICON_SIZE, ICON_SIZE, ICON_SIZE);
        pose.popMatrix();

        int textY = panelY + (TOP_BAR_HEIGHT - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
        int titleColor = logoProgress >= 0
                ? applyAlpha(hsvColor((logoProgress * 2.0) % 1.0), frameAlpha)
                : applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, frameAlpha);
        themedText(g,"LENRETE", iconX + ICON_SIZE + 8, textY, titleColor);

        Rect close = closeButtonRect();
        double hover = closeHoverAnimator.value();
        int borderColor = lerpColor(COLOR_BORDER, 0xFF8A9099, hover);
        strokeRoundedRect(g, close.x, close.y, close.w, close.h, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(borderColor, frameAlpha));
        String label = "X";
        int labelX = close.x + (close.w - this.font.width(label)) / 2;
        int labelY = close.y + (close.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
        int labelColor = lerpColor(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, hover);
        themedText(g,label, labelX, labelY, applyAlpha(labelColor, frameAlpha));

        g.horizontalLine(panelX, panelX + panelWidth - 1, panelY + TOP_BAR_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));

        Rect searchBox = searchBoxRect();
        boolean active = searchQuery.length() > 0;
        strokeRoundedRect(g, searchBox.x, searchBox.y, searchBox.w, searchBox.h, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(active ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));
        String searchText = active ? searchQuery.toString() : "Search...";
        int searchColor = active ? COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY : COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY;
        g.enableScissor(searchBox.x + 4, searchBox.y, searchBox.x + searchBox.w - 4, searchBox.y + searchBox.h);
        themedText(g,searchText, searchBox.x + 6, searchBox.y + (searchBox.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2, applyAlpha(searchColor, frameAlpha));
        g.disableScissor();
    }

    private Rect searchBoxRect() {
        Rect close = closeButtonRect();
        int x = panelX + PANEL_PADDING + ICON_SIZE + 8 + 60;
        int y = panelY + (TOP_BAR_HEIGHT - SEARCH_BOX_HEIGHT) / 2;
        int width = Math.max(0, close.x - 8 - x);
        return new Rect(x, y, width, SEARCH_BOX_HEIGHT);
    }

    private void renderRail(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        g.verticalLine(panelX + RAIL_WIDTH, panelY + TOP_BAR_HEIGHT, panelY + panelHeight - 1, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));

        Rect favRow = favoritesRowRect();
        double favHover = favoritesHoverAnimator.value();
        if (!showingFavorites && favHover > 0) {
            g.fill(favRow.x, favRow.y, favRow.x + favRow.w, favRow.y + favRow.h, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha * favHover));
        }
        if (showingFavorites) {
            g.fill(favRow.x, favRow.y, favRow.x + 2, favRow.y + favRow.h, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_FILL, frameAlpha));
        }
        int favTextY = favRow.y + (favRow.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
        int favTextColor = showingFavorites ? COLOR_ACCENT : lerpColor(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, favHover);
        themedText(g,showingFavorites ? "★ FAVORITES" : "☆ FAVORITES", favRow.x + PANEL_PADDING, favTextY, applyAlpha(favTextColor, frameAlpha));
        g.horizontalLine(panelX + 8, panelX + RAIL_WIDTH - 8, favRow.y + favRow.h, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));

        for (CategoryRow row : computeCategoryRows()) {
            boolean selected = !showingFavorites && row.category == selectedCategory;
            double hover = railHoverAnimators.get(row.category).value();

            if (!selected && hover > 0) {
                g.fill(row.rect.x, row.rect.y, row.rect.x + row.rect.w, row.rect.y + row.rect.h, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha * hover));
            }
            if (selected) {
                g.fill(row.rect.x, row.rect.y, row.rect.x + 2, row.rect.y + row.rect.h, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_FILL, frameAlpha));
            }

            int textY = row.rect.y + (row.rect.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
            int textColor = selected ? COLOR_ACCENT : lerpColor(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, hover);
            themedText(g,row.category.name(), row.rect.x + PANEL_PADDING, textY, applyAlpha(textColor, frameAlpha));
        }

        Rect configRow = configRowRect();
        double configHover = configHoverAnimator.value();
        if (!showingConfig && configHover > 0) {
            g.fill(configRow.x, configRow.y, configRow.x + configRow.w, configRow.y + configRow.h, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha * configHover));
        }
        if (showingConfig) {
            g.fill(configRow.x, configRow.y, configRow.x + 2, configRow.y + configRow.h, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_FILL, frameAlpha));
        }
        g.horizontalLine(panelX + 8, panelX + RAIL_WIDTH - 8, configRow.y, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));
        int configTextY = configRow.y + (configRow.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
        int configTextColor = showingConfig ? COLOR_ACCENT : lerpColor(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, configHover);
        themedText(g,"⚙ CONFIG", configRow.x + PANEL_PADDING, configTextY, applyAlpha(configTextColor, frameAlpha));

        Rect accountsRow = accountsRowRect();
        double accountsHover = accountsHoverAnimator.value();
        if (!showingAccounts && accountsHover > 0) {
            g.fill(accountsRow.x, accountsRow.y, accountsRow.x + accountsRow.w, accountsRow.y + accountsRow.h, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha * accountsHover));
        }
        if (showingAccounts) {
            g.fill(accountsRow.x, accountsRow.y, accountsRow.x + 2, accountsRow.y + accountsRow.h, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_FILL, frameAlpha));
        }
        int accountsTextY = accountsRow.y + (accountsRow.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
        int accountsTextColor = showingAccounts ? COLOR_ACCENT : lerpColor(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, accountsHover);
        themedText(g,"◆ ACCOUNTS", accountsRow.x + PANEL_PADDING, accountsTextY, applyAlpha(accountsTextColor, frameAlpha));
    }

    private Rect configRowRect() {
        int y = panelY + panelHeight - PANEL_PADDING / 2 - RAIL_ITEM_HEIGHT;
        return new Rect(panelX, y, RAIL_WIDTH, RAIL_ITEM_HEIGHT);
    }

    private Rect accountsRowRect() {
        int y = panelY + panelHeight - PANEL_PADDING / 2 - RAIL_ITEM_HEIGHT * 2;
        return new Rect(panelX, y, RAIL_WIDTH, RAIL_ITEM_HEIGHT);
    }

    private void renderGrid(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        List<CardEntry> entries = computeCardEntries();

        if (entries.isEmpty() && showingFavorites) {
            int textX = panelX + RAIL_WIDTH + PANEL_PADDING;
            int textY = panelY + TOP_BAR_HEIGHT + PANEL_PADDING;
            themedText(g,"Star a module to add it here", textX, textY, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, frameAlpha));
            return;
        }

        Rect region = contentRegion();
        g.enableScissor(panelX + RAIL_WIDTH + 1, panelY + TOP_BAR_HEIGHT + 1, panelX + panelWidth, panelY + panelHeight);

        for (int i = 0; i < entries.size(); i++) {
            CardEntry entry = entries.get(i);
            Rect rect = entry.rect;

            if (rect.y + rect.h < region.y || rect.y > region.y + region.h) {
                continue;
            }

            double staggerT = cardStaggerProgress(i);
            double cardAlpha = frameAlpha * staggerT;
            if (cardAlpha <= 0.001) {
                continue;
            }

            double hover = cardHoverAnimators.get(entry.module).value();
            boolean isDragging = entry.module == draggingFavorite && dragMoved;

            fillRoundedRect(g, rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h, PANEL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_PANEL, cardAlpha));
            int borderColor = isDragging ? COLOR_ACCENT : lerpColor(COLOR_BORDER, COLOR_BORDER_HOVER, hover);
            strokeRoundedRect(g, rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rect.h, PANEL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(borderColor, cardAlpha));

            // Themed 1px separator between module cards.
            if (themeItemSeparator) {
                int sepY = rect.y + rect.h + CARD_ROW_SPACING / 2;
                g.fill(rect.x + 2, sepY, rect.x + rect.w - 2, sepY + 1, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, cardAlpha));
            }

            boolean favorite = entry.module.isFavorite();
            String star = favorite ? "★" : "☆";
            themedText(g,star, entry.starRect.x + 3, entry.starRect.y + 3, applyAlpha(favorite ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, cardAlpha));

            boolean unavailable = entry.module.isUnavailable();
            int nameColor;
            if (unavailable) {
                nameColor = COLOR_TEXT_DISABLED;
            } else if (themeAccentEnabledNames) {
                // Classic-style: enabled modules glow in accent, disabled sit in gray.
                nameColor = entry.module.isEnabled() ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY;
            } else {
                nameColor = COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY;
            }
            String name = unavailable ? entry.module.getName() + "  (disabled)" : entry.module.getName();
            themedText(g,name, rect.x + 24, rect.y + 10, applyAlpha(nameColor, cardAlpha));

            int descWidth = rect.w - 24 - 12 - TOGGLE_WIDTH - 8;
            List<String> descLines = wrapText(entry.module.getDescription(), descWidth, 2);
            g.enableScissor(rect.x + 24, rect.y + 22, rect.x + rect.w - 12 - TOGGLE_WIDTH - 8, rect.y + CARD_HEIGHT - 2);
            for (int line = 0; line < descLines.size(); line++) {
                themedText(g,descLines.get(line), rect.x + 24, rect.y + 23 + line * 9, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, cardAlpha));
            }
            g.disableScissor();

            renderToggle(g, entry.module, entry.toggleRect.x, entry.toggleRect.y, cardAlpha);
            if (showsBindBadge(entry.module)) {
                renderBindBadge(g, entry, cardAlpha);
            }

            if (!entry.settingRows.isEmpty()) {
                g.enableScissor(rect.x, rect.y, rect.x + rect.w, rect.y + rect.h);
                g.horizontalLine(rect.x + 8, rect.x + rect.w - 8, rect.y + CARD_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, cardAlpha));
                for (SettingRow row : entry.settingRows) {
                    renderSettingRow(g, row, cardAlpha);
                }
                g.disableScissor();
            }
        }

        g.disableScissor();
    }

    private double cardStaggerProgress(int index) {
        if (Animator.reduceAnimations) {
            return 1.0;
        }
        double delay = Math.min(index * STAGGER_STEP_MS, STAGGER_CAP_MS);
        double t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (screenOpenElapsedMs - delay) / STAGGER_REVEAL_MS));
        return Easing.EASE_OUT.apply(t);
    }

    private void renderToggle(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Module module, int x, int y, double alpha) {
        double t = toggleAnimators.get(module).value();

        int trackColor = lerpColor(COLOR_TOGGLE_OFF, COLOR_TOGGLE_ON, t);
        int radius = TOGGLE_HEIGHT / 2;
        fillRoundedRect(g, x, y, TOGGLE_WIDTH, TOGGLE_HEIGHT, radius, applyAlpha(trackColor, alpha));

        int offX = x + radius;
        int onX = x + TOGGLE_WIDTH - radius;
        int knobCenterX = (int) Math.round(offX + (onX - offX) * t);
        int knobCenterY = y + radius;
        fillCircle(g, knobCenterX, knobCenterY, radius - 2, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, alpha));
    }

    private void renderBindBadge(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, CardEntry entry, double alpha) {
        Rect r = entry.bindRect;
        boolean capturing = capturingModuleBind == entry.module;
        int color = capturing ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_BORDER;
        strokeRoundedRect(g, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(color, alpha));

        String label = capturing ? "press…" : keyName(entry.module.getKey());
        int textColor = capturing ? COLOR_ACCENT : (entry.module.getKey() > 0 ? COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY : COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY);
        int textX = r.x + (r.w - this.font.width(label)) / 2;
        int textY = r.y + (r.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
        g.enableScissor(r.x + 1, r.y, r.x + r.w - 1, r.y + r.h);
        themedText(g,label, textX, textY, applyAlpha(textColor, alpha));
        g.disableScissor();
    }

    /** Word-wraps text into at most {@code maxLines} lines that each fit {@code maxWidth}, ellipsizing overflow. */
    private List<String> wrapText(String text, int maxWidth, int maxLines) {
        List<String> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        if (maxWidth <= 0) {
            lines.add(text);
            return lines;
        }

        StringBuilder current = new StringBuilder();
        for (String word : text.split(" ")) {
            String candidate = current.length() == 0 ? word : current + " " + word;
            if (this.font.width(candidate) <= maxWidth || current.length() == 0) {
                current.setLength(0);
                current.append(candidate);
            } else {
                lines.add(current.toString());
                current.setLength(0);
                current.append(word);
                if (lines.size() == maxLines - 1) {
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if (lines.size() < maxLines && current.length() > 0) {
            lines.add(current.toString());
        } else if (current.length() > 0 && !lines.isEmpty()) {
            // Ran out of lines with text remaining: mark the last line as truncated.
            String last = lines.get(lines.size() - 1);
            while (this.font.width(last + "…") > maxWidth && last.length() > 1) {
                last = last.substring(0, last.length() - 1);
            }
            lines.set(lines.size() - 1, last + "…");
        }
        return lines;
    }

    private Rect iconRect() {
        int x = panelX + PANEL_PADDING;
        int y = panelY + (TOP_BAR_HEIGHT - ICON_SIZE) / 2;
        return new Rect(x, y, ICON_SIZE, ICON_SIZE);
    }

    private void handleIconClick() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (now - lastIconClickMs <= ICON_MULTI_CLICK_WINDOW_MS) {
            iconClickCount++;
        } else {
            iconClickCount = 1;
        }
        lastIconClickMs = now;

        if (iconClickCount >= 5 && guiModule != null) {
            // Five quick clicks steps the logo to the next image.
            iconClickCount = 0;
            ClientImage[] images = ClientImage.values();
            ClientImage next = images[(guiModule.getGuiIcon().ordinal() + 1) % images.length];
            guiModule.setGuiIcon(next);
            ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
            iconSwapAnimMs = 0;
            dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager.show(next.isAvailable()
                    ? "Icon: " + next.label()
                    : next.label() + " could not be loaded");
        }
    }

    private static int hsvColor(double hue) {
        return 0xFF000000 | (java.awt.Color.HSBtoRGB((float) hue, 0.7f, 1.0f) & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }

    private static boolean showsBindBadge(Module module) {
        // ClickTP has its own dedicated Teleport Key setting; HUD and ClickGUI are
        // always-on config modules — a toggle bind would be redundant on all three.
        String name = module.getName();
        return !"ClickTP".equals(name) && !"HUD".equals(name) && !"ClickGUI".equals(name);
    }

    static String keyName(int code) {
        if (code <= 0) {
            return "bind";
        }
        try {
            return InputConstants.Type.KEYSYM.getOrCreate(code).getDisplayName().getString();
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            return "KEY " + code;
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private void renderSettingRow(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, SettingRow row, double alpha) {
        Setting<?> setting = row.setting;
        int labelY = row.labelArea.y + (SETTINGS_ROW_HEIGHT - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
        themedText(g,setting.getName(), row.labelArea.x, labelY, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, alpha));

        Class<?> type = setting.getType();
        Rect control = row.controlRect;

        if (guiModule != null && setting == guiModule.getThemeSetting()) {
            renderThemeCycle(g, control, alpha);
        } else if (type == Boolean.class) {
            renderCheckbox(g, (Setting<Boolean>) setting, control, alpha);
        } else if (type == ColorValue.class) {
            renderColorSwatch(g, (Setting<ColorValue>) setting, control, alpha);
        } else if (type == Keybind.class) {
            renderKeybindField(g, (Setting<Keybind>) setting, control, alpha);
        } else if ((type == Integer.class || type == Double.class) && setting.getMin() != null && setting.getMax() != null) {
            renderSlider(g, setting, control, alpha);
        } else if (type.isEnum()) {
            renderDropdown(g, setting, control, alpha);
        } else {
            renderTextField(g, setting, control, alpha);
        }
    }

    private void renderCheckbox(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Setting<Boolean> setting, Rect control, double alpha) {
        int y = control.y + (control.h - CHECKBOX_SIZE) / 2;
        int x = control.x + control.w - CHECKBOX_SIZE;
        boolean value = Boolean.TRUE.equals(setting.get());
        strokeRoundedRect(g, x, y, CHECKBOX_SIZE, CHECKBOX_SIZE, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, alpha));
        if (value) {
            fillRoundedRect(g, x + 3, y + 3, CHECKBOX_SIZE - 6, CHECKBOX_SIZE - 6, 1, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_FILL, alpha));
        }
    }

    private static int sliderTrackWidth(Rect control) {
        // Leave room for the value text; shrink the track on narrow controls.
        return Math.max(24, Math.min(SLIDER_TRACK_WIDTH, control.w - 30));
    }

    private void renderSlider(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Setting<?> setting, Rect control, double alpha) {
        double min = ((Number) setting.getMin()).doubleValue();
        double max = ((Number) setting.getMax()).doubleValue();
        double value = ((Number) setting.get()).doubleValue();
        double ratio = max > min ? (value - min) / (max - min) : 0;
        ratio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ratio));

        int trackY = control.y + (control.h - SLIDER_TRACK_HEIGHT) / 2;
        int trackX = control.x;
        int trackWidth = sliderTrackWidth(control);
        fillRoundedRect(g, trackX, trackY, trackWidth, SLIDER_TRACK_HEIGHT, SLIDER_TRACK_HEIGHT / 2, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, alpha));
        int filled = (int) Math.round(trackWidth * ratio);
        if (filled > 0) {
            fillRoundedRect(g, trackX, trackY, filled, SLIDER_TRACK_HEIGHT, SLIDER_TRACK_HEIGHT / 2, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_FILL, alpha));
        }

        String valueText = setting.getType() == Integer.class
                ? String.valueOf((int) value)
                : String.format("%.2f", value);
        int textX = trackX + trackWidth + 8;
        int textY = control.y + (control.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
        themedText(g,valueText, textX, textY, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, alpha));
    }

    /** Cycle control over all registered themes; the label always shows the live active theme. */
    private void renderThemeCycle(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Rect control, double alpha) {
        strokeRoundedRect(g, control.x, control.y, control.w, control.h, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, alpha));
        Theme active = ThemeManager.getActive();
        String label = active != null ? active.name() : "Modern";
        themedText(g,label, control.x + 6, control.y + (control.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT, alpha));
        themedText(g,">", control.x + control.w - 10, control.y + (control.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, alpha));
    }

    private void renderDropdown(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Setting<?> setting, Rect control, double alpha) {
        strokeRoundedRect(g, control.x, control.y, control.w, control.h, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, alpha));
        Object value = setting.get();
        String label = value == null ? "" : ((Enum<?>) value).name();
        themedText(g,label, control.x + 6, control.y + (control.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, alpha));
        themedText(g,">", control.x + control.w - 10, control.y + (control.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, alpha));
    }

    private void renderColorSwatch(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Setting<ColorValue> setting, Rect control, double alpha) {
        int x = control.x + control.w - COLOR_SWATCH_WIDTH;
        int y = control.y + (control.h - COLOR_SWATCH_HEIGHT) / 2;
