```java
        ColorValue value = setting.get();
        int argb = value != null ? value.argb() : 0xFFFFFFFF;
        fillRoundedRect(g, x, y, COLOR_SWATCH_WIDTH, COLOR_SWATCH_HEIGHT, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(argb, alpha));
        strokeRoundedRect(g, x, y, COLOR_SWATCH_WIDTH, COLOR_SWATCH_HEIGHT, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, alpha));
    }

    private void renderKeybindField(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Setting<Keybind> setting, Rect control, double alpha) {
        boolean capturing = capturingKeybind == setting;
        strokeRoundedRect(g, control.x, control.y, control.w, control.h, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(capturing ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_BORDER, alpha));
        Keybind value = setting.get();
        String label = capturing ? "..." : (value == null || value.code() <= 0 ? "NONE" : keyName(value.code()));
        themedText(g,label, control.x + 6, control.y + (control.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2, applyAlpha(capturing ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, alpha));
    }

    private void renderTextField(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Setting<?> setting, Rect control, double alpha) {
        boolean focused = focusedField == setting;
        strokeRoundedRect(g, control.x, control.y, control.w, control.h, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(focused ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_BORDER, alpha));
        String text = focused ? textBuffer.toString() + "_" : String.valueOf(setting.get());
        g.enableScissor(control.x + 4, control.y, control.x + control.w - 4, control.y + control.h);
        themedText(g,text, control.x + 6, control.y + (control.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2, applyAlpha(focused ? COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY : COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, alpha));
        g.disableScissor();
    }

    private void renderResizeHandle(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        Rect handle = resizeHandleRect();
        double active = resizeHoverAnimator.value();
        int color = lerpColor(COLOR_BORDER, 0xFF8A9099, active);
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            int inset = 3 + i * 4;
            g.fill(handle.x + handle.w - inset, handle.y + handle.h - 2, handle.x + handle.w - inset + 2, handle.y + handle.h, applyAlpha(color, frameAlpha));
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseClicked(MouseButtonEvent event, boolean doubled) {
        if (closing) {
            return true;
        }

        if (focusedField != null) {
            commitTextField();
        }
        capturingKeybind = null;
        capturingModuleBind = null;

        // Right-click a module's bind badge to unbind it.
        if (event.button() == 1 && !showingConfig && !showingAccounts && searchQuery.length() == 0) {
            for (CardEntry entry : computeCardEntries()) {
                if (showsBindBadge(entry.module) && entry.bindRect.contains(event.x(), event.y())) {
                    entry.module.setKey(-1);
                    ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }

        if (pickerSetting != null && event.button() == 0 && handlePickerClick(event.x(), event.y())) {
            return true;
        }

        if (event.button() != 0) {
            return super.mouseClicked(event, doubled);
        }

        double mx = event.x();
        double my = event.y();

        int edges = detectResizeEdges(mx, my);
        if (edges != 0) {
            resizeEdges = edges;
            return true;
        }

        if (closeButtonRect().contains(mx, my)) {
            requestClose();
            return true;
        }

        if (iconRect().contains(mx, my)) {
            logoAnimMs = 0;
            handleIconClick();
            return true;
        }

        if (my >= panelY && my < panelY + TOP_BAR_HEIGHT) {
            Rect searchBox = searchBoxRect();
            boolean overSearch = mx >= searchBox.x && mx < searchBox.x + searchBox.w;
            if (!overSearch) {
                movingPanel = true;
                return true;
            }
        }

        if (searchQuery.length() > 0) {
            List<SearchRowEntry> rows = computeSearchRows();
            for (int i = 0; i < rows.size(); i++) {
                if (rows.get(i).rect().contains(mx, my)) {
                    selectedSearchIndex = i;
                    ModuleManager.getInstance().toggleModule(rows.get(i).result().module());
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }

        if (favoritesRowRect().contains(mx, my)) {
            showingFavorites = true;
            showingConfig = false;
            showingAccounts = false;
            creatingProfile = false;
            creatingAccount = false;
            clearSearch();
            rememberTab("FAVORITES");
            return true;
        }

        if (configRowRect().contains(mx, my)) {
            showingConfig = true;
            showingFavorites = false;
            showingAccounts = false;
            creatingAccount = false;
            clearSearch();
            rememberTab("CONFIG");
            return true;
        }

        if (accountsRowRect().contains(mx, my)) {
            showingAccounts = true;
            showingConfig = false;
            showingFavorites = false;
            creatingProfile = false;
            clearSearch();
            rememberTab("ACCOUNTS");
            return true;
        }

        for (CategoryRow row : computeCategoryRows()) {
            if (row.rect.contains(mx, my)) {
                showingFavorites = false;
                showingConfig = false;
                showingAccounts = false;
                creatingProfile = false;
                creatingAccount = false;
                selectedCategory = row.category;
                clearSearch();
                rememberTab(row.category.name());
                return true;
            }
        }

        if (showingConfig) {
            handleConfigClick(mx, my);
            return true;
        }

        if (showingAccounts) {
            handleAccountsClick(mx, my);
            return true;
        }

        if (searchQuery.length() > 0) {
            return super.mouseClicked(event, doubled);
        }

        for (CardEntry entry : computeCardEntries()) {
            if (entry.starRect.contains(mx, my)) {
                handleStarClick(entry.module);
                return true;
            }

            if (entry.toggleRect.contains(mx, my)) {
                ModuleManager.getInstance().toggleModule(entry.module);
                return true;
            }

            if (showsBindBadge(entry.module) && entry.bindRect.contains(mx, my)) {
                capturingModuleBind = entry.module;
                return true;
            }

            for (SettingRow row : entry.settingRows) {
                if (row.controlRect.contains(mx, my)) {
                    handleSettingClick(row.setting, row.controlRect, mx);
                    return true;
                }
            }

            if (entry.rect.contains(mx, my)) {
                if (showingFavorites) {
                    draggingFavorite = entry.module;
                    dragStartX = mx;
                    dragStartY = my;
                    dragMoved = false;
                } else if (!entry.module.getSettings().isEmpty()) {
                    toggleExpanded(entry.module);
                }
                return true;
            }
        }

        return super.mouseClicked(event, doubled);
    }

    private void handleStarClick(Module module) {
        boolean newState = !module.isFavorite();
        module.setFavorite(newState);
        if (newState) {
            int maxOrder = 0;
            for (Module other : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
                if (other.isFavorite() && other != module) {
                    maxOrder = Math.max(maxOrder, other.getFavoriteOrder());
                }
            }
            module.setFavoriteOrder(maxOrder + 1);
        }
        ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseDragged(MouseButtonEvent event, double dragX, double dragY) {
        if (closing) {
            return true;
        }

        if (pickerDragMode != 0) {
            updatePickerFromMouse(event.x(), event.y());
            return true;
        }

        if (draggingSetting != null) {
            applySliderValue(draggingSetting, draggingTrackRect, event.x());
            return true;
        }

        if (draggingFavorite != null) {
            double distance = Math.hypot(event.x() - dragStartX, event.y() - dragStartY);
            if (distance > 4) {
                dragMoved = true;
            }
            return true;
        }

        if (movingPanel) {
            panelX = clamp(panelX + (int) Math.round(dragX), 0, Math.max(0, this.width - panelWidth));
            panelY = clamp(panelY + (int) Math.round(dragY), 0, Math.max(0, this.height - panelHeight));
            if (guiModule != null) {
                guiModule.setPanelX(panelX);
                guiModule.setPanelY(panelY);
                ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
            }
            return true;
        }

        if (resizeEdges != 0) {
            int dx = (int) Math.round(dragX);
            int dy = (int) Math.round(dragY);
            int rightEdge = panelX + panelWidth;
            int bottomEdge = panelY + panelHeight;

            int newW = panelWidth;
            int newH = panelHeight;
            if ((resizeEdges & RESIZE_RIGHT) != 0) {
                newW = panelWidth + dx;
            }
            if ((resizeEdges & RESIZE_LEFT) != 0) {
                newW = panelWidth - dx;
            }
            if ((resizeEdges & RESIZE_BOTTOM) != 0) {
                newH = panelHeight + dy;
            }
            if ((resizeEdges & RESIZE_TOP) != 0) {
                newH = panelHeight - dy;
            }

            newW = clamp(newW, MIN_WIDTH, this.width);
            newH = clamp(newH, MIN_HEIGHT, this.height);

            int newX = panelX;
            int newY = panelY;
            if ((resizeEdges & RESIZE_LEFT) != 0) {
                newX = rightEdge - newW;
            }
            if ((resizeEdges & RESIZE_TOP) != 0) {
                newY = bottomEdge - newH;
            }
            newX = clamp(newX, 0, Math.max(0, this.width - newW));
            newY = clamp(newY, 0, Math.max(0, this.height - newH));

            panelX = newX;
            panelY = newY;
            panelWidth = newW;
            panelHeight = newH;
            if (guiModule != null) {
                guiModule.setPanelWidth(newW);
                guiModule.setPanelHeight(newH);
                guiModule.setPanelX(newX);
                guiModule.setPanelY(newY);
                ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
            }
            return true;
        }

        return super.mouseDragged(event, dragX, dragY);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseReleased(MouseButtonEvent event) {
        if (event.button() == 0 && draggingFavorite != null) {
            finishFavoriteDrag(event.x(), event.y());
            return true;
        }

        if (event.button() == 0 && (resizeEdges != 0 || movingPanel || draggingSetting != null || pickerDragMode != 0)) {
            resizeEdges = 0;
            movingPanel = false;
            draggingSetting = null;
            pickerDragMode = 0;
            return true;
        }
        return super.mouseReleased(event);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseScrolled(double mouseX, double mouseY, double scrollX, double scrollY) {
        if (closing) {
            return true;
        }

        Rect region = contentRegion();
        boolean inContent = mouseX >= panelX + RAIL_WIDTH && mouseX < panelX + panelWidth
                && mouseY >= panelY + TOP_BAR_HEIGHT && mouseY < panelY + panelHeight;

        if (inContent && !showingConfig && !showingAccounts && searchQuery.length() == 0) {
            closeColorPicker();
            double maxScroll = Math.max(0, contentTotalHeight - region.h);
            scrollOffset = Math.max(0, Math.min(scrollOffset - scrollY * 30, maxScroll));
            return true;
        }

        return super.mouseScrolled(mouseX, mouseY, scrollX, scrollY);
    }

    private void finishFavoriteDrag(double mx, double my) {
        Module module = draggingFavorite;
        boolean moved = dragMoved;
        draggingFavorite = null;
        dragMoved = false;

        if (!moved) {
            if (!module.getSettings().isEmpty()) {
                toggleExpanded(module);
            }
            return;
        }

        for (CardEntry entry : computeCardEntries()) {
            if (entry.module != module && entry.rect.contains(mx, my)) {
                int order = module.getFavoriteOrder();
                module.setFavoriteOrder(entry.module.getFavoriteOrder());
                entry.module.setFavoriteOrder(order);
                ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean keyPressed(KeyEvent event) {
        if (closing) {
            return true;
        }

        if (capturingKeybind != null) {
            int key = event.key();
            capturingKeybind.set(key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE ? new Keybind(-1) : new Keybind(key));
            ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
            capturingKeybind = null;
            suppressNextChar = true;
            return true;
        }

        if (capturingModuleBind != null) {
            int key = event.key();
            capturingModuleBind.setKey(key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE ? -1 : key);
            ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
            capturingModuleBind = null;
            suppressNextChar = true;
            return true;
        }

        if (focusedField != null) {
            int key = event.key();
            if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE) {
                if (textBuffer.length() > 0) {
                    textBuffer.deleteCharAt(textBuffer.length() - 1);
                }
                return true;
            }
            if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ENTER || key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_KP_ENTER) {
                commitTextField();
                return true;
            }
            if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
                focusedField = null;
                return true;
            }
            return true;
        }

        if (creatingProfile) {
            int key = event.key();
            if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE) {
                if (newProfileBuffer.length() > 0) {
                    newProfileBuffer.deleteCharAt(newProfileBuffer.length() - 1);
                }
                return true;
            }
            if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ENTER || key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_KP_ENTER) {
                String name = newProfileBuffer.toString().trim();
                if (!name.isEmpty()) {
                    ConfigManager.switchProfile(name);
                }
                creatingProfile = false;
                return true;
            }
            if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
                creatingProfile = false;
                return true;
            }
            return true;
        }

        if (creatingAccount) {
            int key = event.key();
            if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE) {
                if (newAccountBuffer.length() > 0) {
                    newAccountBuffer.deleteCharAt(newAccountBuffer.length() - 1);
                }
                return true;
            }
            if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ENTER || key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_KP_ENTER) {
                String name = newAccountBuffer.toString().trim();
                if (!name.isEmpty()) {
                    AccountManager.add(name);
                }
                creatingAccount = false;
                return true;
            }
            if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
                creatingAccount = false;
                return true;
            }
            return true;
        }

        int key = event.key();

        if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
            if (searchQuery.length() > 0) {
                clearSearch();
            } else {
                requestClose();
            }
            return true;
        }

        if (searchQuery.length() > 0) {
            List<SearchResult> results = computeSearchResults(searchQuery.toString());
            if (!results.isEmpty()) {
                int index = Math.max(0, Math.min(selectedSearchIndex, results.size() - 1));
                if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_DOWN) {
                    selectedSearchIndex = Math.min(index + 1, results.size() - 1);
                    return true;
                }
                if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_UP) {
                    selectedSearchIndex = Math.max(index - 1, 0);
                    return true;
                }
                if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ENTER || key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_KP_ENTER) {
                    ModuleManager.getInstance().toggleModule(results.get(index).module());
                    return true;
                }
                if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_TAB) {
                    Module module = results.get(index).module();
                    showingFavorites = false;
                    showingConfig = false;
                    selectedCategory = module.getCategory();
                    expandedModules.add(module);
                    clearSearch();
                    rememberTab(module.getCategory().name());
                    return true;
                }
            }
            if (key == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_BACKSPACE) {
                if (searchQuery.length() > 0) {
                    searchQuery.deleteCharAt(searchQuery.length() - 1);
                    selectedSearchIndex = 0;
                }
                return true;
            }
        }

        return super.keyPressed(event);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean charTyped(CharacterEvent event) {
        if (closing) {
            return true;
        }
        if (suppressNextChar) {
            // Swallow the character that immediately follows a keybind capture so it
            // does not leak into the search box.
            suppressNextChar = false;
            return true;
        }
        if (focusedField != null) {
            if (textBuffer.length() < 64) {
                textBuffer.append(event.codepointAsString());
            }
            return true;
        }
        if (creatingProfile) {
            if (newProfileBuffer.length() < 32) {
                newProfileBuffer.append(event.codepointAsString());
            }
            return true;
        }
        if (creatingAccount) {
            if (newAccountBuffer.length() < 16) {
                newAccountBuffer.append(event.codepointAsString());
            }
            return true;
        }
        if (capturingKeybind == null && !showingConfig && !showingAccounts) {
            if (searchQuery.length() < 48) {
                searchQuery.append(event.codepointAsString());
                selectedSearchIndex = 0;
            }
            return true;
        }
        return super.charTyped(event);
    }

    private void clearSearch() {
        searchQuery.setLength(0);
        selectedSearchIndex = 0;
    }

    private void toggleExpanded(Module module) {
        if (!expandedModules.add(module)) {
            expandedModules.remove(module);
        }
    }

    private void commitTextField() {
        Setting<?> setting = focusedField;
        focusedField = null;
        if (setting == null) {
            return;
        }
        applyTextValue(setting, textBuffer.toString());
        ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private void applyTextValue(Setting<?> setting, String text) {
        Class<?> type = setting.getType();
        try {
            if (type == String.class) {
                ((Setting<String>) setting).set(text);
            } else if (type == Integer.class) {
                ((Setting<Integer>) setting).set(Integer.parseInt(text.trim()));
            } else if (type == Double.class) {
                ((Setting<Double>) setting).set(Double.parseDouble(text.trim()));
            }
        } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
            // Invalid input: keep the previous value.
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private void handleSettingClick(Setting<?> setting, Rect control, double mx) {
        Class<?> type = setting.getType();

        if (guiModule != null && setting == guiModule.getThemeSetting()) {
            cycleTheme();
            return;
        }

        if (type == Boolean.class) {
            Setting<Boolean> boolSetting = (Setting<Boolean>) setting;
            boolSetting.set(!Boolean.TRUE.equals(boolSetting.get()));
            ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
        } else if (type == ColorValue.class) {
            openColorPicker((Setting<ColorValue>) setting, control);
        } else if (type == Keybind.class) {
            capturingKeybind = (Setting<Keybind>) setting;
        } else if ((type == Integer.class || type == Double.class) && setting.getMin() != null && setting.getMax() != null) {
            draggingSetting = setting;
            draggingTrackRect = new Rect(control.x, control.y, sliderTrackWidth(control), control.h);
            applySliderValue(setting, draggingTrackRect, mx);
        } else if (type.isEnum()) {
            cycleEnum(setting);
            ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
        } else {
            focusedField = setting;
            Object current = setting.get();
            textBuffer = new StringBuilder(current == null ? "" : current.toString());
        }
    }

    /** Advances to the next registered theme; takes effect immediately and persists. */
    private void cycleTheme() {
        List<String> names = ThemeManager.names();
        if (names.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        Theme active = ThemeManager.getActive();
        int index = active != null ? names.indexOf(active.name()) : -1;
        String next = names.get((index + 1) % names.size());
        ThemeManager.setActive(next);
        if (guiModule != null) {
            guiModule.setThemeName(next);
        }
        ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
        dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager.show("Theme: " + next);

        // A theme may ship its own menu layout — reopen through its factory if so.
        Screen replacement = openScreen();
        if (this.minecraft != null && !replacement.getClass().equals(getClass())) {
            this.minecraft.setScreenAndShow(replacement);
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
    private void cycleEnum(Setting<?> setting) {
        Object[] constants = setting.getType().getEnumConstants();
        if (constants.length == 0) {
            return;
        }
        Object current = setting.get();
        int index = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < constants.length; i++) {
            if (constants[i] == current) {
                index = i;
                break;
            }
        }
        Object next = constants[(index + 1) % constants.length];
        ((Setting) setting).set(next);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private void applySliderValue(Setting<?> setting, Rect track, double mouseX) {
        double min = ((Number) setting.getMin()).doubleValue();
        double max = ((Number) setting.getMax()).doubleValue();
        double ratio = track.w <= 0 ? 0 : (mouseX - track.x) / track.w;
        ratio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ratio));
        double value = min + ratio * (max - min);

        if (setting.getType() == Integer.class) {
            ((Setting<Integer>) setting).set((int) Math.round(value));
        } else {
            ((Setting<Double>) setting).set(value);
        }
        ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
    }

    private static final int PICKER_WIDTH = 148;
    private static final int PICKER_HEIGHT = 150;
    private static final int PICKER_SV_SIZE = 96;
    private static final int PICKER_PAD = 8;

    private void openColorPicker(Setting<ColorValue> setting, Rect control) {
        pickerSetting = setting;
        pickerDragMode = 0;

        ColorValue value = setting.get();
        int argb = value != null ? value.argb() : 0xFFFFFFFF;
        double[] hsv = rgbToHsv(argb);
        pickerHue = hsv[0];
        pickerSat = hsv[1];
        pickerVal = hsv[2];

        int x = control.x + control.w + 8;
        int y = control.y - PICKER_HEIGHT / 2;
        if (x + PICKER_WIDTH > panelX + panelWidth) {
            x = control.x - PICKER_WIDTH - 8;
        }
        x = clamp(x, 0, Math.max(0, this.width - PICKER_WIDTH));
        y = clamp(y, panelY, Math.max(panelY, this.height - PICKER_HEIGHT));
        pickerRect = new Rect(x, y, PICKER_WIDTH, PICKER_HEIGHT);
    }

    private void closeColorPicker() {
        pickerSetting = null;
        pickerRect = null;
        pickerDragMode = 0;
    }

    private void renderColorPicker(GuiGraphicsExtractor g) {
        if (pickerSetting == null || pickerRect == null) {
            return;
        }

        Rect r = pickerRect;
        fillRoundedRect(g, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, PANEL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_PANEL, frameAlpha));
        strokeRoundedRect(g, r.x, r.y, r.w, r.h, PANEL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(0x30FFFFFF, frameAlpha));

        int svX = r.x + PICKER_PAD;
        int svY = r.y + PICKER_PAD;
        for (int sy = 0; sy < PICKER_SV_SIZE; sy += 4) {
            double val = 1.0 - (double) sy / PICKER_SV_SIZE;
            for (int sx = 0; sx < PICKER_SV_SIZE; sx += 4) {
                double sat = (double) sx / PICKER_SV_SIZE;
                int rgb = hsvToRgb(pickerHue, sat, val);
                g.fill(svX + sx, svY + sy, svX + sx + 4, svY + sy + 4, applyAlpha(0xFF000000 | rgb, frameAlpha));
            }
        }

        int markX = svX + (int) (pickerSat * (PICKER_SV_SIZE - 4));
        int markY = svY + (int) ((1.0 - pickerVal) * (PICKER_SV_SIZE - 4));
        g.outline(markX - 1, markY - 1, 6, 6, applyAlpha(0xFFFFFFFF, frameAlpha));

        int hueY = r.y + PICKER_PAD + PICKER_SV_SIZE + 8;
        for (int hx = 0; hx < PICKER_SV_SIZE; hx += 2) {
            int rgb = hsvToRgb((double) hx / PICKER_SV_SIZE * 360.0, 1.0, 1.0);
            g.fill(svX + hx, hueY, svX + hx + 2, hueY + 10, applyAlpha(0xFF000000 | rgb, frameAlpha));
        }
        int hueMarkX = svX + (int) (pickerHue / 360.0 * (PICKER_SV_SIZE - 2));
        g.fill(hueMarkX, hueY - 2, hueMarkX + 2, hueY + 12, applyAlpha(0xFFFFFFFF, frameAlpha));

        int previewX = r.x + PICKER_PAD + PICKER_SV_SIZE + 8;
        int currentRgb = hsvToRgb(pickerHue, pickerSat, pickerVal);
        fillRoundedRect(g, previewX, svY, 28, 28, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(0xFF000000 | currentRgb, frameAlpha));
        strokeRoundedRect(g, previewX, svY, 28, 28, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(0x30FFFFFF, frameAlpha));

        int chipY = hueY + 18;
        for (int i = 0; i < COLOR_PRESETS.length; i++) {
            int chipX = r.x + PICKER_PAD + i * 16;
            fillRoundedRect(g, chipX, chipY, 12, 12, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_PRESETS[i], frameAlpha));
            strokeRoundedRect(g, chipX, chipY, 12, 12, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(0x30FFFFFF, frameAlpha));
        }
    }

    private boolean handlePickerClick(double mx, double my) {
        if (pickerSetting == null || pickerRect == null) {
            return false;
        }
        if (!pickerRect.contains(mx, my)) {
            closeColorPicker();
            return true;
        }

        Rect r = pickerRect;
        double lx = mx - r.x;
        double ly = my - r.y;

        if (lx >= PICKER_PAD && lx < PICKER_PAD + PICKER_SV_SIZE && ly >= PICKER_PAD && ly < PICKER_PAD + PICKER_SV_SIZE) {
            pickerDragMode = 1;
            updatePickerFromMouse(mx, my);
            return true;
        }

        int hueTop = PICKER_PAD + PICKER_SV_SIZE + 6;
        if (lx >= PICKER_PAD && lx < PICKER_PAD + PICKER_SV_SIZE && ly >= hueTop && ly < hueTop + 14) {
            pickerDragMode = 2;
            updatePickerFromMouse(mx, my);
            return true;
        }

        int chipTop = PICKER_PAD + PICKER_SV_SIZE + 26;
        if (ly >= chipTop && ly < chipTop + 12) {
            int index = (int) ((lx - PICKER_PAD) / 16);
            if (index >= 0 && index < COLOR_PRESETS.length && (lx - PICKER_PAD) % 16 < 12) {
                double[] hsv = rgbToHsv(COLOR_PRESETS[index]);
                pickerHue = hsv[0];
                pickerSat = hsv[1];
                pickerVal = hsv[2];
                applyPickerColor();
            }
        }
        return true;
    }

    private void updatePickerFromMouse(double mx, double my) {
        if (pickerRect == null) {
            return;
        }
        double lx = mx - pickerRect.x - PICKER_PAD;
        double ly = my - pickerRect.y - PICKER_PAD;

        if (pickerDragMode == 1) {
            pickerSat = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, lx / PICKER_SV_SIZE));
            pickerVal = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 1.0 - ly / PICKER_SV_SIZE));
        } else if (pickerDragMode == 2) {
            pickerHue = Math.max(0, Math.min(360, lx / PICKER_SV_SIZE * 360.0));
        }
        applyPickerColor();
    }

    private void applyPickerColor() {
        if (pickerSetting == null) {
            return;
        }
        ColorValue old = pickerSetting.get();
        int alpha = old != null ? (old.argb() >>> 24) : 0xFF;
        if (alpha == 0) {
            alpha = 0xFF;
        }
        pickerSetting.set(new ColorValue((alpha << 24) | hsvToRgb(pickerHue, pickerSat, pickerVal)));
        ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
    }

    private static int hsvToRgb(double hue, double sat, double val) {
        double c = val * sat;
        double h = (hue % 360) / 60.0;
        double x = c * (1 - Math.abs(h % 2 - 1));
        double r = 0;
        double g = 0;
        double b = 0;
        if (h < 1) {
            r = c;
            g = x;
        } else if (h < 2) {
            r = x;
            g = c;
        } else if (h < 3) {
            g = c;
            b = x;
        } else if (h < 4) {
            g = x;
            b = c;
        } else if (h < 5) {
            r = x;
            b = c;
        } else {
            r = c;
            b = x;
        }
        double m = val - c;
        int ri = (int) Math.round((r + m) * 255);
        int gi = (int) Math.round((g + m) * 255);
        int bi = (int) Math.round((b + m) * 255);
        return (ri << 16) | (gi << 8) | bi;
    }

    private static double[] rgbToHsv(int argb) {
        double r = ((argb >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255.0;
        double g = ((argb >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255.0;
        double b = (argb & 0xFF) / 255.0;
        double max = Math.max(r, Math.max(g, b));
        double min = Math.min(r, Math.min(g, b));
        double delta = max - min;

        double hue;
        if (delta == 0) {
            hue = 0;
        } else if (max == r) {
            hue = 60 * (((g - b) / delta) % 6);
        } else if (max == g) {
            hue = 60 * ((b - r) / delta + 2);
        } else {
            hue = 60 * ((r - g) / delta + 4);
        }
        if (hue < 0) {
            hue += 360;
        }

        double sat = max == 0 ? 0 : delta / max;
        return new double[]{hue, sat, max};
    }

    private Rect closeButtonRect() {
        int x = panelX + panelWidth - PANEL_PADDING - CLOSE_BUTTON_SIZE;
        int y = panelY + (TOP_BAR_HEIGHT - CLOSE_BUTTON_SIZE) / 2;
        return new Rect(x, y, CLOSE_BUTTON_SIZE, CLOSE_BUTTON_SIZE);
    }

    private Rect resizeHandleRect() {
        int x = panelX + panelWidth - RESIZE_HANDLE_SIZE;
        int y = panelY + panelHeight - RESIZE_HANDLE_SIZE;
        return new Rect(x, y, RESIZE_HANDLE_SIZE, RESIZE_HANDLE_SIZE);
    }

    private Rect favoritesRowRect() {
        int y = panelY + TOP_BAR_HEIGHT + PANEL_PADDING / 2;
        return new Rect(panelX, y, RAIL_WIDTH, RAIL_ITEM_HEIGHT);
    }

    private List<CategoryRow> computeCategoryRows() {
        List<CategoryRow> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        int y = favoritesRowRect().y + RAIL_ITEM_HEIGHT;
        for (Category category : Category.values()) {
            rows.add(new CategoryRow(category, new Rect(panelX, y, RAIL_WIDTH, RAIL_ITEM_HEIGHT)));
            y += RAIL_ITEM_HEIGHT;
        }
        return rows;
    }

    private Rect contentRegion() {
        int regionX = panelX + RAIL_WIDTH + PANEL_PADDING;
        int regionY = panelY + TOP_BAR_HEIGHT + PANEL_PADDING;
        int regionWidth = panelWidth - RAIL_WIDTH - PANEL_PADDING * 2;
        int regionBottom = panelY + panelHeight - PANEL_PADDING;
        return new Rect(regionX, regionY, regionWidth, regionBottom - regionY);
    }

    private List<CardEntry> computeCardEntries() {
        List<CardEntry> entries = new ArrayList<>();

        Rect region = contentRegion();
        int regionX = region.x;
        int regionY = region.y;

        // Responsive: drop to a single column when there is not enough width for two cards
        // wide enough to hold their setting controls.
        int columns = ((region.w - GRID_GUTTER) / 2 >= TWO_COLUMN_MIN_COLUMN) ? 2 : 1;
        int columnWidth = columns == 2 ? (region.w - GRID_GUTTER) / 2 : region.w;

        List<Module> modules = new ArrayList<>();
        if (showingFavorites) {
            for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
                if (module.isFavorite()) {
                    modules.add(module);
                }
            }
            modules.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(Module::getFavoriteOrder));
        } else {
            modules.addAll(ModuleManager.getInstance().getModulesByCategory(selectedCategory));
            modules.sort(Comparator.comparing(Module::getName));
        }

        int maxScroll = Math.max(0, contentTotalHeight - region.h);
        scrollOffset = Math.max(0, Math.min(scrollOffset, maxScroll));

        int y = regionY - (int) Math.round(scrollOffset);
        for (int i = 0; i < modules.size(); i += columns) {
            int rowHeight = 0;
            for (int c = 0; c < columns && i + c < modules.size(); c++) {
                Module module = modules.get(i + c);
                int height = cardHeight(module);
                rowHeight = Math.max(rowHeight, height);
                int cardX = regionX + c * (columnWidth + GRID_GUTTER);
                entries.add(buildCardEntry(module, cardX, y, columnWidth, height));
            }
            y += rowHeight + CARD_ROW_SPACING;
        }

        contentTotalHeight = (y + (int) Math.round(scrollOffset)) - regionY;
        return entries;
    }

    private int cardHeight(Module module) {
        Animator animator = expandAnimators.get(module);
        double progress = animator != null ? animator.value() : (expandedModules.contains(module) ? 1 : 0);
        return CARD_HEIGHT + (int) Math.round(settingsBlockHeight(module) * progress);
    }

    private int settingsBlockHeight(Module module) {
        int count = visibleSettings(module).size();
        if (count == 0) {
            return 0;
        }
        return SETTINGS_TOP_PADDING + count * SETTINGS_ROW_HEIGHT + SETTINGS_BOTTOM_PADDING;
    }

    private List<Setting<?>> visibleSettings(Module module) {
        List<Setting<?>> visible = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Setting<?> setting : module.getSettings()) {
            if (setting.isVisible()) {
                visible.add(setting);
            }
        }
        return visible;
    }

    private CardEntry buildCardEntry(Module module, int x, int y, int w, int h) {
        Rect rect = new Rect(x, y, w, h);
        Rect toggleRect = new Rect(x + w - 12 - TOGGLE_WIDTH, y + 12, TOGGLE_WIDTH, TOGGLE_HEIGHT);
        Rect starRect = new Rect(x + 4, y + 4, 16, 16);
        Rect bindRect = new Rect(x + w - 12 - 52, y + CARD_HEIGHT - 20, 52, 14);

        List<SettingRow> settingRows = new ArrayList<>();
        if (expandedModules.contains(module)) {
            // Shrink the control when the card is narrow so it never overflows or hides the label.
            int controlWidth = Math.max(48, Math.min(CONTROL_WIDTH, w - 24 - 50));
            List<Setting<?>> visible = visibleSettings(module);
            for (int i = 0; i < visible.size(); i++) {
                int rowY = y + CARD_HEIGHT + SETTINGS_TOP_PADDING + i * SETTINGS_ROW_HEIGHT;
                Rect labelArea = new Rect(x + 12, rowY, w - 24 - controlWidth - 8, SETTINGS_ROW_HEIGHT);
                int controlY = rowY + (SETTINGS_ROW_HEIGHT - CONTROL_HEIGHT) / 2;
                Rect controlRect = new Rect(x + w - 12 - controlWidth, controlY, controlWidth, CONTROL_HEIGHT);
                settingRows.add(new SettingRow(visible.get(i), labelArea, controlRect));
            }
        }

        return new CardEntry(module, rect, toggleRect, starRect, bindRect, settingRows);
    }

    private List<SearchResult> computeSearchResults(String query) {
        List<SearchResult> results = new ArrayList<>();
        if (query.isEmpty()) {
            return results;
        }

        for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
            MatchResult nameMatch = match(module.getName(), query);
            MatchResult best = nameMatch;
            best = betterMatch(best, match(module.getDescription(), query));
            for (Setting<?> setting : module.getSettings()) {
                best = betterMatch(best, match(setting.getName(), query));
            }
            if (best != null) {
                results.add(new SearchResult(module, best, nameMatch));
            }
        }

        results.sort(Comparator
                .comparingInt((SearchResult r) -> r.best().tier())
                .thenComparingInt(r -> r.best().startIndex())
                .thenComparing(r -> r.module().getName()));
        return results;
    }

    private List<SearchRowEntry> computeSearchRows() {
        List<SearchRowEntry> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        List<SearchResult> results = computeSearchResults(searchQuery.toString());
        Rect region = contentRegion();

        for (int i = 0; i < results.size(); i++) {
            int y = region.y + i * SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT;
            if (y + SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT > region.y + region.h) {
                break;
            }
            rows.add(new SearchRowEntry(results.get(i), new Rect(region.x, y, region.w, SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT)));
        }
        return rows;
    }

    private void renderSearchResults(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        List<SearchRowEntry> rows = computeSearchRows();
        if (rows.isEmpty()) {
            Rect region = contentRegion();
            themedText(g,"No matches", region.x, region.y, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, frameAlpha));
            return;
        }

        int index = Math.max(0, Math.min(selectedSearchIndex, rows.size() - 1));

        for (int i = 0; i < rows.size(); i++) {
            SearchRowEntry row = rows.get(i);
            Rect rect = row.rect();
            boolean selected = i == index;
            boolean hovered = rect.contains(mouseX, mouseY);
            int rowBottom = rect.h - 2;

            if (selected) {
                fillRoundedRect(g, rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rowBottom, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_TINT, frameAlpha));
                strokeRoundedRect(g, rect.x, rect.y, rect.w, rowBottom, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_FILL, frameAlpha));
            } else if (hovered) {
                g.fill(rect.x, rect.y, rect.x + rect.w, rect.y + rowBottom, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));
            }

            Module module = row.result().module();
            int textY = rect.y + (rowBottom - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;

            // Enabled indicator dot on the left.
            boolean on = module.isEnabled();
            int dotColor = on ? COLOR_ACCENT_FILL : 0xFF5A5F66;
            fillRoundedRect(g, rect.x + 6, rect.y + rowBottom / 2 - 3, 6, 6, 3, applyAlpha(dotColor, frameAlpha));

            int[] highlight = row.result().nameMatch() != null ? row.result().nameMatch().matchedIndices() : null;
            drawHighlightedText(g, module.getName(), highlight, rect.x + 18, textY, on ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, COLOR_ACCENT);

            String categoryLabel = "[" + module.getCategory().name() + "]";
            int labelWidth = this.font.width(categoryLabel);
            themedText(g,categoryLabel, rect.x + rect.w - 8 - labelWidth, textY, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, frameAlpha));
        }
    }

    private void drawHighlightedText(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, String text, int[] matchedIndices, int x, int y, int baseColor, int highlightColor) {
        Set<Integer> matched = new HashSet<>();
        if (matchedIndices != null) {
            for (int idx : matchedIndices) {
                matched.add(idx);
            }
        }

        int cursorX = x;
        for (int i = 0; i < text.length(); i++) {
            String ch = String.valueOf(text.charAt(i));
            int color = matched.contains(i) ? highlightColor : baseColor;
            themedText(g,ch, cursorX, y, applyAlpha(color, frameAlpha));
            cursorX += this.font.width(ch);
        }
    }

    private static MatchResult match(String field, String query) {
        if (query.isEmpty() || field.isEmpty()) {
            return null;
        }

        String lowerField = field.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        String lowerQuery = query.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);

        if (lowerField.startsWith(lowerQuery)) {
            return new MatchResult(0, 0, sequentialIndices(0, lowerQuery.length()));
        }

        for (int i = 1; i < lowerField.length(); i++) {
            boolean boundary = !Character.isLetterOrDigit(lowerField.charAt(i - 1));
            if (boundary && lowerField.startsWith(lowerQuery, i)) {
                return new MatchResult(1, i, sequentialIndices(i, lowerQuery.length()));
            }
        }

        int[] fuzzyIndices = new int[lowerQuery.length()];
        int searchFrom = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < lowerQuery.length(); i++) {
            int idx = lowerField.indexOf(lowerQuery.charAt(i), searchFrom);
            if (idx < 0) {
                return null;
            }
            fuzzyIndices[i] = idx;
            searchFrom = idx + 1;
        }
        return new MatchResult(2, fuzzyIndices[0], fuzzyIndices);
    }

    private static int[] sequentialIndices(int start, int length) {
        int[] indices = new int[length];
        for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
            indices[i] = start + i;
        }
        return indices;
    }

    private static MatchResult betterMatch(MatchResult a, MatchResult b) {
        if (a == null) {
            return b;
        }
        if (b == null) {
            return a;
        }
        if (a.tier() != b.tier()) {
            return a.tier() < b.tier() ? a : b;
        }
        return a.startIndex() <= b.startIndex() ? a : b;
    }

    private ConfigLayout computeConfigLayout() {
        Rect region = contentRegion();
        List<String> profiles = ConfigManager.listProfiles();

        List<ConfigProfileRow> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        int y = region.y;
        for (String name : profiles) {
            Rect rect = new Rect(region.x, y, region.w, SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT);
            Rect deleteRect = new Rect(region.x + region.w - 24, y, 20, SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT - 2);
            rows.add(new ConfigProfileRow(name, rect, deleteRect));
            y += SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT;
        }

        Rect newProfileRow = new Rect(region.x, y, region.w, SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT);
        y += SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT + 8;

        Rect exportRect = new Rect(region.x, y, 90, 20);
        Rect importRect = new Rect(region.x + 98, y, 90, 20);

        return new ConfigLayout(rows, newProfileRow, exportRect, importRect);
    }

    private void renderConfigPanel(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        ConfigLayout layout = computeConfigLayout();
        String active = ConfigManager.getActiveProfile();

        for (ConfigProfileRow row : layout.profileRows()) {
            boolean isActive = row.name().equals(active);
            boolean hovered = row.rect().contains(mouseX, mouseY);
            int rowBottom = row.rect().h - 2;

            if (isActive) {
                fillRoundedRect(g, row.rect().x, row.rect().y, row.rect().w, rowBottom, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_TINT, frameAlpha));
                strokeRoundedRect(g, row.rect().x, row.rect().y, row.rect().w, rowBottom, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_FILL, frameAlpha));
            } else if (hovered) {
                g.fill(row.rect().x, row.rect().y, row.rect().x + row.rect().w, row.rect().y + rowBottom, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));
            }

            int textY = row.rect().y + (rowBottom - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
            themedText(g,row.name(), row.rect().x + 8, textY, applyAlpha(isActive ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, frameAlpha));

            if (!isActive && layout.profileRows().size() > 1) {
                themedText(g,"×", row.deleteRect().x, textY, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, frameAlpha));
            }
        }

        Rect newRow = layout.newProfileRow();
        strokeRoundedRect(g, newRow.x, newRow.y, newRow.w, newRow.h - 2, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(creatingProfile ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));
        String newLabel = creatingProfile ? newProfileBuffer.toString() + "_" : "+ New profile";
        int newTextY = newRow.y + (newRow.h - 2 - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
        themedText(g,newLabel, newRow.x + 8, newTextY, applyAlpha(creatingProfile ? COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY : COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, frameAlpha));

        Rect exportRect = layout.exportRect();
        Rect importRect = layout.importRect();
        strokeRoundedRect(g, exportRect.x, exportRect.y, exportRect.w, exportRect.h, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));
        themedText(g,"Export", exportRect.x + 8, exportRect.y + (exportRect.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, frameAlpha));
        strokeRoundedRect(g, importRect.x, importRect.y, importRect.w, importRect.h, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));
        themedText(g,"Import", importRect.x + 8, importRect.y + (importRect.h - this.font.lineHeight) / 2, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, frameAlpha));
    }

    private void handleConfigClick(double mx, double my) {
        ConfigLayout layout = computeConfigLayout();

        for (ConfigProfileRow row : layout.profileRows()) {
            if (row.deleteRect().contains(mx, my) && !row.name().equals(ConfigManager.getActiveProfile()) && layout.profileRows().size() > 1) {
                ConfigManager.deleteProfile(row.name());
                return;
            }
            if (row.rect().contains(mx, my)) {
                ConfigManager.switchProfile(row.name());
                return;
            }
        }

        if (layout.newProfileRow().contains(mx, my)) {
            creatingProfile = true;
            newProfileBuffer = new StringBuilder();
            return;
        }

        if (layout.exportRect().contains(mx, my)) {
            this.minecraft.keyboardHandler.setClipboard(ConfigManager.exportActiveProfile());
            return;
        }

        if (layout.importRect().contains(mx, my)) {
            String clipboard = this.minecraft.keyboardHandler.getClipboard();
            if (clipboard != null && !clipboard.isBlank()) {
                try {
                    ConfigManager.importActiveProfile(clipboard);
                } catch (RuntimeException ignored) {
                    // Invalid clipboard content: ignore.
                }
            }
            return;
        }

        creatingProfile = false;
    }

    private List<AccountRow> computeAccountRows() {
        Rect region = contentRegion();
        List<AccountRow> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        int y = region.y;
        for (String name : AccountManager.getAccounts()) {
            Rect rect = new Rect(region.x, y, region.w, SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT);
            Rect deleteRect = new Rect(region.x + region.w - 24, y, 20, SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT - 2);
            rows.add(new AccountRow(name, rect, deleteRect));
            y += SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT;
        }
        return rows;
    }

    private Rect newAccountRowRect() {
        Rect region = contentRegion();
        int y = region.y + AccountManager.getAccounts().size() * SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT;
        return new Rect(region.x, y, region.w, SEARCH_ROW_HEIGHT);
    }

    private void renderAccountsPanel(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int mouseX, int mouseY) {
        Rect region = contentRegion();
        String selected = AccountManager.getSelected();

        String note = "Offline usernames only — no passwords or Microsoft login.";
        themedText(g,note, region.x, region.y - 26, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, frameAlpha));
        themedText(g,"Current: " + AccountManager.getCurrentUsername(), region.x, region.y - 14, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, frameAlpha));

        for (AccountRow row : computeAccountRows()) {
            boolean isActive = row.name().equals(selected);
            boolean hovered = row.rect().contains(mouseX, mouseY);
            int rowBottom = row.rect().h - 2;

            if (isActive) {
                fillRoundedRect(g, row.rect().x, row.rect().y, row.rect().w, rowBottom, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_TINT, frameAlpha));
                strokeRoundedRect(g, row.rect().x, row.rect().y, row.rect().w, rowBottom, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(COLOR_ACCENT_FILL, frameAlpha));
            } else if (hovered) {
                g.fill(row.rect().x, row.rect().y, row.rect().x + row.rect().w, row.rect().y + rowBottom, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));
            }

            int textY = row.rect().y + (rowBottom - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
            themedText(g,row.name(), row.rect().x + 8, textY, applyAlpha(isActive ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY, frameAlpha));
            themedText(g,"×", row.deleteRect().x, textY, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, frameAlpha));
        }

        Rect newRow = newAccountRowRect();
        strokeRoundedRect(g, newRow.x, newRow.y, newRow.w, newRow.h - 2, CONTROL_RADIUS, applyAlpha(creatingAccount ? COLOR_ACCENT : COLOR_BORDER, frameAlpha));
        String newLabel = creatingAccount ? newAccountBuffer.toString() + "_" : "+ Add offline account";
        int newTextY = newRow.y + (newRow.h - 2 - this.font.lineHeight) / 2;
        themedText(g,newLabel, newRow.x + 8, newTextY, applyAlpha(creatingAccount ? COLOR_TEXT_PRIMARY : COLOR_TEXT_SECONDARY, frameAlpha));
    }

    private void handleAccountsClick(double mx, double my) {
        for (AccountRow row : computeAccountRows()) {
            if (row.deleteRect().contains(mx, my)) {
                AccountManager.remove(row.name());
                return;
            }
            if (row.rect().contains(mx, my)) {
                boolean applied = AccountManager.select(row.name());
                dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager.show(applied
                        ? "Account set to " + row.name() + " (reconnect for offline servers)"
                        : "Saved selection; runtime swap unavailable");
                return;
            }
        }

        if (newAccountRowRect().contains(mx, my)) {
            creatingAccount = true;
            newAccountBuffer = new StringBuilder();
            return;
        }

        creatingAccount = false;
    }

    private static int clamp(int value, int min, int max) {
        return Math.max(min, Math.min(value, Math.max(min, max)));
    }

    private static int applyAlpha(int color, double alpha) {
        int baseAlpha = (color >>> 24) == 0 ? 0xFF : (color >>> 24);
        int scaled = (int) Math.round(baseAlpha * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha)));
        return (scaled << 24) | (color & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }

    private static int lerpColor(int fromColor, int toColor, double t) {
        t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
        int fromA = (fromColor >>> 24) == 0 ? 0xFF : (fromColor >>> 24);
        int toA = (toColor >>> 24) == 0 ? 0xFF : (toColor >>> 24);
        int a = (int) Math.round(fromA + (toA - fromA) * t);
        int r = (int) Math.round(((fromColor >> 16) & 0xFF) + (((toColor >> 16) & 0xFF) - ((fromColor >> 16) & 0xFF)) * t);
        int gr = (int) Math.round(((fromColor >> 8) & 0xFF) + (((toColor >> 8) & 0xFF) - ((fromColor >> 8) & 0xFF)) * t);
        int b = (int) Math.round((fromColor & 0xFF) + ((toColor & 0xFF) - (fromColor & 0xFF)) * t);
        return (a << 24) | (r << 16) | (gr << 8) | b;
    }

    private static void fillRoundedRect(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int x, int y, int width, int height, int radius, int color) {
        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) {
            return;
        }

        int r = Math.max(0, Math.min(radius, Math.min(width, height) / 2));
        if (r == 0) {
            g.fill(x, y, x + width, y + height, color);
            return;
        }

        g.fill(x + r, y, x + width - r, y + height, color);
        g.fill(x, y + r, x + r, y + height - r, color);
        g.fill(x + width - r, y + r, x + width, y + height - r, color);

        fillCornerDisk(g, x + r - 1, y + r - 1, r, color, -1, -1);
        fillCornerDisk(g, x + width - r, y + r - 1, r, color, 1, -1);
        fillCornerDisk(g, x + r - 1, y + height - r, r, color, -1, 1);
        fillCornerDisk(g, x + width - r, y + height - r, r, color, 1, 1);
    }

    private static void strokeRoundedRect(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int x, int y, int width, int height, int radius, int color) {
        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) {
            return;
        }

        int r = Math.max(0, Math.min(radius, Math.min(width, height) / 2));
        g.horizontalLine(x + r, x + width - r - 1, y, color);
        g.horizontalLine(x + r, x + width - r - 1, y + height - 1, color);
        g.verticalLine(x, y + r, y + height - r - 1, color);
        g.verticalLine(x + width - 1, y + r, y + height - r - 1, color);

        if (r > 0) {
            strokeCornerArc(g, x + r - 1, y + r - 1, r, color, -1, -1);
            strokeCornerArc(g, x + width - r, y + r - 1, r, color, 1, -1);
            strokeCornerArc(g, x + r - 1, y + height - r, r, color, -1, 1);
            strokeCornerArc(g, x + width - r, y + height - r, r, color, 1, 1);
        }
    }

    private static void fillCornerDisk(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int originX, int originY, int radius, int color, int signX, int signY) {
        int rSquared = radius * radius;
        for (int dy = 0; dy < radius; dy++) {
            for (int dx = 0; dx < radius; dx++) {
                if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= rSquared) {
                    int px = signX > 0 ? originX + dx : originX - dx;
                    int py = signY > 0 ? originY + dy : originY - dy;
                    g.fill(px, py, px + 1, py + 1, color);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static void strokeCornerArc(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int originX, int originY, int radius, int color, int signX, int signY) {
        int outer = radius * radius;
        int inner = (radius - 1) * (radius - 1);
        for (int dy = 0; dy < radius; dy++) {
            for (int dx = 0; dx < radius; dx++) {
                int distSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy;
                if (distSquared <= outer && distSquared > inner) {
                    int px = signX > 0 ? originX + dx : originX - dx;
                    int py = signY > 0 ? originY + dy : originY - dy;
                    g.fill(px, py, px + 1, py + 1, color);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static void fillCircle(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int centerX, int centerY, int radius, int color) {
        int rSquared = radius * radius;
        for (int dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy++) {
            int dxMax = (int) Math.sqrt(rSquared - dy * dy);
            g.fill(centerX - dxMax, centerY + dy, centerX + dxMax + 1, centerY + dy + 1, color);
        }
    }

    private enum Easing {
        LINEAR {
            @Override
            double apply(double t) {
                return t;
            }
        },
        EASE_OUT {
            @Override
            double apply(double t) {
                double p = 1 - t;
                return 1 - p * p * p;
            }
        },
        EASE_IN {
            @Override
            double apply(double t) {
                return t * t * t;
            }
        },
        EASE_OUT_BACK {
            @Override
            double apply(double t) {
                double c1 = 1.70158;
                double c3 = c1 + 1;
                double p = t - 1;
                return 1 + c3 * p * p * p + c1 * p * p;
            }
        };

        abstract double apply(double t);
    }

    private static final class Animator {
        private static boolean reduceAnimations;
        /** Theme-controlled duration multiplier; small values make every transition snappy. */
        private static double speedMultiplier = 1.0;

        private double startValue;
        private double targetValue;
        private double currentValue;
        private double elapsedMs;
        private double durationMs = 1;
        private Easing easing = Easing.LINEAR;

        void animateTo(double target, double baseDurationMs, Easing easing) {
            double durationMs = baseDurationMs * speedMultiplier;
            if (this.targetValue == target && this.durationMs == durationMs && this.easing == easing) {
                return;
            }
            this.startValue = currentValue;
            this.targetValue = target;
            this.durationMs = Math.max(1, durationMs);
            this.easing = easing;
            this.elapsedMs = 0;
        }

        void tick(double deltaMs) {
            if (reduceAnimations) {
                currentValue = targetValue;
                elapsedMs = durationMs;
                return;
            }
            if (elapsedMs >= durationMs) {
                return;
            }
            elapsedMs = Math.min(durationMs, elapsedMs + deltaMs);
            double t = elapsedMs / durationMs;
            currentValue = startValue + (targetValue - startValue) * easing.apply(t);
        }

        double value() {
            return currentValue;
        }

        double targetValue() {
            return targetValue;
        }

        double elapsedMs() {
            return elapsedMs;
        }

        boolean isSettled() {
            return elapsedMs >= durationMs;
        }
    }

    private record Rect(int x, int y, int w, int h) {
        boolean contains(double px, double py) {
            return px >= x && px < x + w && py >= y && py < y + h;
        }
    }

    private record CategoryRow(Category category, Rect rect) {
    }

    private record CardEntry(Module module, Rect rect, Rect toggleRect, Rect starRect, Rect bindRect, List<SettingRow> settingRows) {
    }

    private record SettingRow(Setting<?> setting, Rect labelArea, Rect controlRect) {
    }

    private record MatchResult(int tier, int startIndex, int[] matchedIndices) {
    }

    private record SearchResult(Module module, MatchResult best, MatchResult nameMatch) {
    }

    private record SearchRowEntry(SearchResult result, Rect rect) {
    }

    private record ConfigProfileRow(String name, Rect rect, Rect deleteRect) {
    }

    private record ConfigLayout(List<ConfigProfileRow> profileRows, Rect newProfileRow, Rect exportRect, Rect importRect) {
    }

    private record AccountRow(String name, Rect rect, Rect deleteRect) {
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/gui/ClientImage.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.gui;

import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import net.minecraft.resources.Identifier;

/**
 * The pictures shipped in {@code assets/lenrete/textures/gui}, usable anywhere the client
 * shows its logo — the ClickGUI title bar and the HUD watermark. Animated entries resolve to
 * the GIF frame due at the current time; static ones resolve to their PNG.
 */
public enum ClientImage {
    PERONA("perona.png", false),
    TORO_HAPPY("toro happy.gif", true),
    TORO_LAUGH("toro laugh.gif", true),
    FLUSTER_TORO("fluster toro.gif", true);

    private final String fileName;
    private final boolean animated;
    private final Identifier staticTexture;

    ClientImage(String fileName, boolean animated) {
        this.fileName = fileName;
        this.animated = animated;
        // Only non-animated files are addressed directly; GIF names may contain characters
        // an Identifier forbids, so those go through GifTexture's sanitized ids instead.
        this.staticTexture = animated
                ? null
                : Identifier.fromNamespaceAndPath(Lenrete.MOD_ID, "textures/gui/" + fileName);
    }

    /** The texture to draw right now, or the Perona fallback if this image failed to load. */
    public Identifier texture(long elapsedMs) {
        if (!animated) {
            return staticTexture;
        }
        GifTexture gif = GifTexture.of(fileName);
        Identifier frame = gif.frameAt(elapsedMs);
        return frame != null ? frame : PERONA.staticTexture;
    }

    public boolean isAvailable() {
        return !animated || GifTexture.of(fileName).isAvailable();
    }

    /** Human-readable label for the settings row. */
    public String label() {
        String lower = name().toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT).replace('_', ' ');
        return Character.toUpperCase(lower.charAt(0)) + lower.substring(1);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/gui/GifTexture.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.gui;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.NativeImage;
import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.texture.DynamicTexture;
import net.minecraft.resources.Identifier;
import org.w3c.dom.Node;

import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import javax.imageio.ImageReader;
import javax.imageio.metadata.IIOMetadata;
import javax.imageio.metadata.IIOMetadataNode;
import javax.imageio.stream.ImageInputStream;
import java.awt.AlphaComposite;
import java.awt.Composite;
import java.awt.Graphics2D;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Decodes an animated GIF from the mod's assets into registered dynamic textures and hands
 * back the frame due at a given time. Loading is lazy and cached per file.
 *
 * <p>GIFs are read as classpath streams, so their file names may contain characters (spaces,
 * capitals) that a Minecraft {@link Identifier} forbids; the registered texture ids are
 * sanitized separately. A file that is missing or fails to decode leaves the instance
 * unavailable and callers fall back to a static image.
 */
public final class GifTexture {
    private static final Map<String, GifTexture> CACHE = new HashMap<>();

    private final List<Identifier> frames = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<Integer> delaysMs = new ArrayList<>();
    private int totalMs;

    private GifTexture(String fileName) {
        try (InputStream in = GifTexture.class.getResourceAsStream(
                "/assets/" + Lenrete.MOD_ID + "/textures/gui/" + fileName)) {
            if (in == null) {
                Lenrete.LOGGER.warn("GUI image {} not found in assets", fileName);
                return;
            }
            decode(in, sanitize(fileName));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Failed to load GUI image {}", fileName, e);
        }
    }

    /** Cached loader; the first call decodes, later calls reuse the frames. */
    public static synchronized GifTexture of(String fileName) {
        return CACHE.computeIfAbsent(fileName, GifTexture::new);
    }

    public boolean isAvailable() {
        return !frames.isEmpty();
    }

    /** The frame to display for the given elapsed time (loops forever), or null if unavailable. */
    public Identifier frameAt(long elapsedMs) {
        if (frames.isEmpty()) {
            return null;
        }
        if (totalMs <= 0) {
            return frames.get(0);
        }
        long t = Math.floorMod(elapsedMs, totalMs);
        long acc = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < frames.size(); i++) {
            acc += delaysMs.get(i);
            if (t < acc) {
                return frames.get(i);
            }
        }
        return frames.get(frames.size() - 1);
    }

    /** Identifier paths allow only [a-z0-9_.-/]; map anything else onto an underscore. */
    private static String sanitize(String fileName) {
        String base = fileName.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).replaceAll("\\.[a-z0-9]+$", "");
        return base.replaceAll("[^a-z0-9_.-]", "_");
    }

    private void decode(InputStream in, String id) throws Exception {
        ImageInputStream iis = ImageIO.createImageInputStream(in);
        Iterator<ImageReader> readers = ImageIO.getImageReadersByFormatName("gif");
        if (!readers.hasNext()) {
            return;
        }
        ImageReader reader = readers.next();
        reader.setInput(iis);

        int count = reader.getNumImages(true);
        // The logical screen size, not frame 0 — a first frame smaller than the canvas would
        // otherwise crop every later frame.
        int[] screen = screenSize(reader, count);
        int width = screen[0];
        int height = screen[1];

        // Frames are usually partial (only the changed rectangle) and each declares how the
        // canvas must be cleaned up before the next one. Ignoring that leaves earlier frames
        // painted underneath, which reads as doubled/ghosted motion.
        BufferedImage canvas = new BufferedImage(width, height, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
        Graphics2D gfx = canvas.createGraphics();
        Minecraft minecraft = Minecraft.getInstance();

        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            IIOMetadata metadata = reader.getImageMetadata(i);
            BufferedImage frame = reader.read(i);
            int[] offset = frameOffset(metadata);
            String disposal = disposalMethod(metadata);

            // "restoreToPrevious" needs the pre-frame canvas kept aside.
            BufferedImage restorePoint = null;
            if ("restoreToPrevious".equals(disposal)) {
                restorePoint = deepCopy(canvas);
            }

            gfx.drawImage(frame, offset[0], offset[1], null);

            NativeImage image = toNativeImage(canvas);
            String textureName = "lenrete-" + id;
            DynamicTexture texture = new DynamicTexture(() -> textureName, image);
            Identifier identifier = Identifier.fromNamespaceAndPath(Lenrete.MOD_ID, id + "_frame_" + i);
            minecraft.getTextureManager().register(identifier, texture);

            frames.add(identifier);
            int delay = frameDelayMs(metadata);
            delaysMs.add(delay);
            totalMs += delay;

            // Apply this frame's disposal so the next one starts from the right canvas.
            switch (disposal) {
                case "restoreToBackgroundColor" -> {
                    Composite previous = gfx.getComposite();
                    gfx.setComposite(AlphaComposite.Clear);
                    gfx.fillRect(offset[0], offset[1], frame.getWidth(), frame.getHeight());
                    gfx.setComposite(previous);
                }
                case "restoreToPrevious" -> {
                    if (restorePoint != null) {
                        Composite previous = gfx.getComposite();
                        gfx.setComposite(AlphaComposite.Src);
                        gfx.drawImage(restorePoint, 0, 0, null);
                        gfx.setComposite(previous);
                    }
                }
                default -> {
                    // "none"/"doNotDispose": the frame stays as the base for the next one.
                }
            }
        }

        gfx.dispose();
        reader.dispose();
    }

    /** The GIF's logical screen size, falling back to the largest frame extent. */
    private static int[] screenSize(ImageReader reader, int count) throws java.io.IOException {
        int width = 0;
        int height = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            int[] offset = frameOffset(reader.getImageMetadata(i));
            width = Math.max(width, offset[0] + reader.getWidth(i));
            height = Math.max(height, offset[1] + reader.getHeight(i));
        }
        return new int[]{Math.max(1, width), Math.max(1, height)};
    }

    private static BufferedImage deepCopy(BufferedImage source) {
        BufferedImage copy = new BufferedImage(source.getWidth(), source.getHeight(), BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
        Graphics2D g = copy.createGraphics();
        g.setComposite(AlphaComposite.Src);
        g.drawImage(source, 0, 0, null);
        g.dispose();
        return copy;
    }

    /** GIF disposal method for a frame: none, doNotDispose, restoreToBackgroundColor, restoreToPrevious. */
    private static String disposalMethod(IIOMetadata metadata) {
        IIOMetadataNode control = findNode(metadata, "GraphicControlExtension");
        if (control == null) {
            return "none";
        }
        String value = control.getAttribute("disposalMethod");
        return value == null || value.isEmpty() ? "none" : value;
    }

    private static NativeImage toNativeImage(BufferedImage source) {
        int width = source.getWidth();
        int height = source.getHeight();
        NativeImage image = new NativeImage(width, height, false);
        for (int y = 0; y < height; y++) {
            for (int x = 0; x < width; x++) {
                int argb = source.getRGB(x, y);
                int a = (argb >>> 24) & 0xFF;
                int r = (argb >> 16) & 0xFF;
                int g = (argb >> 8) & 0xFF;
                int b = argb & 0xFF;
                // NativeImage stores pixels in ABGR order.
                image.setPixelABGR(x, y, (a << 24) | (b << 16) | (g << 8) | r);
            }
        }
        return image;
    }

    private static int[] frameOffset(IIOMetadata metadata) {
        IIOMetadataNode descriptor = findNode(metadata, "ImageDescriptor");
        if (descriptor == null) {
            return new int[]{0, 0};
        }
        int x = parseInt(descriptor.getAttribute("imageLeftPosition"), 0);
        int y = parseInt(descriptor.getAttribute("imageTopPosition"), 0);
        return new int[]{x, y};
    }

    private static int frameDelayMs(IIOMetadata metadata) {
        IIOMetadataNode control = findNode(metadata, "GraphicControlExtension");
        if (control == null) {
            return 100;
        }
        int centiseconds = parseInt(control.getAttribute("delayTime"), 10);
        return Math.max(20, centiseconds * 10);
    }

    private static IIOMetadataNode findNode(IIOMetadata metadata, String name) {
        if (metadata == null) {
            return null;
        }
        IIOMetadataNode root = (IIOMetadataNode) metadata.getAsTree(metadata.getNativeMetadataFormatName());
        for (Node child = root.getFirstChild(); child != null; child = child.getNextSibling()) {
            if (child.getNodeName().equalsIgnoreCase(name) && child instanceof IIOMetadataNode node) {
                return node;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static int parseInt(String value, int fallback) {
        try {
            return value == null || value.isEmpty() ? fallback : Integer.parseInt(value);
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return fallback;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/gui/GuiBackground.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.gui;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc.ClickGuiModule.BackgroundAnimation;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor;

/**
 * The animated GUI backdrops, shared by every menu layout so each theme's screen gets the
 * same set of effects. All effects are deterministic functions of elapsed time and the
 * accent color — no per-frame allocations, no retained particle state.
 */
public final class GuiBackground {
    private GuiBackground() {
    }

    /**
     * Draws the selected backdrop.
     *
     * @param elapsedMs time the screen has been open, drives every animation
     * @param accent    the active theme's accent color
     * @param alpha     0-1 fade applied on top of each effect's own opacity
     */
    public static void render(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, BackgroundAnimation animation,
                              int width, int height, double elapsedMs, int accent, double alpha) {
        if (animation == null) {
            return;
        }
        double t = elapsedMs / 1000.0;
        switch (animation) {
            case PARTICLES -> particles(g, width, height, t, accent, alpha);
            case WAVES -> waves(g, width, height, t, accent, alpha);
            case STARFIELD -> starfield(g, width, height, t, accent, alpha);
            case RAIN -> rain(g, width, height, t, accent, alpha);
            case GRID -> grid(g, width, height, t, accent, alpha);
            case SNOW -> snow(g, width, height, t, alpha);
            case MATRIX -> matrix(g, width, height, t, accent, alpha);
            case AURORA -> aurora(g, width, height, t, alpha);
            case BUBBLES -> bubbles(g, width, height, t, accent, alpha);
            case FIREFLIES -> fireflies(g, width, height, t, alpha);
            default -> {
            }
        }
    }

    private static void starfield(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int width, int height, double t, int accent, double alpha) {
        double cx = width / 2.0;
        double cy = height / 2.0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 70; i++) {
            double seed = (i * 2654435761L & 0xFFFF) / 65535.0;
            double angle = seed * Math.PI * 2;
            // Stars stream outward from the center and recycle.
            double progress = (t * (0.15 + seed * 0.35) + seed) % 1.0;
            double dist = progress * Math.max(width, height) * 0.7;
            double x = cx + Math.cos(angle) * dist;
            double y = cy + Math.sin(angle) * dist;
            if (x < 0 || x >= width || y < 0 || y >= height) {
                continue;
            }
            int size = progress > 0.6 ? 2 : 1;
            int color = applyAlpha(accent, alpha * progress);
            g.fill((int) x, (int) y, (int) x + size, (int) y + size, color);
        }
    }

    private static void rain(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int width, int height, double t, int accent, double alpha) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
            double seed = (i * 2654435761L & 0xFFFF) / 65535.0;
            double x = seed * width;
            double speed = 220 + seed * 260;
            double y = (t * speed + seed * height) % (height + 20) - 10;
            if (y < 0 || y >= height - 8) {
                continue;
            }
            int color = applyAlpha(accent, alpha * 0.4);
            g.fill((int) x, (int) y, (int) x + 1, (int) y + 8, color);
        }
    }

    private static void grid(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int width, int height, double t, int accent, double alpha) {
        int spacing = 32;
        double pulse = 0.10 + 0.06 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 1.5));
        int color = applyAlpha(accent, alpha * pulse);
        int offset = (int) ((t * 12) % spacing);
        for (int x = offset; x < width; x += spacing) {
            g.fill(x, 0, x + 1, height, color);
        }
        for (int y = offset; y < height; y += spacing) {
            g.fill(0, y, width, y + 1, color);
        }
    }

    private static void particles(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int width, int height, double t, int accent, double alpha) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 48; i++) {
            // Deterministic per-particle parameters from the index; no per-frame allocations.
            double seed = (i * 2654435761L & 0xFFFF) / 65535.0;
            double speed = 12 + seed * 26;
            double baseX = seed * width;
            double wobble = Math.sin(t * (0.6 + seed) + i) * 14;
            double y = height - ((t * speed + seed * height * 3) % (height + 8)) + 4;
            double x = baseX + wobble;

            if (x < 0 || x >= width - 2 || y < 0 || y >= height - 2) {
                continue;
            }

            int size = 1 + (i % 3);
            double twinkle = 0.35 + 0.3 * Math.sin(t * 2 + i * 1.7);
            int color = applyAlpha(accent, alpha * twinkle);
            g.fill((int) x, (int) y, (int) x + size, (int) y + size, color);
        }
    }

    private static void waves(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int width, int height, double t, int accent, double alpha) {
        for (int wave = 0; wave < 3; wave++) {
            double baseY = height * (0.72 + wave * 0.09);
            double amplitude = 10 + wave * 6;
            double frequency = 0.014 - wave * 0.003;
            double phase = t * (0.8 + wave * 0.35);
            int color = applyAlpha(accent, alpha * (0.22 - wave * 0.05));

            for (int x = 0; x < width; x += 3) {
                int y = (int) (baseY + Math.sin(x * frequency + phase) * amplitude);
                if (y >= 0 && y < height - 2) {
                    g.fill(x, y, x + 3, y + 2, color);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static void snow(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int width, int height, double t, double alpha) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 90; i++) {
            double seed = (i * 2654435761L & 0xFFFF) / 65535.0;
            double drift = Math.sin(t * (0.5 + seed) + i) * 18;
            double x = (seed * width + drift + width) % width;
            double fall = 20 + seed * 45;
            double y = (t * fall + seed * height) % (height + 6);
            if (y < 0 || y >= height - 2) {
                continue;
            }
            int size = seed > 0.66 ? 2 : 1;
            int color = applyAlpha(0xFFFFFFFF, alpha * (0.35 + seed * 0.45));
            g.fill((int) x, (int) y, (int) x + size, (int) y + size, color);
        }
    }
```
