```java
    private static void matrix(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int width, int height, double t, int accent, double alpha) {
        int columns = Math.max(1, width / 12);
        for (int c = 0; c < columns; c++) {
            double seed = (c * 2654435761L & 0xFFFF) / 65535.0;
            int x = c * 12 + 2;
            double speed = 60 + seed * 160;
            double headY = (t * speed + seed * height * 2) % (height + 60) - 30;
            for (int tail = 0; tail < 6; tail++) {
                int y = (int) (headY - tail * 10);
                if (y < 0 || y >= height - 8) {
                    continue;
                }
                double fade = (1.0 - tail / 6.0);
                int color = applyAlpha(accent, alpha * fade * 0.7);
                g.fill(x, y, x + 6, y + 8, color);
            }
        }
    }

    private static void aurora(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int width, int height, double t, double alpha) {
        // Two gradient quads per column instead of a per-pixel inner loop, so the whole
        // effect costs roughly as much as the waves animation instead of thousands of fills.
        int step = 8;
        for (int band = 0; band < 3; band++) {
            double baseY = height * (0.28 + band * 0.17);
            double amplitude = 26 + band * 10;
            double frequency = 0.011 + band * 0.002;
            double phase = t * (0.45 + band * 0.2);
            for (int x = 0; x < width; x += step) {
                int coreY = (int) (baseY + Math.sin(x * frequency + phase) * amplitude);
                if (coreY + 22 < 0 || coreY - 8 >= height) {
                    continue;
                }
                double hue = ((x / (double) width) + t * 0.08 + band * 0.25) % 1.0;
                int base = hsvColor(hue);
                int strong = applyAlpha(base, alpha * 0.16);
                int clear = base & 0x00FFFFFF;
                // Fade in above the core line, fade out below it.
                g.fillGradient(x, coreY - 8, x + step, coreY, clear, strong);
                g.fillGradient(x, coreY, x + step, coreY + 22, strong, clear);
            }
        }
    }

    private static void bubbles(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int width, int height, double t, int accent, double alpha) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
            double seed = (i * 2654435761L & 0xFFFF) / 65535.0;
            double sway = Math.sin(t * (0.7 + seed) + i * 1.3) * 12;
            double x = seed * width + sway;
            double speed = 18 + seed * 34;
            double y = height - ((t * speed + seed * height) % (height + 20));
            if (x < 0 || x >= width || y < 0 || y >= height) {
                continue;
            }
            int radius = 2 + (i % 4);
            int color = applyAlpha(accent, alpha * 0.30);
            // Simple ring: top/bottom/left/right pixels for a hollow-bubble look.
            g.fill((int) x - radius, (int) y, (int) x + radius, (int) y + 1, color);
            g.fill((int) x, (int) y - radius, (int) x + 1, (int) y + radius, color);
        }
    }

    private static void fireflies(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int width, int height, double t, double alpha) {
        for (int i = 0; i < 44; i++) {
            double seed = (i * 2654435761L & 0xFFFF) / 65535.0;
            double seed2 = ((i * 40503L) & 0xFFFF) / 65535.0;
            double x = (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * (0.3 + seed * 0.5) + i)) * width;
            double y = (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.cos(t * (0.25 + seed2 * 0.5) + i * 1.7)) * height;
            double glow = 0.3 + 0.5 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * 3 + i * 2.1));
            int size = glow > 0.6 ? 2 : 1;
            int color = applyAlpha(0xFFB6FF6C, alpha * glow);
            g.fill((int) x, (int) y, (int) x + size, (int) y + size, color);
        }
    }

    private static int applyAlpha(int color, double alpha) {
        int baseAlpha = (color >>> 24) == 0 ? 0xFF : (color >>> 24);
        int scaled = (int) Math.round(baseAlpha * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha)));
        return (scaled << 24) | (color & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }

    private static int hsvColor(double hue) {
        return 0xFF000000 | (java.awt.Color.HSBtoRGB((float) hue, 0.7f, 1.0f) & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.gui;

/**
 * Marker for the mod's own GUI screens, so the open/close keybind can recognise and dismiss
 * whichever layout the active theme provides.
 */
public interface LenreteScreen {
    /** Begins closing the screen (may play a close animation before it actually goes away). */
    void requestClose();
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/gui/theme/ClassicTheme.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.gui.theme;

/**
 * Old-school cheat client aesthetic (Nodus / Huzuni / Wolfram era): hard blocky edges,
 * near-black translucent panels, a bright cyan accent with a purple secondary, gradient
 * headers, Minecraft font with drop shadow, and snappy, no-nonsense transitions.
 * Clean and readable — not cluttered.
 */
public class ClassicTheme implements Theme {
    private static final int CYAN = 0xFF00CFE8;
    private static final int PURPLE = 0xFF8B5CF6;

    @Override
    public String name() {
        return "Classic";
    }

    private static dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc.ClickGuiModule gui() {
        return dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager.getInstance()
                .getModule(dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc.ClickGuiModule.class);
    }

    /** Darkens a color toward black — used for the header gradient's lower half. */
    private static int darken(int color, double factor) {
        int a = color >>> 24;
        int r = (int) (((color >> 16) & 0xFF) * factor);
        int g = (int) (((color >> 8) & 0xFF) * factor);
        int b = (int) ((color & 0xFF) * factor);
        return (a << 24) | (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ colors

    @Override
    public int background() {
        return 0xA6050507;
    }

    @Override
    public int panelBackground() {
        var g = gui();
        return g != null ? g.getClassicPanelColor() : 0xFF0A0A0E;
    }

    @Override
    public int header() {
        return accent();
    }

    @Override
    public int headerGradientEnd() {
        return darken(accent(), 0.45);
    }

    @Override
    public int accent() {
        var g = gui();
        return g != null ? g.getClassicAccent() : CYAN;
    }

    @Override
    public int accentHover() {
        var g = gui();
        return g != null ? g.getClassicSecondary() : PURPLE;
    }

    @Override
    public int textPrimary() {
        return 0xFFF0F0F0;
    }

    @Override
    public int textSecondary() {
        return 0xFF9CA3AF;
    }

    @Override
    public int textDisabled() {
        return 0xFF6B7280;
    }

    @Override
    public int enabledToggle() {
        return accent();
    }

    @Override
    public int disabledToggle() {
        return 0xFF3A3A44;
    }

    @Override
    public int outline() {
        return (0xCC << 24) | (accent() & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }

    @Override
    public int outlineHover() {
        return accentHover();
    }

    @Override
    public int scrollbar() {
        return (0x99 << 24) | (accent() & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ shape

    @Override
    public int cornerRadius() {
        return 0;
    }

    @Override
    public int controlRadius() {
        return 0;
    }

    @Override
    public int outlineWidth() {
        return 1;
    }

    @Override
    public int panelPadding() {
        return 16;
    }

    @Override
    public int itemHeight() {
        var g = gui();
        return g != null ? g.getClassicRowHeight() : 12;
    }

    @Override
    public int cardHeight() {
        return 64;
    }

    @Override
    public int headerHeight() {
        return 48;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ effects

    @Override
    public double panelOpacity() {
        var g = gui();
        return g != null ? g.getClassicPanelOpacity() : 0.85;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean useGradientHeader() {
        var g = gui();
        return g == null || g.isClassicGradientHeader();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean useTextShadow() {
        var g = gui();
        return g == null || g.isClassicTextShadow();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean useGlow() {
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean useItemSeparator() {
        var g = gui();
        return g == null || g.isClassicSeparators();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean accentEnabledNames() {
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public double animationSpeedMultiplier() {
        // Snappy, almost instant — the old clients had no easing to speak of.
        return 0.15;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ font

    @Override
    public float fontScale() {
        return 1.0f;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ layout

    /** Classic ships its own menu: draggable per-category tab panels, not the modern panel. */
    @Override
    public net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen createScreen() {
        return new dev.dd.lenrete.gui.ClassicGuiScreen();
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/gui/theme/ModernTheme.java`:

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/gui/theme/Theme.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.gui.theme;

/**
 * A complete visual skin for the ClickGUI (and HUD accents). Themes change colors, shapes,
 * fonts and animation feel — never layout or widget structure. Every value the GUI renders
 * with comes from here; rendering code must not contain color or radius literals.
 *
 * <p>Adding a new theme means implementing this interface and registering the instance in
 * {@link ThemeManager#init()} (or via {@link ThemeManager#register(Theme)}) — nothing else.
 */
public interface Theme {
    /** Unique display name, used in the Theme setting and the .theme command. */
    String name();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ colors (ARGB)

    /** Full-screen dim behind the GUI. */
    int background();

    /** Panel/card fill. Alpha is further scaled by {@link #panelOpacity()}. */
    int panelBackground();

    /** Header fill (top of the gradient when {@link #useGradientHeader()} is on). */
    int header();

    /** Bottom of the header gradient; ignored unless {@link #useGradientHeader()}. */
    int headerGradientEnd();

    int accent();

    /** Accent variant used for hovered/active accents. */
    int accentHover();

    int textPrimary();

    int textSecondary();

    /** Text of unavailable/disabled entries. */
    int textDisabled();

    /** Toggle track when the module is enabled. */
    int enabledToggle();

    /** Toggle track when the module is disabled. */
    int disabledToggle();

    /** Panel and control outlines. */
    int outline();

    /** Outline color when hovered. */
    int outlineHover();

    /** Scrollbar color (reserved; the current layout scrolls without a visible bar). */
    int scrollbar();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ shape (px)

    /** Corner radius of panels and cards. 0 = hard, blocky edges. */
    int cornerRadius();

    /** Corner radius of small controls (badges, buttons, input rows). */
    int controlRadius();

    int outlineWidth();

    int panelPadding();

    /** Height of one rail/list row. */
    int itemHeight();

    /** Height of one module card. */
    int cardHeight();

    /** Height of the GUI top bar. */
    int headerHeight();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ effects

    /** 0–1 multiplier applied to the panel background alpha. */
    double panelOpacity();

    boolean useGradientHeader();

    boolean useTextShadow();

    /** Reserved hook for glow effects around accents. */
    boolean useGlow();

    /** Thin 1px separator line between module cards. */
    boolean useItemSeparator();

    /** Render enabled module names in the accent color and disabled ones in gray. */
    boolean accentEnabledNames();

    /**
     * Multiplies every animation duration. 1.0 = normal feel; small values (e.g. 0.15)
     * make transitions near-instant and snappy.
     */
    double animationSpeedMultiplier();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ font

    /** Scale applied to the Minecraft font renderer. 1.0 = vanilla size. */
    float fontScale();

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ layout

    /**
     * The screen this theme opens. Themes that are pure skins leave this alone and get the
     * standard panel layout; a theme may override it to ship an entirely different menu
     * (e.g. the Classic draggable category tabs).
     */
    default net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen createScreen() {
        return new dev.dd.lenrete.gui.ClickGuiScreen();
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/gui/theme/ThemeManager.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.gui.theme;

import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Registry of available {@link Theme}s and the currently active one. Themes register at
 * client init; switching takes effect immediately because the GUI re-reads the active theme
 * every frame.
 */
public final class ThemeManager {
    private static final Map<String, Theme> THEMES = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private static Theme active;

    private ThemeManager() {
    }

    /** Registers the built-in themes. Called once from client init. */
    public static void init() {
        register(new ModernTheme());
        register(new ClassicTheme());
    }

    public static void register(Theme theme) {
        THEMES.put(theme.name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT), theme);
        if (active == null) {
            active = theme;
        }
        Lenrete.LOGGER.info("Registered GUI theme {}", theme.name());
    }

    /** Never null: falls back to the first registered theme (Modern). */
    public static Theme getActive() {
        if (active == null && !THEMES.isEmpty()) {
            active = THEMES.values().iterator().next();
        }
        return active;
    }

    /**
     * Activates the theme with the given name (case-insensitive).
     *
     * @return true if the theme exists and is now active; false leaves the current theme.
     */
    public static boolean setActive(String name) {
        if (name == null) {
            return false;
        }
        Theme theme = THEMES.get(name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
        if (theme == null) {
            return false;
        }
        active = theme;
        return true;
    }

    /** True when the named theme is the active one — used to show/hide theme-specific settings. */
    public static boolean isActive(String name) {
        Theme theme = getActive();
        return theme != null && theme.name().equalsIgnoreCase(name);
    }

    public static Theme byName(String name) {
        return name == null ? null : THEMES.get(name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
    }

    /** Display names of all registered themes, in registration order. */
    public static List<String> names() {
        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Theme theme : THEMES.values()) {
            names.add(theme.name());
        }
        return names;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/hud/HudRenderer.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.hud;

import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.hud.HudModule;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.hud.HudModule.Corner;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.render.FakeCoordsModule;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.event.lifecycle.v1.ClientTickEvents;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.hud.HudElementRegistry;
import net.minecraft.client.DeltaTracker;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderPipelines;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.resources.Identifier;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import org.joml.Matrix3x2fStack;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;

public final class HudRenderer {
    private static final HudRenderer INSTANCE = new HudRenderer();
    private static final int MARGIN = 4;
    private static final int LINE_HEIGHT = 10;
    private static final Identifier ICON_TEXTURE = Identifier.fromNamespaceAndPath(Lenrete.MOD_ID, "textures/gui/perona.png");
    private static final int ICON_SIZE = 12;

    private static final long SESSION_START_MS = System.currentTimeMillis();

    private final List<Module> enabledModules = new ArrayList<>();

    private HudRenderer() {
    }

    public static void init() {
        ClientTickEvents.END_CLIENT_TICK.register(INSTANCE::onTick);
        HudElementRegistry.addLast(Identifier.fromNamespaceAndPath(Lenrete.MOD_ID, "hud"), INSTANCE::extractRenderState);
    }

    private void onTick(Minecraft client) {
        enabledModules.clear();
        for (Module module : ModuleManager.getInstance().getModules()) {
            if (module.isEnabled()) {
                enabledModules.add(module);
            }
        }
        enabledModules.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(module -> module.getName().length()));
    }

    private void extractRenderState(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, DeltaTracker deltaTracker) {
        Minecraft client = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (client.getDebugOverlay().showDebugScreen()) {
            return;
        }

        HudModule hud = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(HudModule.class);
        if (hud == null || !hud.isEnabled()) {
            return;
        }

        int textColor = hud.getTextColor();
        double scale = hud.getScale();

        if (hud.isShowWatermark()) {
            renderWatermark(g, client, hud, textColor, scale);
        }
        if (hud.isShowModuleList()) {
            renderModuleList(g, client, hud, textColor, scale);
        }
        renderInfoLines(g, client, hud, textColor, scale);
    }

    /**
     * Wall-clock, frame-independent rainbow colour. {@code nanoTime} keeps the animation
     * running continuously regardless of tick rate or whether any GUI is open.
     */
    private static int rainbow(int index, double speed, double offset, float saturation, float brightness) {
        double seconds = System.nanoTime() / 1_000_000_000.0;
        float hue = (float) (((seconds * speed) + index * offset) % 1.0);
        if (hue < 0) {
            hue += 1.0f;
        }
        return 0xFF000000 | (java.awt.Color.HSBtoRGB(hue, saturation, brightness) & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }

    private void renderWatermark(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Minecraft client, HudModule hud, int textColor, double scale) {
        Corner corner = hud.getWatermarkPosition();
        boolean icon = hud.isWatermarkIcon();

        String full = buildWatermarkText(client, hud);
        double t = System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000.0;

        // Text animation resolves which characters are visible and any whole-label alpha/scale effect.
        HudModule.TextAnim tAnim = hud.getWatermarkTextAnim();
        double speed = hud.getWatermarkTextAnimSpeed();
        String visible = applyTextAnim(tAnim, full, t, speed, hud.getWatermarkTextAnimDelay(),
                hud.isWatermarkTextAnimLoop(), hud.getWatermarkScrollDir());
        double labelAlpha = textAnimAlpha(tAnim, t, speed);
        double pulseScale = tAnim == HudModule.TextAnim.PULSE ? 1.0 + 0.12 * Math.sin(t * speed * 3.0) : 1.0;
        if (visible.isEmpty()) {
            visible = " ";
        }

        int iconWidth = icon ? ICON_SIZE + 4 : 0;
        int textWidth = client.font.width(visible);
        int unitWidth = iconWidth + textWidth;
        int screenW = g.guiWidth();
        int screenH = g.guiHeight();

        boolean right = corner == Corner.TOP_RIGHT || corner == Corner.BOTTOM_RIGHT;
        boolean bottom = corner == Corner.BOTTOM_LEFT || corner == Corner.BOTTOM_RIGHT;
        int x = right ? screenW - MARGIN - unitWidth : MARGIN;
        int y = bottom ? screenH - MARGIN - ICON_SIZE : MARGIN;

        Matrix3x2fStack pose = g.pose();
        pose.pushMatrix();
        applyCornerScale(pose, corner, screenW, screenH, scale);
        if (pulseScale != 1.0) {
            // Pulse about the label centre so it grows/shrinks in place.
            float px = x + unitWidth / 2f;
            float py = y + ICON_SIZE / 2f;
            pose.translate(px, py);
            pose.scale((float) pulseScale);
            pose.translate(-px, -py);
        }

        if (icon) {
            g.blit(RenderPipelines.GUI_TEXTURED, watermarkTexture(hud), x, y, 0f, 0f, ICON_SIZE, ICON_SIZE, ICON_SIZE, ICON_SIZE);
        }

        int textY = y + (ICON_SIZE - client.font.lineHeight) / 2;
        int cursorX = x + iconWidth;
        int count = visible.length();
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            String ch = String.valueOf(visible.charAt(i));
            int color = watermarkColor(hud, textColor, i, count, t);
            color = applyAlphaFactor(color, labelAlpha);
            g.text(client.font, ch, cursorX, textY, color);
            cursorX += client.font.width(ch);
        }
        pose.popMatrix();
    }

    /** The watermark icon the user picked; animated images resolve to their current frame. */
    private static Identifier watermarkTexture(HudModule hud) {
        Identifier texture = hud.getWatermarkImage().texture(System.currentTimeMillis());
        return texture != null ? texture : ICON_TEXTURE;
    }

    /** Builds the watermark string from the user's format tokens ({text} {name} {version} {fps} {ping}). */
    private static String buildWatermarkText(Minecraft client, HudModule hud) {
        String out = hud.getWatermarkFormat();
        if (out == null || out.isEmpty()) {
            out = "{name} {version}";
        }
        out = out.replace("{text}", hud.getWatermarkText());
        out = out.replace("{name}", "Lenrete");
        out = out.replace("{version}", modVersion());
        out = out.replace("{fps}", String.valueOf(client.getFps()));
        out = out.replace("{ping}", String.valueOf(watermarkPing(client)));
        return out;
    }

    private static int watermarkPing(Minecraft client) {
        if (client.player == null || client.getConnection() == null) {
            return 0;
        }
        var info = client.getConnection().getPlayerInfo(client.player.getUUID());
        return info != null ? info.getLatency() : 0;
    }

    private static String modVersion() {
        return net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader.getInstance()
                .getModContainer(Lenrete.MOD_ID)
                .map(c -> c.getMetadata().getVersion().getFriendlyString())
                .orElse("");
    }

    /** Returns the visible portion/arrangement of the text for the current text animation. */
    private static String applyTextAnim(HudModule.TextAnim anim, String full, double t, double speed, double delay,
                                        boolean loop, HudModule.AnimDirection scrollDir) {
        switch (anim) {
            case TYPEWRITER -> {
                int len = full.length();
                if (len == 0) {
                    return full;
                }
                double charInterval = 0.12 / Math.max(0.1, speed);
                double revealTime = len * charInterval;
                double cycle = revealTime + delay;
                double phase = loop ? (t % cycle) : Math.min(t, revealTime);
                int shown = (int) Math.min(len, Math.floor(phase / charInterval) + 1);
                if (shown < 0) {
                    shown = 0;
                }
                return full.substring(0, Math.min(len, shown));
            }
            case SCROLL -> {
                int len = full.length();
                if (len <= 1) {
                    return full;
                }
                // Character marquee: rotate the string so it appears to slide continuously.
                double stepInterval = 0.25 / Math.max(0.1, speed);
                int steps = (int) Math.floor(t / stepInterval) % len;
                if (scrollDir == HudModule.AnimDirection.RIGHT) {
                    steps = (len - steps) % len;
                }
                return full.substring(steps) + full.substring(0, steps);
            }
            default -> {
                return full;
            }
        }
    }

    /** Whole-label alpha factor for fade/blink text animations (1.0 for the rest). */
    private static double textAnimAlpha(HudModule.TextAnim anim, double t, double speed) {
        return switch (anim) {
            case FADE -> 0.35 + 0.65 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * speed * 2.0));
            case BLINK -> ((t * speed) % 1.0) < 0.5 ? 1.0 : 0.15;
            default -> 1.0;
        };
    }

    /** Resolves a single character's colour for the watermark's colour animation. */
    private static int watermarkColor(HudModule hud, int textColor, int index, int count, double t) {
        return animatedColor(hud.getWatermarkColorAnim(), hud.getWatermarkColorSpeed(),
                hud.getWatermarkColorSaturation(), hud.getWatermarkColorBrightness(),
                hud.getWatermarkGradient1(), hud.getWatermarkGradient2(), hud.getWatermarkColorDir(),
                textColor, index, count, t);
    }

    /**
     * Per-character colour for any animated label. Shared by the watermark and FakeCoords so
     * both offer the same colour vocabulary.
     */
    private static int animatedColor(HudModule.ColorAnim anim, double speed, float saturation, float brightness,
                                     int gradient1, int gradient2, HudModule.AnimDirection direction,
                                     int textColor, int index, int count, double t) {
        switch (anim) {
            case RAINBOW -> {
                return rainbow(index, speed, 0.06, saturation, brightness);
            }
            case GRADIENT -> {
                double frac = count <= 1 ? 0.0 : (double) index / (count - 1);
                double sweep = (t * speed) % 1.0;
                if (direction == HudModule.AnimDirection.RIGHT) {
                    frac = 1.0 - frac;
                }
                double mix = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin((frac + sweep) * Math.PI * 2.0);
                return lerpColor(gradient1, gradient2, mix);
            }
            case FADE -> {
                double mix = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * speed * 2.0);
                return lerpColor(gradient1, gradient2, mix);
            }
            case PULSE -> {
                double wave = 0.4 + 0.6 * (0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t * speed * 3.0));
                return scaleBrightness(textColor, wave);
            }
            default -> {
                return textColor;
            }
        }
    }

    private static int lerpColor(int a, int b, double factor) {
        factor = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, factor));
        int aa = a >>> 24, ar = (a >> 16) & 0xFF, ag = (a >> 8) & 0xFF, ab = a & 0xFF;
        int ba = b >>> 24, br = (b >> 16) & 0xFF, bg = (b >> 8) & 0xFF, bb = b & 0xFF;
        int ra = (int) (aa + (ba - aa) * factor);
        int rr = (int) (ar + (br - ar) * factor);
        int rg = (int) (ag + (bg - ag) * factor);
        int rb = (int) (ab + (bb - ab) * factor);
        return (ra << 24) | (rr << 16) | (rg << 8) | rb;
    }

    private static int applyAlphaFactor(int color, double factor) {
        factor = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, factor));
        int a = (int) ((color >>> 24) * factor);
        return (a << 24) | (color & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }

    private static int scaleBrightness(int color, double factor) {
        factor = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, factor));
        int a = color >>> 24;
        int r = (int) Math.min(255, ((color >> 16) & 0xFF) * factor + 40 * (1 - factor));
        int gr = (int) Math.min(255, ((color >> 8) & 0xFF) * factor + 40 * (1 - factor));
        int b = (int) Math.min(255, (color & 0xFF) * factor + 40 * (1 - factor));
        return (a << 24) | (r << 16) | (gr << 8) | b;
    }

    private void renderModuleList(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Minecraft client, HudModule hud, int textColor, double scale) {
        if (enabledModules.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        boolean rainbow = hud.isRainbowModuleList();
        double speed = hud.getRainbowSpeed();
        double offset = hud.getRainbowOffset();
        float saturation = hud.getRainbowSaturation();
        float brightness = hud.getRainbowBrightness();

        Corner corner = hud.getModuleListPosition();
        int screenW = g.guiWidth();
        int screenH = g.guiHeight();
        boolean right = corner == Corner.TOP_RIGHT || corner == Corner.BOTTOM_RIGHT;
        boolean bottom = corner == Corner.BOTTOM_LEFT || corner == Corner.BOTTOM_RIGHT;
        int topOffset = (hud.isShowWatermark() && hud.getWatermarkPosition() == corner) ? ICON_SIZE + 4 : 0;

        Matrix3x2fStack pose = g.pose();
        pose.pushMatrix();
        applyCornerScale(pose, corner, screenW, screenH, scale);

        // Rendered directly from enabledModules (whatever the sort order) with the colour
        // computed inline, so no per-frame list/array allocations.
        int count = enabledModules.size();
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            String name = enabledModules.get(i).getName();
            int color = rainbow ? rainbow(i, speed, offset, saturation, brightness) : textColor;
            int width = client.font.width(name);
            int x = right ? screenW - MARGIN - width : MARGIN;
            int y = bottom
                    ? screenH - MARGIN - (count - i) * LINE_HEIGHT
                    : MARGIN + topOffset + i * LINE_HEIGHT;
            g.text(client.font, name, x, y, color);
        }

        pose.popMatrix();
    }

    private void renderInfoLines(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Minecraft client, HudModule hud, int textColor, double scale) {
        LocalPlayer player = client.player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        List<HudLine> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        if (hud.isShowFps()) {
            lines.add(new HudLine("FPS: " + client.getFps(), textColor, false));
        }
        if (hud.isShowCoordinates()) {
            lines.add(coordinateLine(player, textColor));
        }
        if (hud.isShowDirection()) {
            lines.add(new HudLine("Facing: " + capitalize(Direction.fromYRot(player.getYRot()).getName()), textColor, false));
        }
        if (hud.isShowSpeed()) {
            double dx = player.getX() - player.xOld;
            double dz = player.getZ() - player.zOld;
            lines.add(new HudLine(String.format("Speed: %.1f b/s", Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz) * 20.0), textColor, false));
        }
        if (hud.isShowArmor()) {
            lines.add(new HudLine("Armor: " + player.getArmorValue(), textColor, false));
        }
        if (hud.isShowPing() && client.getConnection() != null) {
            var info = client.getConnection().getPlayerInfo(player.getUUID());
            if (info != null) {
                lines.add(new HudLine("Ping: " + info.getLatency() + "ms", textColor, false));
            }
        }
        if (hud.isShowEffects()) {
            for (MobEffectInstance effect : player.getActiveEffects()) {
                String name = effect.getEffect().value().getDisplayName().getString();
                lines.add(new HudLine(name + " " + (effect.getAmplifier() + 1) + " ("
                        + formatDuration(effect.getDuration()) + ")", textColor, false));
            }
        }
        if (hud.isShowClock()) {
            lines.add(new HudLine("Time: " + java.time.LocalTime.now().withNano(0), textColor, false));
        }
        if (hud.isShowBiome()) {
            lines.add(new HudLine("Biome: " + biomeName(client, player), textColor, false));
        }
        if (hud.isShowSessionTime()) {
            lines.add(new HudLine("Session: "
                    + formatDuration((int) ((System.currentTimeMillis() - SESSION_START_MS) / 50)), textColor, false));
        }

        if (lines.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        renderCornerLines(g, client, hud, hud.getInfoPosition(), scale, lines, 0);
    }

    /**
     * The XYZ readout — real coordinates, or whatever FakeCoords is configured to show instead.
     * In text mode the line carries the module's text animation and is drawn per character.
     */
    private static HudLine coordinateLine(LocalPlayer player, int textColor) {
        FakeCoordsModule fake = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(FakeCoordsModule.class);
        if (fake == null || !fake.isEnabled()) {
            return new HudLine(String.format("XYZ: %.1f / %.1f / %.1f",
                    player.getX(), player.getY(), player.getZ()), textColor, false);
        }

        String value = fake.coordinateLine(player.getX(), player.getY(), player.getZ());
        if (!fake.isTextMode()) {
            return new HudLine("XYZ: " + value, textColor, false);
        }

        double t = System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000.0;
        String visible = applyTextAnim(fake.getTextAnim(), value, t, fake.getAnimSpeed(),
                fake.getAnimDelay(), fake.isAnimLoop(), fake.getScrollDir());
        if (visible.isEmpty()) {
            visible = " ";
        }
        return new HudLine("XYZ: " + visible, textColor, true);
    }

    /** One HUD info line; animated lines are drawn character by character with a live colour. */
    private record HudLine(String text, int color, boolean animated) {
    }

    private static String biomeName(Minecraft client, LocalPlayer player) {
        try {
            return client.level.getBiome(player.blockPosition()).unwrapKey()
                    .map(key -> capitalize(key.identifier().getPath().replace('_', ' ')))
                    .orElse("Unknown");
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            return "Unknown";
        }
    }

    private void renderCornerLines(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Minecraft client, HudModule hud, Corner corner, double scale, List<HudLine> lines, int topOffset) {
        int screenW = g.guiWidth();
        int screenH = g.guiHeight();
        boolean right = corner == Corner.TOP_RIGHT || corner == Corner.BOTTOM_RIGHT;
        boolean bottom = corner == Corner.BOTTOM_LEFT || corner == Corner.BOTTOM_RIGHT;
        boolean shadow = hud.isTextShadow();

        Matrix3x2fStack pose = g.pose();
        pose.pushMatrix();
        applyCornerScale(pose, corner, screenW, screenH, scale);

        int count = lines.size();

        // Optional translucent panel behind the whole block of lines.
        if (hud.isInfoBackground()) {
            int widest = 0;
            for (HudLine line : lines) {
                widest = Math.max(widest, client.font.width(line.text()));
            }
            int boxX = right ? screenW - MARGIN - widest : MARGIN;
            int firstY = bottom ? screenH - MARGIN - count * LINE_HEIGHT : MARGIN + topOffset;
            int bg = (hud.getInfoBackgroundOpacity() & 0xFF) << 24;
            g.fill(boxX - 2, firstY - 1, boxX + widest + 2, firstY + count * LINE_HEIGHT + 1, bg);
        }

        FakeCoordsModule fake = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(FakeCoordsModule.class);
        double t = System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000.0;

        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            HudLine line = lines.get(i);
            String text = line.text();
            int width = client.font.width(text);
            int x = right ? screenW - MARGIN - width : MARGIN;
            int y = bottom
                    ? screenH - MARGIN - (count - i) * LINE_HEIGHT
                    : MARGIN + topOffset + i * LINE_HEIGHT;

            if (!line.animated() || fake == null) {
                g.text(client.font, text, x, y, line.color(), shadow);
                continue;
            }

            // Animated line: colour resolves per character, and fade/blink dim the whole line.
            double alpha = textAnimAlpha(fake.getTextAnim(), t, fake.getAnimSpeed());
            int cursorX = x;
            int length = text.length();
            for (int c = 0; c < length; c++) {
                String ch = String.valueOf(text.charAt(c));
                int color = animatedColor(fake.getColorAnim(), fake.getColorSpeed(),
                        fake.getColorSaturation(), fake.getColorBrightness(),
                        fake.getGradient1(), fake.getGradient2(), fake.getColorDir(),
                        line.color(), c, length, t);
                g.text(client.font, ch, cursorX, y, applyAlphaFactor(color, alpha), shadow);
                cursorX += client.font.width(ch);
            }
        }

        pose.popMatrix();
    }

    private void applyCornerScale(Matrix3x2fStack pose, Corner corner, int screenW, int screenH, double scale) {
        float anchorX = (corner == Corner.TOP_RIGHT || corner == Corner.BOTTOM_RIGHT) ? screenW : 0;
        float anchorY = (corner == Corner.BOTTOM_LEFT || corner == Corner.BOTTOM_RIGHT) ? screenH : 0;
        pose.translate(anchorX, anchorY);
        pose.scale((float) scale);
        pose.translate(-anchorX, -anchorY);
    }

    private static String capitalize(String value) {
        return value.isEmpty() ? value : Character.toUpperCase(value.charAt(0)) + value.substring(1);
    }

    private static String formatDuration(int ticks) {
        if (ticks < 0) {
            return "∞";
        }
        int seconds = ticks / 20;
        return String.format("%d:%02d", seconds / 60, seconds % 60);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/mixin/CameraMixin.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.mixin;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement.FreecamModule;
import net.minecraft.client.Camera;
import net.minecraft.client.DeltaTracker;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Shadow;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

/**
 * Freecam overrides only the camera position here, per frame, leaving the rotation to come
 * from the player entity as vanilla does — so mouse look stays as responsive/smooth as normal
 * play regardless of frame rate. The player's real position is never touched.
 */
@Mixin(Camera.class)
public abstract class CameraMixin {
    @Shadow
    protected abstract void setPosition(Vec3 vec3);

    @Inject(method = "update", at = @At("TAIL"), require = 0)
    private void lenrete$freecamPosition(DeltaTracker deltaTracker, CallbackInfo ci) {
        Vec3 position = FreecamModule.getCameraPosition(deltaTracker.getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(true));
        if (position != null) {
            setPosition(position);
        }
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.mixin;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc.BlinkerModule;

import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

@Mixin(ClientCommonPacketListenerImpl.class)
public class ClientCommonPacketListenerMixin {
    @Inject(method = "send(Lnet/minecraft/network/protocol/Packet;)V", at = @At("HEAD"), cancellable = true, require = 0)
    private void lenrete$blinker(Packet<?> packet, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (BlinkerModule.shouldBlockMovement(packet)) {
            ci.cancel();
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/mixin/ClientInputAccessor.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.mixin;

import net.minecraft.client.player.ClientInput;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec2;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.gen.Accessor;

/**
 * moveVector is declared on ClientInput (the superclass of KeyboardInput), so a @Shadow on the
 * KeyboardInput mixin cannot resolve it. This accessor exposes a setter on the class that actually
 * owns the field, which the KeyboardInput mixin uses to overwrite the movement impulse.
 */
@Mixin(ClientInput.class)
public interface ClientInputAccessor {
    @Accessor("moveVector")
    void lenrete$setMoveVector(Vec2 moveVector);
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/mixin/FoodDataMixin.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.mixin;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.player.AntiHungerModule;
import net.minecraft.world.food.FoodData;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.ModifyVariable;

/**
 * AntiHunger reduces exhaustion at the source rather than forcing hunger/saturation values,
 * so eating and normal gameplay stay intact. Client-side only — a server tracks its own
 * exhaustion — but it removes the unnecessary local exhaustion that drains the visible bar.
 */
@Mixin(FoodData.class)
public class FoodDataMixin {
    @ModifyVariable(method = "addExhaustion", at = @At("HEAD"), argsOnly = true, require = 0)
    private float lenrete$scaleExhaustion(float exhaustion) {
        return exhaustion * AntiHungerModule.exhaustionFactor();
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/mixin/KeyboardInputMixin.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.mixin;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement.AutoWalkModule;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement.InventoryMoveModule;
import net.minecraft.client.player.ClientInput;
import net.minecraft.client.player.KeyboardInput;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Input;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec2;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

/**
 * KeyboardInput.tick() computes keyPresses from the key mappings and then derives moveVector
 * (the forward/strafe impulse the player actually moves with). Overriding only keyPresses is
 * not enough — moveVector must be recomputed too, or WASD does nothing. This is the correct,
 * vanilla input path (no faked velocity) for InventoryMove and AutoWalk.
 */
@Mixin(KeyboardInput.class)
public class KeyboardInputMixin {
    @Inject(method = "tick", at = @At("TAIL"), require = 0)
    private void lenrete$movementOverride(CallbackInfo ci) {
        ClientInput self = (ClientInput) (Object) this;

        Input override = InventoryMoveModule.override(self.keyPresses);
        if (override == null) {
            override = AutoWalkModule.modify(self.keyPresses);
        }
        if (override == null) {
            return;
        }

        self.keyPresses = override;
        float forward = impulse(override.forward(), override.backward());
        float strafe = impulse(override.left(), override.right());
        ((ClientInputAccessor) self).lenrete$setMoveVector(new Vec2(strafe, forward).normalized());
    }

    private static float impulse(boolean positive, boolean negative) {
        if (positive == negative) {
            return 0.0f;
        }
        return positive ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/mixin/ServerListMixin.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.mixin;

import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ServerData;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ServerList;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Shadow;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Pins a default entry to the very top of the multiplayer server list every time it loads.
 * The entry is not persisted to servers.dat — it is re-inserted in memory on each load, so it
 * always shows first without duplicating if the user also saves it themselves.
 */
@Mixin(ServerList.class)
public abstract class ServerListMixin {
    @Shadow
    @org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Final
    private List<ServerData> serverList;

    private static final String LENRETE$IP = "eternel.eu";
    private static final String LENRETE$NAME = "Anarchy Server";

    @Inject(method = "load", at = @At("TAIL"), require = 0)
    private void lenrete$pinDefaultServer(CallbackInfo ci) {
        // Drop any existing copy (saved or previously pinned) so it never appears twice.
        serverList.removeIf(data -> data != null && LENRETE$IP.equalsIgnoreCase(data.ip));
        ServerData pinned = new ServerData(LENRETE$NAME, LENRETE$IP, ServerData.Type.OTHER);
        serverList.add(0, pinned);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/Module.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module;

import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

public abstract class Module {
    private final String name;
    private final String description;
    private final Category category;
    private final boolean unavailable;
    private final List<Setting<?>> settings = new ArrayList<>();
    private boolean enabled;
    private boolean favorite;
    private int favoriteOrder;
    private int key;
    private boolean disabledByError;
    private boolean errorLogged;
    private boolean settingsDiscovered;

    protected Module() {
        ModuleMetadata metadata = getClass().getAnnotation(ModuleMetadata.class);
        if (metadata == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(getClass().getName() + " is missing @ModuleMetadata");
        }

        this.name = metadata.name();
        this.description = metadata.description();
        this.category = metadata.category();
        this.key = metadata.key();
        this.unavailable = metadata.unavailable();
        // Settings discovery is deferred (see ensureSettingsDiscovered): subclass field
        // initializers for Setting<?> fields haven't run yet at this point in construction.
    }

    protected Minecraft getMinecraft() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance();
    }

    public String getName() {
        return name;
    }

    public String getDescription() {
        return description;
    }

    public Category getCategory() {
        return category;
    }

    public boolean isEnabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    public boolean isUnavailable() {
        return unavailable;
    }

    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        // Temporarily-disabled modules stay visible/configurable but can never turn on.
        if (unavailable && enabled) {
            return;
        }
        if (this.enabled == enabled) {
            return;
        }

        this.enabled = enabled;
        if (enabled) {
            disabledByError = false;
            errorLogged = false;
            onEnable();
        } else {
            onDisable();
        }
    }

    public void toggle() {
        setEnabled(!enabled);
    }

    public int getKey() {
        return key;
    }

    public void setKey(int key) {
        this.key = key;
    }

    public boolean isFavorite() {
        return favorite;
    }

    public void setFavorite(boolean favorite) {
        this.favorite = favorite;
    }

    public int getFavoriteOrder() {
        return favoriteOrder;
    }

    public void setFavoriteOrder(int favoriteOrder) {
        this.favoriteOrder = favoriteOrder;
    }

    public boolean isDisabledByError() {
        return disabledByError;
    }

    public void disableAfterError() {
        disabledByError = true;
        enabled = false;
        try {
            onDisable();
        } catch (RuntimeException ignored) {
            // The manager logs the original failure that caused the disable.
        }
    }

    public boolean markErrorLogged() {
        if (errorLogged) {
            return false;
        }
        errorLogged = true;
        return true;
    }

    public List<Setting<?>> getSettings() {
        ensureSettingsDiscovered();
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(settings);
    }

    public Setting<?> getSetting(String name) {
        ensureSettingsDiscovered();
        return settings.stream()
                .filter(setting -> setting.getName().equals(name))
                .findFirst()
                .orElse(null);
    }

    private void ensureSettingsDiscovered() {
        if (settingsDiscovered) {
            return;
        }
        settingsDiscovered = true;
        discoverSettings();
    }

    private void discoverSettings() {
        Class<?> type = getClass();
        while (type != null && Module.class.isAssignableFrom(type)) {
            for (Field field : type.getDeclaredFields()) {
                if (!Setting.class.isAssignableFrom(field.getType())) {
                    continue;
                }

                try {
                    field.setAccessible(true);
                    Setting<?> setting = (Setting<?>) field.get(this);
                    if (setting != null) {
                        settings.add(setting);
                    }
                } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
                    throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot read setting " + field.getName(), e);
                }
            }
            type = type.getSuperclass();
        }
    }

    protected void onEnable() {
    }

    protected void onDisable() {
    }

    public void onTick() {
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/ModuleManager.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import dev.dd.lenrete.config.ConfigManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.event.EventBus;
import dev.dd.lenrete.event.events.TickEvent;
import dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.event.lifecycle.v1.ClientTickEvents;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.JarURLConnection;
import java.net.URISyntaxException;
import java.net.URL;
import java.security.CodeSource;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.Enumeration;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.jar.JarEntry;
import java.util.jar.JarFile;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public class ModuleManager {
    private static final String MODULE_PACKAGE = "dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl";
    private static final String MODULE_PATH = "dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl";
    private static ModuleManager instance;

    private final List<Module> modules = new ArrayList<>();
    private final Map<Integer, Boolean> keyStateMap = new HashMap<>();
    private final EventBus eventBus = new EventBus();

    public ModuleManager() {
        instance = this;
        discoverModules();
        registerEvents();
    }

    public static ModuleManager getInstance() {
        if (instance == null) {
            instance = new ModuleManager();
        }
        return instance;
    }

    public List<Module> getModules() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(modules);
    }

    public List<Module> getModulesByCategory(Category category) {
        return modules.stream()
                .filter(module -> module.getCategory() == category)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public <T extends Module> T getModule(Class<T> clazz) {
        return (T) modules.stream()
                .filter(module -> module.getClass().equals(clazz))
                .findFirst()
                .orElse(null);
    }

    public void toggleModule(Module module) {
        if (module.isUnavailable()) {
            ToastManager.show(module.getName() + " is temporarily unavailable");
            return;
        }
        runGuarded(module, () -> {
            module.toggle();
            ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
            ToastManager.show(module.getName() + (module.isEnabled() ? " enabled" : " disabled"));
        });
    }

    private void registerEvents() {
        ClientTickEvents.END_CLIENT_TICK.register(this::onTick);
    }

    private void onTick(Minecraft client) {
        if (client.player == null || client.level == null) {
            return;
        }

        // Do not process module keybinds while a screen (GUI, chat, inventory) is open,
        // so typing or binding a key never toggles modules.
        boolean screenOpen = client.gui.screen() != null;
        for (Module module : modules) {
            if (module.getKey() <= 0) {
                continue;
            }

            boolean isPressed = !screenOpen && InputConstants.isKeyDown(client.getWindow(), module.getKey());
            boolean wasPressed = keyStateMap.getOrDefault(module.getKey(), false);
            if (isPressed && !wasPressed) {
                toggleModule(module);
            }
            keyStateMap.put(module.getKey(), isPressed);
        }

        for (Module module : modules) {
            if (module.isEnabled()) {
                runGuarded(module, module::onTick);
            }
        }

        eventBus.post(new TickEvent(client));
        ConfigManager.flushPendingSave();
    }

    private void register(Module module) {
        modules.add(module);
        eventBus.register(module);
        Lenrete.LOGGER.info("Registered module {}", module.getName());
    }

    private void runGuarded(Module module, Runnable action) {
        try {
            action.run();
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            module.disableAfterError();
            if (module.markErrorLogged()) {
                Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Module {} failed and was disabled", module.getName(), e);
                ToastManager.show(module.getName() + " error: disabled");
            }
        }
    }

    private void discoverModules() {
        List<Class<? extends Module>> discovered = findModuleClasses();
        discovered.sort(Comparator.comparing(Class::getName));
        for (Class<? extends Module> moduleClass : discovered) {
            try {
                register(moduleClass.getDeclaredConstructor().newInstance());
            } catch (ReflectiveOperationException | RuntimeException e) {
                Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Failed to create module {}", moduleClass.getName(), e);
            }
        }
    }

    private List<Class<? extends Module>> findModuleClasses() {
        List<Class<? extends Module>> result = new ArrayList<>();
        ClassLoader loader = Thread.currentThread().getContextClassLoader();

        try {
            Enumeration<URL> resources = loader.getResources(MODULE_PATH);
            while (resources.hasMoreElements()) {
                URL url = resources.nextElement();
                if ("file".equals(url.getProtocol())) {
                    scanDirectory(new File(url.toURI()), MODULE_PACKAGE, result);
                } else if ("jar".equals(url.getProtocol())) {
                    JarURLConnection connection = (JarURLConnection) url.openConnection();
                    scanJar(connection.getJarFile(), result);
                }
            }

            if (result.isEmpty()) {
                scanOwnJar(result);
            }
        } catch (IOException | URISyntaxException e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.error("Module discovery failed", e);
        }

        return result;
    }

    private void scanOwnJar(List<Class<? extends Module>> result) throws IOException, URISyntaxException {
        CodeSource source = ModuleManager.class.getProtectionDomain().getCodeSource();
        if (source == null) {
            return;
        }

        File location = new File(source.getLocation().toURI());
        if (!location.isFile()) {
            return;
        }

        try (JarFile jar = new JarFile(location)) {
            scanJar(jar, result);
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private void addModuleClass(String className, List<Class<? extends Module>> result) {
        try {
            Class<?> type = Class.forName(className);
            if (Module.class.isAssignableFrom(type) && type.isAnnotationPresent(ModuleMetadata.class)) {
                result.add((Class<? extends Module>) type);
            }
        } catch (ClassNotFoundException | NoClassDefFoundError e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.warn("Skipping unavailable module class {}", className);
        }
    }

    private void scanDirectory(File directory, String packageName, List<Class<? extends Module>> result) {
        File[] files = directory.listFiles();
        if (files == null) {
            return;
        }

        for (File file : files) {
            if (file.isDirectory()) {
                scanDirectory(file, packageName + "." + file.getName(), result);
            } else if (file.getName().endsWith(".class") && !file.getName().contains("$")) {
                String simpleName = file.getName().substring(0, file.getName().length() - ".class".length());
                addModuleClass(packageName + "." + simpleName, result);
            }
        }
    }

    private void scanJar(JarFile jar, List<Class<? extends Module>> result) {
        Enumeration<JarEntry> entries = jar.entries();
        while (entries.hasMoreElements()) {
            JarEntry entry = entries.nextElement();
            String name = entry.getName();
            if (!name.startsWith(MODULE_PATH) || !name.endsWith(".class") || name.contains("$")) {
                continue;
            }

            String className = name.substring(0, name.length() - ".class".length()).replace('/', '.');
            addModuleClass(className, result);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/ModuleMetadata.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module;

import java.lang.annotation.ElementType;
import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target(ElementType.TYPE)
public @interface ModuleMetadata {
    String name();
    String description();
    Category category();
    int key() default -1;

    /** Marks a module as temporarily disabled: it stays visible/configurable but cannot be enabled. */
    boolean unavailable() default false;
}
```
