`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/combat/AimAssistModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.combat;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.Animal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.Enemy;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "AimAssist",
        description = "Smoothly pulls aim toward the nearest valid target",
        category = Category.COMBAT
)
public class AimAssistModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> fov = new Setting<>("FOV", Double.class, 60.0, 5.0, 180.0, 5.0, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> range = new Setting<>("Range", Double.class, 4.5, 3.0, 6.0, 0.1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> horizontalSpeed = new Setting<>("Horizontal Speed", Double.class, 0.3, 0.0, 1.0, 0.05, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> verticalSpeed = new Setting<>("Vertical Speed", Double.class, 0.2, 0.0, 1.0, 0.05, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> targetPlayers = new Setting<>("Players", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> targetHostile = new Setting<>("Hostile", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> targetPassive = new Setting<>("Passive", Boolean.class, false);

    private long lastNanos = -1;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        lastNanos = -1;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null || getMinecraft().level == null) {
            return;
        }

        long now = System.nanoTime();
        double deltaSeconds = lastNanos < 0 ? 1.0 / 20.0 : Math.min((now - lastNanos) / 1_000_000_000.0, 0.2);
        lastNanos = now;

        LivingEntity target = findTarget(player);
        if (target == null) {
            return;
        }

        Vec3 eye = player.getEyePosition();
        Vec3 aimPoint = target.getEyePosition();
        double dx = aimPoint.x - eye.x;
        double dy = aimPoint.y - eye.y;
        double dz = aimPoint.z - eye.z;

        double horizontalDistance = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
        float targetYaw = (float) (Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(dz, dx)) - 90.0);
        float targetPitch = (float) (-Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(dy, horizontalDistance)));

        float yawStep = (float) (wrapDegrees(targetYaw - player.getYRot()) * horizontalSpeed.get() * (deltaSeconds * 20.0));
        float pitchStep = (float) ((targetPitch - player.getXRot()) * verticalSpeed.get() * (deltaSeconds * 20.0));

        player.setYRot(player.getYRot() + yawStep);
        player.setXRot(Math.max(-90, Math.min(90, player.getXRot() + pitchStep)));
    }

    private LivingEntity findTarget(LocalPlayer player) {
        LivingEntity best = null;
        double bestAngle = fov.get();
        double maxRange = range.get();

        for (Entity entity : getMinecraft().level.entitiesForRendering()) {
            if (!(entity instanceof LivingEntity living) || entity == player) {
                continue;
            }
            if (!living.isAlive() || living.isDeadOrDying() || !isValidTarget(living)) {
                continue;
            }
            if (player.distanceTo(living) > maxRange) {
                continue;
            }

            double angle = angleToEntity(player, living);
            if (angle < bestAngle) {
                bestAngle = angle;
                best = living;
            }
        }
        return best;
    }

    private double angleToEntity(LocalPlayer player, LivingEntity target) {
        Vec3 eye = player.getEyePosition();
        Vec3 aimPoint = target.getEyePosition();
        double dx = aimPoint.x - eye.x;
        double dy = aimPoint.y - eye.y;
        double dz = aimPoint.z - eye.z;

        double horizontalDistance = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
        double targetYaw = Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(dz, dx)) - 90.0;
        double targetPitch = -Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(dy, horizontalDistance));

        double yawDiff = wrapDegrees(targetYaw - player.getYRot());
        double pitchDiff = targetPitch - player.getXRot();
        return Math.sqrt(yawDiff * yawDiff + pitchDiff * pitchDiff);
    }

    private boolean isValidTarget(LivingEntity living) {
        if (living instanceof Player) {
            return Boolean.TRUE.equals(targetPlayers.get());
        }
        if (living instanceof Enemy) {
            return Boolean.TRUE.equals(targetHostile.get());
        }
        if (living instanceof Animal) {
            return Boolean.TRUE.equals(targetPassive.get());
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static double wrapDegrees(double degrees) {
        double wrapped = degrees % 360.0;
        if (wrapped >= 180.0) {
            wrapped -= 360.0;
        }
        if (wrapped < -180.0) {
            wrapped += 360.0;
        }
        return wrapped;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/combat/AutoTotemModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.combat;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.ContainerInput;
import net.minecraft.world.inventory.Slot;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "AutoTotem",
        description = "Keeps a Totem of Undying in your offhand and replaces it the moment one pops",
        category = Category.COMBAT
)
public class AutoTotemModule extends Module {
    /** Offhand slot index inside the player's own inventory menu. */
    private static final int OFFHAND_SLOT = 45;

    /** Crafting-result slot: items can never be swapped out of it. */
    private static final int CRAFT_RESULT_SLOT = 0;

    private final Setting<Double> healthThreshold = new Setting<>("Health Threshold", Double.class, 20.0, 1.0, 20.0, 0.5, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Integer> delay = new Setting<>("Delay", Integer.class, 1, 0, 20, 1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> notify = new Setting<>("Notify On Pop", Boolean.class, true);

    private int cooldown;
    private boolean hadTotem;

    @Override
    protected void onEnable() {
        cooldown = 0;
        hadTotem = false;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        if (player == null || minecraft.gameMode == null) {
            return;
        }

        // Only the player's own inventory menu may be touched: while a chest or crafting table
        // is open the slot indices belong to that container, so a swap would move its contents.
        if (player.containerMenu != player.inventoryMenu) {
            return;
        }

        ItemStack offhand = player.getOffhandItem();
        boolean holdingTotem = offhand.is(Items.TOTEM_OF_UNDYING);

        // A totem that was there last tick and is gone now was consumed.
        if (hadTotem && !holdingTotem && notify.get()) {
            dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager.show("Totem popped");
        }
        hadTotem = holdingTotem;

        if (cooldown > 0) {
            cooldown--;
            return;
        }
        if (holdingTotem || player.getHealth() > healthThreshold.get()) {
            return;
        }

        int slot = findTotemSlot(player);
        if (slot < 0) {
            return;
        }

        // SWAP moves the clicked slot straight into the offhand in one action, so no totem is
        // ever left floating on the cursor if the tick is interrupted.
        minecraft.gameMode.handleContainerInput(player.inventoryMenu.containerId, slot,
                Inventory.SLOT_OFFHAND, ContainerInput.SWAP, player);
        cooldown = delay.get();
    }

    /** Menu-slot index of a totem anywhere in the inventory, or -1 if there is none. */
    private int findTotemSlot(LocalPlayer player) {
        for (Slot slot : player.inventoryMenu.slots) {
            // Skip the offhand itself and the crafting output, which cannot be swapped from.
            if (slot.index == OFFHAND_SLOT || slot.index == CRAFT_RESULT_SLOT) {
                continue;
            }
            if (slot.getItem().is(Items.TOTEM_OF_UNDYING)) {
                return slot.index;
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/combat/AutoTpAuraModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.combat;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.EntityHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "TargetTP",
        description = "Teleports you onto an enemy the moment you hit it",
        category = Category.COMBAT
)
public class AutoTpAuraModule extends Module {
    public enum StrafeMode {
        AROUND,
        RANDOM,
        ADVANCED,
        UP
    }

    private final Setting<Double> maxDistance = new Setting<>("Max Distance", Double.class, 30.0, 5.0, 100.0, 5.0, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> keepDistance = new Setting<>("Keep Distance", Double.class, 1.5, 0.0, 4.0, 0.5, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> rotateCamera = new Setting<>("Rotate Camera", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> strafe = new Setting<>("Strafe Around", Boolean.class, false);
    private final Setting<StrafeMode> strafeMode = new Setting<>("Strafe Mode", StrafeMode.class, StrafeMode.AROUND);
    private final Setting<Double> strafeRadius = new Setting<>("Strafe Radius", Double.class, 2.0, 1.0, 5.0, 0.5, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Integer> strafeSteps = new Setting<>("Strafe Directions", Integer.class, 4, 2, 8, 1, () -> true);
    // Advanced random settings.
    private final Setting<Double> minRadius = new Setting<>("Min Radius", Double.class, 1.5, 0.5, 5.0, 0.5, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> verticalVariation = new Setting<>("Vertical Variation", Double.class, 1.0, 0.0, 4.0, 0.5, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> randomStrength = new Setting<>("Randomization Strength", Double.class, 1.0, 0.1, 1.0, 0.1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> avoidRepeat = new Setting<>("Avoid Repeats", Boolean.class, true);

    private boolean attackWasDown;
    private int strafeIndex;
    private double lastAngle = Double.NaN;
    private final java.util.Random random = new java.util.Random();

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        attackWasDown = false;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        if (player == null || minecraft.gui.screen() != null) {
            attackWasDown = false;
            return;
        }

        boolean attackDown = minecraft.options.keyAttack.isDown();
        boolean pressed = attackDown && !attackWasDown;
        attackWasDown = attackDown;
        if (!pressed) {
            return;
        }

        HitResult hit = minecraft.hitResult;
        if (!(hit instanceof EntityHitResult entityHit) || hit.getType() != HitResult.Type.ENTITY) {
            return;
        }

        Entity target = entityHit.getEntity();
        if (target == null || target == player) {
            return;
        }
        if (player.distanceTo(target) > maxDistance.get()) {
            return;
        }

        teleportTo(player, target);
    }

    /** Called by other modules (e.g. KillAura) when they land a hit, so TargetTP follows their attacks too. */
    public void onExternalAttack(Entity target) {
        if (!isEnabled()) {
            return;
        }
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null || target == null || target == player) {
            return;
        }
        if (player.distanceTo(target) > maxDistance.get()) {
            return;
        }
        teleportTo(player, target);
    }

    private void teleportTo(LocalPlayer player, Entity target) {
        Vec3 from = player.position();
        Vec3 to = target.position();
        Vec3 destination = Boolean.TRUE.equals(strafe.get())
                ? strafeDestination(to)
                : approachDestination(from, to);

        int steps = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil(from.distanceTo(destination) / 4.0));
        for (int i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
            double t = (double) i / steps;
            player.setPos(
                    from.x + (destination.x - from.x) * t,
                    from.y + (destination.y - from.y) * t,
                    from.z + (destination.z - from.z) * t);
        }

        // Face the target after moving so hits keep landing — unless the user disabled camera movement.
        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(rotateCamera.get())) {
            double dx = to.x - destination.x;
            double dz = to.z - destination.z;
            player.setYRot((float) (Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(dz, dx)) - 90.0));
        }
    }

    /** Stop a short distance in front of the target rather than inside it. */
    private Vec3 approachDestination(Vec3 from, Vec3 to) {
        Vec3 direction = to.subtract(from);
        double distance = direction.length();
        if (distance <= 0.001) {
            return to;
        }
        return to.subtract(direction.scale(keepDistance.get() / distance));
    }

    /**
     * Blink to a rotating point around the target each hit, so an enemy trying to aim
     * at you never finds you in the same spot twice.
     */
    private Vec3 strafeDestination(Vec3 to) {
        double radius = strafeRadius.get();
        StrafeMode mode = strafeMode.get();

        switch (mode) {
            case UP -> {
                // Perch above the target so it has to aim up to reach you.
                return new Vec3(to.x, to.y + radius + 1.5, to.z);
            }
            case AROUND -> {
                int directions = Math.max(2, strafeSteps.get());
                strafeIndex = (strafeIndex + 1) % directions;
                double angle = (Math.PI * 2 / directions) * strafeIndex;
                return new Vec3(to.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius, to.y, to.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius);
            }
            case RANDOM -> {
                double angle = nextAngle(0.6);
                return new Vec3(to.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius, to.y, to.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius);
            }
            default -> {
                // ADVANCED: vary angle, radius and height, blending toward a purely uniform
                // pick as strength rises, and avoid landing on the previous spot.
                double strength = randomStrength.get();
                double angle = nextAngle(0.9 * strength);
                double minR = Math.min(minRadius.get(), radius);
                double r = minR + random.nextDouble() * (radius - minR) * strength;
                double vertical = (random.nextDouble() * 2 - 1) * verticalVariation.get() * strength;
                return new Vec3(to.x + Math.cos(angle) * r, to.y + vertical, to.z + Math.sin(angle) * r);
            }
        }
    }

    /** A fresh random angle, rerolled so it differs from the previous one by at least {@code minDelta} radians. */
    private double nextAngle(double minDelta) {
        double angle = random.nextDouble() * Math.PI * 2;
        if (avoidRepeat.get() && !Double.isNaN(lastAngle)) {
            for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 6 && angularDistance(angle, lastAngle) < minDelta; attempt++) {
                angle = random.nextDouble() * Math.PI * 2;
            }
        }
        lastAngle = angle;
        return angle;
    }

    private static double angularDistance(double a, double b) {
        double diff = Math.abs(a - b) % (Math.PI * 2);
        return diff > Math.PI ? Math.PI * 2 - diff : diff;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/combat/CriticalsModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.combat;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundMovePlayerPacket;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "Criticals",
        description = "Sends micro-hop packets so ground attacks register as critical hits",
        category = Category.COMBAT
)
public class CriticalsModule extends Module {
    public enum Mode {
        PACKET,
        MINI_JUMP
    }

    private final Setting<Mode> mode = new Setting<>("Mode", Mode.class, Mode.PACKET);
    private final Setting<Boolean> onlyWhileSprinting = new Setting<>("Only While Sprinting", Boolean.class, false);

    private int packetPhase;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        packetPhase = 0;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }
        if (!player.onGround() || player.isInWater() || player.isInLava()) {
            return;
        }
        if (onlyWhileSprinting.get() && !player.isSprinting()) {
            return;
        }

        if (mode.get() == Mode.MINI_JUMP) {
            player.setDeltaMovement(player.getDeltaMovement().x, 0.1, player.getDeltaMovement().z);
            return;
        }

        // Packet mode: a small up/down offset sequence makes the server treat the
        // next attack as if the player were falling, without visibly moving.
        double x = player.getX();
        double y = player.getY();
        double z = player.getZ();
        boolean horizontalCollision = player.horizontalCollision;

        switch (packetPhase) {
            case 0 -> player.connection.send(new ServerboundMovePlayerPacket.Pos(x, y + 0.0625, z, false, horizontalCollision));
            case 1 -> player.connection.send(new ServerboundMovePlayerPacket.Pos(x, y, z, false, horizontalCollision));
            case 2 -> player.connection.send(new ServerboundMovePlayerPacket.Pos(x, y + 1.1e-5, z, false, horizontalCollision));
            default -> player.connection.send(new ServerboundMovePlayerPacket.Pos(x, y, z, true, horizontalCollision));
        }
        packetPhase = (packetPhase + 1) % 4;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/combat/KillAuraModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.combat;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.Animal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.Enemy;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "KillAura",
        description = "Attacks entities in range automatically, respecting the vanilla attack cooldown",
        category = Category.COMBAT
)
public class KillAuraModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> range = new Setting<>("Range", Double.class, 4.5, 3.0, 6.0, 0.1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Integer> delay = new Setting<>("Delay", Integer.class, 0, 0, 20, 1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> targetPlayers = new Setting<>("Players", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> targetHostile = new Setting<>("Hostile", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> targetPassive = new Setting<>("Passive", Boolean.class, false);
    private final Setting<Integer> maxTargets = new Setting<>("Max Targets", Integer.class, 1, 1, 8, 1, () -> true);

    private final List<LivingEntity> targets = new ArrayList<>();
    private int ticksSinceAttack;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        targets.clear();
        ticksSinceAttack = 0;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null || getMinecraft().level == null || player.isDeadOrDying()) {
            return;
        }

        ticksSinceAttack++;
        if (delay.get() > 0) {
            if (ticksSinceAttack < delay.get()) {
                return;
            }
        } else if (player.getAttackStrengthScale(0.0f) < 1.0f) {
            return;
        }

        targets.clear();
        double maxRange = range.get();
        for (Entity entity : getMinecraft().level.entitiesForRendering()) {
            if (!(entity instanceof LivingEntity living) || entity == player) {
                continue;
            }
            if (!living.isAlive() || living.isDeadOrDying()) {
                continue;
            }
            if (player.distanceTo(living) > maxRange) {
                continue;
            }
            if (!isValidTarget(living)) {
                continue;
            }
            targets.add(living);
        }

        if (targets.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        targets.sort(Comparator.comparingDouble(player::distanceTo));
        int count = Math.min(targets.size(), maxTargets.get());
        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            getMinecraft().gameMode.attack(player, targets.get(i));
        }
        player.swing(InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
        ticksSinceAttack = 0;

        // Let TargetTP follow KillAura's hits (it otherwise only sees manual attack-key presses).
        AutoTpAuraModule targetTp = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(AutoTpAuraModule.class);
        if (targetTp != null && targetTp.isEnabled()) {
            targetTp.onExternalAttack(targets.get(0));
        }
    }

    private boolean isValidTarget(LivingEntity living) {
        if (living instanceof Player) {
            return Boolean.TRUE.equals(targetPlayers.get());
        }
        if (living instanceof Enemy) {
            return Boolean.TRUE.equals(targetHostile.get());
        }
        if (living instanceof Animal) {
            return Boolean.TRUE.equals(targetPassive.get());
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/combat/MaceHitModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.combat;

import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.ClientLevel;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundMovePlayerPacket;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundMoveVehiclePacket;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Block;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.EntityHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "MaceHit",
        description = "Spikes up before a mace smash so fall damage one-shots the target",
        category = Category.COMBAT
)
public class MaceHitModule extends Module {
    private static final int MAX_HEIGHT = 170;

    private final Setting<Integer> height = new Setting<>("Height", Integer.class, MAX_HEIGHT, 1, MAX_HEIGHT, 1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> maximum = new Setting<>("Maximum", Boolean.class, true);

    private boolean attackWasDown;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        attackWasDown = false;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        if (player == null || minecraft.level == null || minecraft.gui.screen() != null) {
            attackWasDown = false;
            return;
        }

        boolean attackDown = minecraft.options.keyAttack.isDown();
        boolean attackPressed = attackDown && !attackWasDown;
        attackWasDown = attackDown;
        if (!attackPressed) {
            return;
        }

        HitResult hit = minecraft.hitResult;
        if (!(hit instanceof EntityHitResult entityHit) || hit.getType() != HitResult.Type.ENTITY) {
            return;
        }

        Entity target = entityHit.getEntity();
        if (target == null || target.isRemoved() || !target.isAlive()) {
            return;
        }
        if (!player.getMainHandItem().is(Items.MACE)) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            execute(minecraft, player, target);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            print("Error: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void execute(Minecraft minecraft, LocalPlayer player, Entity target) {
        ClientLevel level = minecraft.level;
        int requestedHeight = maximum.get() ? MAX_HEIGHT : height.get();

        int x = player.getBlockX();
        int z = player.getBlockZ();
        int startY = player.getBlockY() + requestedHeight;

        Integer safeY = findSafePosition(level, x, z, startY);
        if (safeY == null) {
            print("No suitable position found");
            return;
        }

        Vec3 origin = player.position();
        boolean mounted = player.isPassenger() && player.getVehicle() != null;
        Entity vehicle = mounted ? player.getVehicle() : null;

        // 1) Establish the high position (server registers a large drop as fall distance).
        sendHighPosition(player, vehicle, origin, safeY);
        // 2) Resolve the attack while the server still thinks we are high up / falling.
        minecraft.gameMode.attack(player, target);
        player.swing(InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
        // 3) Restore the real position so we do not actually move.
        sendRestore(player, vehicle, origin);
    }

    private void sendHighPosition(LocalPlayer player, Entity vehicle, Vec3 origin, int safeY) {
        if (vehicle != null) {
            player.connection.send(new ServerboundMoveVehiclePacket(
                    new Vec3(origin.x, safeY, origin.z), vehicle.getYRot(), vehicle.getXRot(), false));
        } else {
            // Two packets: teleport up (not on ground), then "drop" back so the server
            // accumulates the fall distance the smash bonus is based on.
            player.connection.send(new ServerboundMovePlayerPacket.Pos(
                    origin.x, safeY, origin.z, false, player.horizontalCollision));
            player.connection.send(new ServerboundMovePlayerPacket.Pos(
                    origin.x, origin.y, origin.z, false, player.horizontalCollision));
        }
    }

    private void sendRestore(LocalPlayer player, Entity vehicle, Vec3 origin) {
        if (vehicle != null) {
            player.connection.send(ServerboundMoveVehiclePacket.fromEntity(vehicle));
        } else {
            player.connection.send(new ServerboundMovePlayerPacket.Pos(
                    origin.x, origin.y, origin.z, player.onGround(), player.horizontalCollision));
        }
    }

    private Integer findSafePosition(ClientLevel level, int x, int z, int startY) {
        int minY = level.getMinY();
        for (int y = Math.min(startY, level.getMaxY()); y >= minY; y--) {
            if (isAir(level, x, y, z) && isAir(level, x, y + 1, z)) {
                return y;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    private boolean isAir(ClientLevel level, int x, int y, int z) {
        Block block = level.getBlockState(new BlockPos(x, y, z)).getBlock();
        return block == Blocks.AIR || block == Blocks.CAVE_AIR || block == Blocks.VOID_AIR;
    }

    private void print(String message) {
        Lenrete.LOGGER.info("[MaceHit] {}", message);
        ToastManager.show(message);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/combat/ReachModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.combat;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "Reach",
        description = "Extends block and entity interaction range",
        category = Category.COMBAT
)
public class ReachModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> blockReach = new Setting<>("Block Reach", Double.class, 6.0, 3.0, 12.0, 0.5, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> entityReach = new Setting<>("Entity Reach", Double.class, 6.0, 3.0, 12.0, 0.5, () -> true);

    private Double originalBlock;
    private Double originalEntity;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        restore();
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        AttributeInstance block = player.getAttribute(Attributes.BLOCK_INTERACTION_RANGE);
        AttributeInstance entity = player.getAttribute(Attributes.ENTITY_INTERACTION_RANGE);

        if (block != null) {
            if (originalBlock == null) {
                originalBlock = block.getBaseValue();
            }
            block.setBaseValue(blockReach.get());
        }
        if (entity != null) {
            if (originalEntity == null) {
                originalEntity = entity.getBaseValue();
            }
            entity.setBaseValue(entityReach.get());
        }
    }

    private void restore() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player != null) {
            AttributeInstance block = player.getAttribute(Attributes.BLOCK_INTERACTION_RANGE);
            AttributeInstance entity = player.getAttribute(Attributes.ENTITY_INTERACTION_RANGE);
            if (block != null && originalBlock != null) {
                block.setBaseValue(originalBlock);
            }
            if (entity != null && originalEntity != null) {
                entity.setBaseValue(originalEntity);
            }
        }
        originalBlock = null;
        originalEntity = null;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/combat/SpearHitModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.combat;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.EntityHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "SpearHit",
        description = "Dashes at the target at top speed with a spear and strikes on arrival",
        category = Category.COMBAT
)
public class SpearHitModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> speed = new Setting<>("Dash Speed", Double.class, 2.0, 0.5, 5.0, 0.1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> range = new Setting<>("Range", Double.class, 8.0, 3.0, 20.0, 1.0, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Integer> cooldown = new Setting<>("Cooldown", Integer.class, 10, 0, 40, 1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> anyItem = new Setting<>("Any Item", Boolean.class, false);

    private static final int MAX_DASH_TICKS = 30;

    private int cooldownTicks;
    private Entity dashTarget;
    private int dashTicks;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        cooldownTicks = 0;
        endDash();
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        if (player == null || minecraft.level == null) {
            endDash();
            return;
        }

        // An in-progress dash keeps flying at the target every tick until it connects,
        // regardless of the attack button — this is what actually lands the hit.
        if (dashTarget != null) {
            continueDash(minecraft, player);
            return;
        }

        if (cooldownTicks > 0) {
            cooldownTicks--;
            return;
        }
        if (minecraft.gui.screen() != null) {
            return;
        }

        // Trigger while the attack button is held near an enemy, so it works on a hold.
        if (!minecraft.options.keyAttack.isDown()) {
            return;
        }
        if (!anyItem.get() && !isSpear(player.getMainHandItem().getItem())) {
            return;
        }

        Entity target = crosshairTarget(minecraft);
        if (target == null) {
            target = nearestTarget(player);
        }
        if (target == null || player.distanceTo(target) > range.get()) {
            return;
        }

        dashTarget = target;
        dashTicks = 0;
    }

    private void continueDash(Minecraft minecraft, LocalPlayer player) {
        dashTicks++;
        if (dashTarget == null || !dashTarget.isAlive() || dashTarget.isRemoved()
                || dashTicks > MAX_DASH_TICKS
                || player.distanceTo(dashTarget) > range.get() * 2) {
            endDash();
            return;
        }

        double distance = player.distanceTo(dashTarget);

        // In reach: strike, brake, and start the cooldown.
        if (distance <= 3.0) {
            minecraft.gameMode.attack(player, dashTarget);
            player.swing(InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
            player.setDeltaMovement(player.getDeltaMovement().scale(0.2));
            cooldownTicks = cooldown.get();
            endDash();
            return;
        }

        // Not there yet: keep accelerating straight at the target's body.
        Vec3 to = dashTarget.position().add(0, dashTarget.getBbHeight() * 0.5, 0);
        Vec3 from = player.getEyePosition();
        Vec3 direction = to.subtract(from);
        double length = direction.length();
        if (length < 0.001) {
            return;
        }
        player.setDeltaMovement(direction.scale(speed.get() / length));
        player.fallDistance = 0;
        player.setYRot((float) (Math.toDegrees(Math.atan2(to.z - from.z, to.x - from.x)) - 90.0));
    }

    private void endDash() {
        dashTarget = null;
        dashTicks = 0;
    }

    private Entity crosshairTarget(Minecraft minecraft) {
        HitResult hit = minecraft.hitResult;
        if (hit instanceof EntityHitResult entityHit && hit.getType() == HitResult.Type.ENTITY) {
            Entity target = entityHit.getEntity();
            if (target != null && target.isAlive() && !target.isRemoved()) {
                return target;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    private Entity nearestTarget(LocalPlayer player) {
        Entity best = null;
        double bestDistance = range.get();
        for (Entity entity : getMinecraft().level.entitiesForRendering()) {
            if (entity == player || !entity.isAlive() || entity.isRemoved()) {
                continue;
            }
            if (!(entity instanceof net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity)) {
                continue;
            }
            double distance = player.distanceTo(entity);
            if (distance < bestDistance && isInFront(player, entity)) {
                bestDistance = distance;
                best = entity;
            }
        }
        return best;
    }

    private boolean isInFront(LocalPlayer player, Entity entity) {
        Vec3 look = player.getViewVector(1.0f);
        Vec3 toEntity = entity.position().subtract(player.position()).normalize();
        return look.dot(toEntity) > 0.5;
    }

    private boolean isSpear(Item item) {
        return item == Items.WOODEN_SPEAR
                || item == Items.STONE_SPEAR
                || item == Items.COPPER_SPEAR
                || item == Items.IRON_SPEAR
                || item == Items.GOLDEN_SPEAR
                || item == Items.DIAMOND_SPEAR
                || item == Items.NETHERITE_SPEAR
                || item == Items.TRIDENT;
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "AutoMessage",
        description = "Sends a configured message in chat when toggled on",
        category = Category.MISC
)
public class AutoMessageModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<String> message = new Setting<>("Message", String.class, "gg");

    @Override
    protected void onEnable() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player != null) {
            String text = message.get();
            if (text != null && !text.isBlank()) {
                player.connection.sendChat(text);
            }
        }
        setEnabled(false);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/misc/AutoSignModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc;

import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.screen.v1.ScreenEvents;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.AbstractSignEditScreen;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "AutoSign",
        description = "Fills and confirms sign edit screens with configured text",
        category = Category.MISC
)
public class AutoSignModule extends Module {
    private static boolean listenerRegistered;

    private final Setting<String> line1 = new Setting<>("Line 1", String.class, "");
    private final Setting<String> line2 = new Setting<>("Line 2", String.class, "");
    private final Setting<String> line3 = new Setting<>("Line 3", String.class, "");
    private final Setting<String> line4 = new Setting<>("Line 4", String.class, "");

    public AutoSignModule() {
        registerListener();
    }

    private static void registerListener() {
        if (listenerRegistered) {
            return;
        }
        listenerRegistered = true;
        ScreenEvents.AFTER_INIT.register((client, screen, width, height) -> {
            if (!(screen instanceof AbstractSignEditScreen signScreen)) {
                return;
            }
            AutoSignModule module = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(AutoSignModule.class);
            if (module != null && module.isEnabled()) {
                module.applyTo(signScreen);
            }
        });
    }

    private void applyTo(AbstractSignEditScreen signScreen) {
        try {
            Field messagesField = AbstractSignEditScreen.class.getDeclaredField("messages");
            messagesField.setAccessible(true);
            String[] messages = (String[]) messagesField.get(signScreen);
            String[] values = {line1.get(), line2.get(), line3.get(), line4.get()};
            for (int i = 0; i < messages.length && i < values.length; i++) {
                messages[i] = values[i] == null ? "" : values[i];
            }

            Method onDone = AbstractSignEditScreen.class.getDeclaredMethod("onDone");
            onDone.setAccessible(true);
            onDone.invoke(signScreen);
        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.warn("AutoSign could not apply to the sign screen", e);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/misc/BlinkerModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.Packet;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundMovePlayerPacket;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundMoveVehiclePacket;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "Blinker",
        description = "Holds back your movement from the server, then releases it all at once when disabled",
        category = Category.MISC
)
public class BlinkerModule extends Module {
    @Override
    protected void onEnable() {
        ToastManager.show("Blinker: server frozen — disable to blink");
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        ToastManager.show("Blinker: released");
    }

    /** True if the outgoing packet is a movement packet that should be withheld while blinking. */
    public static boolean shouldBlockMovement(Packet<?> packet) {
        BlinkerModule module = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(BlinkerModule.class);
        if (module == null || !module.isEnabled()) {
            return false;
        }
        return packet instanceof ServerboundMovePlayerPacket || packet instanceof ServerboundMoveVehiclePacket;
    }
}
```

```java
    private static int packColor(ColorValue color, Integer opacity, int defaultAlpha, int defaultRgb) {
        int rgb = color != null ? (color.argb() & 0x00FFFFFF) : defaultRgb;
        int alpha = opacity != null ? opacity : defaultAlpha;
        return ((alpha & 0xFF) << 24) | rgb;
    }
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/misc/NukerModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.MultiPlayerGameMode;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "Nuker",
        description = "Breaks nearby blocks within a radius around the player",
        category = Category.MISC
)
public class NukerModule extends Module {
    public enum Mode {
        NEAREST,
        ALL
    }

    private final Setting<Integer> radius = new Setting<>("Radius", Integer.class, 4, 1, 6, 1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Mode> mode = new Setting<>("Mode", Mode.class, Mode.NEAREST);
    private final Setting<Boolean> respectTool = new Setting<>("Respect Tool", Boolean.class, true);

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        MultiPlayerGameMode gameMode = minecraft.gameMode;
        if (player == null || gameMode == null || minecraft.level == null) {
            return;
        }

        int r = radius.get();
        boolean creative = gameMode.getPlayerMode().isCreative();
        BlockPos origin = player.blockPosition();

        BlockPos best = null;
        double bestDistance = Double.MAX_VALUE;

        for (int dx = -r; dx <= r; dx++) {
            for (int dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {
                for (int dz = -r; dz <= r; dz++) {
                    BlockPos pos = origin.offset(dx, dy, dz);
                    BlockState state = minecraft.level.getBlockState(pos);
                    if (state.isAir() || state.liquid()) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (player.distanceToSqr(pos.getX() + 0.5, pos.getY() + 0.5, pos.getZ() + 0.5) > (double) r * r) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (respectTool.get() && !creative && state.getDestroySpeed(minecraft.level, pos) < 0) {
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (mode.get() == Mode.ALL) {
                        breakBlock(gameMode, player, pos, creative);
                    } else {
                        double distance = player.distanceToSqr(pos.getX() + 0.5, pos.getY() + 0.5, pos.getZ() + 0.5);
                        if (distance < bestDistance) {
                            bestDistance = distance;
                            best = pos;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (best != null) {
            breakBlock(gameMode, player, best, creative);
        }
    }

    private void breakBlock(MultiPlayerGameMode gameMode, LocalPlayer player, BlockPos pos, boolean creative) {
        if (creative) {
            gameMode.destroyBlock(pos);
        } else {
            gameMode.startDestroyBlock(pos, Direction.UP);
            gameMode.continueDestroyBlock(pos, Direction.UP);
        }
        player.swing(InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/AirJumpModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "AirJump",
        description = "Lets you jump again while in mid-air",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class AirJumpModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Boolean> unlimited = new Setting<>("Unlimited", Boolean.class, false);
    private final Setting<Integer> maxJumps = new Setting<>("Extra Jumps", Integer.class, 1, 1, 5, 1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> jumpPower = new Setting<>("Jump Power", Double.class, 0.42, 0.2, 1.0, 0.02, () -> true);

    private int jumpsUsed;
    private boolean jumpWasDown;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        jumpsUsed = 0;
        jumpWasDown = false;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        if (player == null || minecraft.gui.screen() != null) {
            jumpWasDown = false;
            return;
        }

        if (player.onGround() || player.onClimbable() || player.isInWater() || player.getAbilities().flying) {
            jumpsUsed = 0;
        }

        boolean jumpDown = minecraft.options.keyJump.isDown();
        boolean pressed = jumpDown && !jumpWasDown;
        jumpWasDown = jumpDown;

        boolean allowed = unlimited.get() || jumpsUsed < maxJumps.get();
        if (pressed && !player.onGround() && allowed) {
            Vec3 motion = player.getDeltaMovement();
            player.setDeltaMovement(motion.x, jumpPower.get(), motion.z);
            player.fallDistance = 0;
            jumpsUsed++;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/AutoWalkModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Input;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "AutoWalk",
        description = "Holds the movement key so you keep walking automatically",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class AutoWalkModule extends Module {
    public enum Facing {
        FORWARD,
        BACKWARD
    }

    private final Setting<Facing> facing = new Setting<>("Facing", Facing.class, Facing.FORWARD);

    /**
     * Applied from the ClientInput mixin after vanilla computes the movement keys (which only
     * happens while no screen is open), so the forced direction actually drives the player.
     */
    public static Input modify(Input current) {
        AutoWalkModule module = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(AutoWalkModule.class);
        if (module == null || !module.isEnabled()) {
            return null;
        }
        if (Minecraft.getInstance().gui.screen() != null) {
            return null;
        }

        boolean forward = module.facing.get() == Facing.FORWARD;
        return new Input(forward, !forward, current.left(), current.right(),
                current.jump(), current.shift(), current.sprint());
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/ClickTpModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Keybind;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundMovePlayerPacket;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "ClickTP",
        description = "While enabled, press the teleport key to blink to where you are aiming",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class ClickTpModule extends Module {
    private static final double UNLIMITED_DISTANCE = 256.0;

    private final Setting<Keybind> teleportKey = new Setting<>("Teleport Key", Keybind.class, new Keybind(GLFW.GLFW_KEY_G));
    private final Setting<Boolean> unlimitedReach = new Setting<>("Unlimited Reach", Boolean.class, false);
    private final Setting<Double> maxDistance = new Setting<>("Max Distance", Double.class, 30.0, 5.0, 100.0, 5.0, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> movePackets = new Setting<>("Move Packets", Boolean.class, false);

    private boolean keyWasDown;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        keyWasDown = false;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        if (player == null || minecraft.gui.screen() != null) {
            keyWasDown = false;
            return;
        }

        Keybind bind = teleportKey.get();
        int code = bind != null ? bind.code() : -1;
        boolean keyDown = code > 0 && InputConstants.isKeyDown(minecraft.getWindow(), code);

        if (keyDown && !keyWasDown) {
            teleport(player);
        }
        keyWasDown = keyDown;
    }

    private void teleport(LocalPlayer player) {
        Vec3 target = resolveTarget(player);
        if (target == null) {
            ToastManager.show("ClickTP: no target");
            return;
        }

        Vec3 from = player.position();
        double distance = from.distanceTo(target);

        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(movePackets.get())) {
            // Stream many small movement packets in one tick. Because each step is under the
            // server's per-packet delta limit, they are accepted in sequence and carry you
            // horizontally straight through blocks (no client-side collision is applied).
            int steps = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil(distance / 0.9));
            for (int i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
                double t = (double) i / steps;
                double x = from.x + (target.x - from.x) * t;
                double y = from.y + (target.y - from.y) * t;
                double z = from.z + (target.z - from.z) * t;
                player.connection.send(new ServerboundMovePlayerPacket.Pos(x, y, z, false, false));
            }
            player.setPos(target.x, target.y, target.z);
            player.setDeltaMovement(Vec3.ZERO);
            ToastManager.show("ClickTP: teleported");
            return;
        }

        // Move in short steps so the server accepts each position delta.
        int steps = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil(distance / 4.0));
        for (int i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
            double t = (double) i / steps;
            player.setPos(
                    from.x + (target.x - from.x) * t,
                    from.y + (target.y - from.y) * t,
                    from.z + (target.z - from.z) * t);
        }
        ToastManager.show("ClickTP: teleported");
    }

    private Vec3 resolveTarget(LocalPlayer player) {
        HitResult hit = getMinecraft().hitResult;
        if (hit != null && hit.getType() != HitResult.Type.MISS) {
            return hit.getLocation();
        }
        Vec3 eye = player.getEyePosition();
        Vec3 look = player.getViewVector(1.0f);
        double reach = unlimitedReach.get() ? UNLIMITED_DISTANCE : maxDistance.get();
        return eye.add(look.scale(reach));
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/EntityControlModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Input;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.equine.AbstractHorse;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.vehicle.boat.AbstractBoat;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.vehicle.minecart.AbstractMinecart;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "EntityControl",
        description = "Steer rideable entities (horses, boats, minecarts) with your movement keys",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class EntityControlModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> forwardSpeed = new Setting<>("Forward Speed", Double.class, 0.5, 0.1, 2.0, 0.05, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> strafeSpeed = new Setting<>("Strafe Speed", Double.class, 0.4, 0.0, 2.0, 0.05, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> sprintMultiplier = new Setting<>("Sprint Multiplier", Double.class, 1.5, 1.0, 3.0, 0.1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> jumpStrength = new Setting<>("Jump Strength", Double.class, 0.5, 0.1, 1.5, 0.05, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> jump = new Setting<>("Jump", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> horses = new Setting<>("Horses", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> boats = new Setting<>("Boats", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> minecarts = new Setting<>("Minecarts", Boolean.class, true);

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null || !player.isPassenger() || player.input == null) {
            return;
        }
        Entity vehicle = player.getVehicle();
        if (vehicle == null || !isSupported(vehicle)) {
            return;
        }

        Input keys = player.input.keyPresses;
        double forwardInput = impulse(keys.forward(), keys.backward());
        double strafeInput = impulse(keys.left(), keys.right());
        boolean jumping = jump.get() && keys.jump();

        if (forwardInput == 0.0 && strafeInput == 0.0 && !jumping) {
            return;
        }

        // Drive the vehicle relative to where the PLAYER is looking (that's what the rider steers with).
        float yawRad = (float) Math.toRadians(player.getYRot());
        double sin = Math.sin(yawRad);
        double cos = Math.cos(yawRad);

        double speed = keys.sprint() ? sprintMultiplier.get() : 1.0;
        double fwd = forwardInput * forwardSpeed.get() * speed;
        double str = strafeInput * strafeSpeed.get() * speed;

        // Forward vector = (-sin, cos); right vector = (cos, sin) in world XZ.
        double moveX = fwd * -sin + str * cos;
        double moveZ = fwd * cos + str * sin;

        Vec3 motion = vehicle.getDeltaMovement();
        double motionY = motion.y;
        if (jumping && vehicle.onGround()) {
            motionY = jumpStrength.get();
        }
        vehicle.setDeltaMovement(moveX, motionY, moveZ);
        vehicle.setYRot(player.getYRot());
        vehicle.yRotO = player.getYRot();
    }

    private boolean isSupported(Entity vehicle) {
        if (vehicle instanceof AbstractHorse) {
            return horses.get();
        }
        if (vehicle instanceof AbstractBoat) {
            return boats.get();
        }
        if (vehicle instanceof AbstractMinecart) {
            return minecarts.get();
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static double impulse(boolean positive, boolean negative) {
        if (positive == negative) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        return positive ? 1.0 : -1.0;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/EntityFlyModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Input;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.equine.AbstractHorse;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.vehicle.boat.AbstractBoat;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.vehicle.minecart.AbstractMinecart;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "EntityFly",
        description = "Lets the mount/vehicle you are riding fly freely with your movement keys",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class EntityFlyModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> speed = new Setting<>("Speed", Double.class, 0.6, 0.1, 3.0, 0.05, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> verticalSpeed = new Setting<>("Vertical Speed", Double.class, 0.5, 0.1, 3.0, 0.05, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> horses = new Setting<>("Horses", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> boats = new Setting<>("Boats", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> minecarts = new Setting<>("Minecarts", Boolean.class, true);

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null || !player.isPassenger() || player.input == null) {
            return;
        }
        Entity vehicle = player.getVehicle();
        if (vehicle == null || !isSupported(vehicle)) {
            return;
        }

        Input keys = player.input.keyPresses;
        double forwardInput = impulse(keys.forward(), keys.backward());
        double strafeInput = impulse(keys.left(), keys.right());

        // Horizontal motion relative to where the rider is looking.
        float yawRad = (float) Math.toRadians(player.getYRot());
        double sin = Math.sin(yawRad);
        double cos = Math.cos(yawRad);
        double s = speed.get();
        double moveX = (forwardInput * -sin + strafeInput * cos) * s;
        double moveZ = (forwardInput * cos + strafeInput * sin) * s;

        // Vertical: jump rises, sneak descends, otherwise hover in place (gravity cancelled).
        double moveY = 0.0;
        if (keys.jump()) {
            moveY += verticalSpeed.get();
        }
        if (keys.shift()) {
            moveY -= verticalSpeed.get();
        }

        vehicle.setDeltaMovement(moveX, moveY, moveZ);
        vehicle.fallDistance = 0.0;
        vehicle.setYRot(player.getYRot());
        vehicle.yRotO = player.getYRot();
    }

    private boolean isSupported(Entity vehicle) {
        if (vehicle instanceof AbstractHorse) {
            return horses.get();
        }
        if (vehicle instanceof AbstractBoat) {
            return boats.get();
        }
        if (vehicle instanceof AbstractMinecart) {
            return minecarts.get();
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static double impulse(boolean positive, boolean negative) {
        if (positive == negative) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        return positive ? 1.0 : -1.0;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/EntitySpeedModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.equine.AbstractHorse;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.vehicle.boat.AbstractBoat;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.vehicle.minecart.AbstractMinecart;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "EntitySpeed",
        description = "Increases the movement speed of the mount/vehicle you are riding",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class EntitySpeedModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> multiplier = new Setting<>("Multiplier", Double.class, 1.5, 1.0, 5.0, 0.1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> maxSpeed = new Setting<>("Max Speed", Double.class, 2.0, 0.2, 5.0, 0.1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> horses = new Setting<>("Horses", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> boats = new Setting<>("Boats", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> minecarts = new Setting<>("Minecarts", Boolean.class, true);

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null || !player.isPassenger()) {
            return;
        }
        Entity vehicle = player.getVehicle();
        if (vehicle == null || !isSupported(vehicle)) {
            return;
        }

        // Amplify the vehicle's own motion rather than forcing an absolute velocity, so it keeps
        // moving in the direction it already is and vanilla control stays intact.
        Vec3 motion = vehicle.getDeltaMovement();
        double horizontal = Math.sqrt(motion.x * motion.x + motion.z * motion.z);
        if (horizontal < 1.0e-4) {
            return;
        }

        double factor = multiplier.get();
        double cap = maxSpeed.get();
        double scaled = Math.min(horizontal * factor, cap);
        double ratio = scaled / horizontal;
        vehicle.setDeltaMovement(motion.x * ratio, motion.y, motion.z * ratio);
    }

    private boolean isSupported(Entity vehicle) {
        if (vehicle instanceof AbstractHorse) {
            return horses.get();
        }
        if (vehicle instanceof AbstractBoat) {
            return boats.get();
        }
        if (vehicle instanceof AbstractMinecart) {
            return minecarts.get();
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/FlightModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.config.ConfigManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Abilities;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec2;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "Flight",
        description = "Creative-style flight",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class FlightModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> speed = new Setting<>("Speed", Double.class, 0.6, 0.1, 3.0, 0.05, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> verticalSpeed = new Setting<>("Vertical Speed", Double.class, 0.6, 0.1, 3.0, 0.05, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> antiKick = new Setting<>("Anti-Kick", Boolean.class, false);
    private final Setting<Boolean> disableOnDamage = new Setting<>("Disable On Damage", Boolean.class, true);

    private boolean originalFlying;
    private boolean originalMayFly;
    private float lastHealth = -1;
    private boolean wasHurt;
    private int ticks;

    @Override
    protected void onEnable() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        Abilities abilities = player.getAbilities();
        originalFlying = abilities.flying;
        originalMayFly = abilities.mayfly;
        abilities.mayfly = true;
        abilities.flying = true;
        player.onUpdateAbilities();

        lastHealth = player.getHealth();
        wasHurt = player.hurtTime > 0;
        ticks = 0;
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        Abilities abilities = player.getAbilities();
        abilities.flying = originalFlying;
        abilities.mayfly = originalMayFly;
        player.onUpdateAbilities();
        lastHealth = -1;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        // Disable the moment we take a hit: either health dropped or the hurt animation
        // just started (catches damage the health check alone would miss).
        boolean hurtNow = player.hurtTime > 0;
        boolean tookDamage = (lastHealth >= 0 && player.getHealth() < lastHealth) || (hurtNow && !wasHurt);
        wasHurt = hurtNow;
        if (disableOnDamage.get() && tookDamage) {
            setEnabled(false);
            dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager.show("Flight disabled: damage taken");
            ConfigManager.saveConfigDebounced();
            return;
        }
        lastHealth = player.getHealth();

        if (!player.getAbilities().flying) {
            player.getAbilities().flying = true;
            player.onUpdateAbilities();
        }

        ticks++;
        double vertical;
        if (player.input.keyPresses.jump()) {
            vertical = verticalSpeed.get();
        } else if (player.input.keyPresses.shift()) {
            vertical = -verticalSpeed.get();
        } else if (antiKick.get() && ticks % 40 == 0) {
            vertical = -0.05;
        } else {
            vertical = 0;
        }

        Vec2 move = player.input.getMoveVector();
        double yaw = Math.toRadians(player.getYRot());
        double forward = move.y * speed.get();
        double strafe = move.x * speed.get();
        double sinYaw = Math.sin(yaw);
        double cosYaw = Math.cos(yaw);
        double dx = strafe * cosYaw - forward * sinYaw;
        double dz = forward * cosYaw + strafe * sinYaw;

        player.setDeltaMovement(dx, vertical, dz);
        player.fallDistance = 0;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/FreecamModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import dev.dd.lenrete.util.MovementKeys;
import net.minecraft.client.CameraType;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Input;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "Freecam",
        description = "Detaches the camera so you can fly around while your body stays put",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class FreecamModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> speed = new Setting<>("Speed", Double.class, 0.6, 0.1, 3.0, 0.1, () -> true);

    // Camera position updated each tick; the previous value is kept so the Camera mixin can
    // interpolate per frame for smooth flight.
    private Vec3 cameraPos = Vec3.ZERO;
    private Vec3 prevCameraPos = Vec3.ZERO;
    private Vec3 frozenPos;
    private boolean originalNoPhysics;
    private CameraType originalCameraType;

    @Override
    protected void onEnable() {
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        if (player == null || minecraft.level == null) {
            setEnabled(false);
            return;
        }

        frozenPos = player.position();
        originalNoPhysics = player.noPhysics;
        cameraPos = new Vec3(player.getX(), player.getEyeY(), player.getZ());
        prevCameraPos = cameraPos;

        // Freeze the real body: no physics means it neither falls nor drifts.
        player.noPhysics = true;
        player.setDeltaMovement(Vec3.ZERO);

        // Detach the view so the game renders your real player model at the frozen position.
        originalCameraType = minecraft.options.getCameraType();
        minecraft.options.setCameraType(CameraType.THIRD_PERSON_BACK);
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        if (player != null) {
            player.noPhysics = originalNoPhysics;
            if (frozenPos != null) {
                player.setPos(frozenPos.x, frozenPos.y, frozenPos.z);
                player.setDeltaMovement(Vec3.ZERO);
            }
        }
        if (originalCameraType != null) {
            minecraft.options.setCameraType(originalCameraType);
            originalCameraType = null;
        }
        frozenPos = null;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        // Hold the body still.
        player.input.keyPresses = Input.EMPTY;
        player.setDeltaMovement(Vec3.ZERO);
        if (frozenPos != null) {
            player.setPos(frozenPos.x, frozenPos.y, frozenPos.z);
        }

        prevCameraPos = cameraPos;

        Input keys = MovementKeys.poll(minecraft);
        double s = speed.get() * (keys.sprint() ? 2.0 : 1.0);
        double yaw = Math.toRadians(player.getYRot());
        double sin = Math.sin(yaw);
        double cos = Math.cos(yaw);

        // forward = (-sin, cos); right = (-cos, -sin), so D moves to the camera's right.
        double dx = 0;
        double dz = 0;
        if (keys.forward()) {
            dx += -sin * s;
            dz += cos * s;
        }
        if (keys.backward()) {
            dx += sin * s;
            dz += -cos * s;
        }
        if (keys.right()) {
            dx += -cos * s;
            dz += -sin * s;
        }
        if (keys.left()) {
            dx += cos * s;
            dz += sin * s;
        }
        double dy = 0;
        if (keys.jump()) {
            dy += s;
        }
        if (keys.shift()) {
            dy -= s;
        }

        cameraPos = cameraPos.add(dx, dy, dz);
    }

    /** The interpolated freecam camera position, or null when inactive (read by the Camera mixin). */
    public static Vec3 getCameraPosition(float partialTick) {
        FreecamModule module = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(FreecamModule.class);
        if (module == null || !module.isEnabled()) {
            return null;
        }
        Vec3 prev = module.prevCameraPos;
        Vec3 cur = module.cameraPos;
        return new Vec3(
                prev.x + (cur.x - prev.x) * partialTick,
                prev.y + (cur.y - prev.y) * partialTick,
                prev.z + (cur.z - prev.z) * partialTick);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/InventoryMoveModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import dev.dd.lenrete.util.MovementKeys;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.components.EditBox;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.inventory.AbstractContainerScreen;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Input;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "InventoryMove",
        description = "walk around while an inventory screen is open",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT,
        unavailable = false
)
public class InventoryMoveModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Boolean> allowSneak = new Setting<>("Allow Sneak", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> allowJump = new Setting<>("Allow Jump", Boolean.class, true);

    /**
     * Called from the ClientInput mixin (run from LocalPlayer.aiStep every tick, including
     * while a screen is open). Returns raw-polled movement to override the empty key presses
     * vanilla produces during a container screen, or null to leave input alone.
     */
    public static Input override(Input current) {
        InventoryMoveModule module = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(InventoryMoveModule.class);
        if (module == null || !module.isEnabled()) {
            return null;
        }

        Minecraft minecraft = Minecraft.getInstance();
        // Only supported container screens — never chat, sign, book, or config screens.
        if (!(minecraft.gui.screen() instanceof AbstractContainerScreen<?>)) {
            return null;
        }

        if (minecraft.gui.screen().getFocused() instanceof EditBox editBox && editBox.isFocused()) {
            return null;
        }

        Input polled = MovementKeys.poll(minecraft);
        boolean sneak = module.allowSneak.get() && polled.shift();
        boolean jump = module.allowJump.get() && polled.jump();
        return new Input(polled.forward(), polled.backward(), polled.left(), polled.right(),
                jump, sneak, polled.sprint());
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/NoFallModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundMovePlayerPacket;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "NoFall",
        description = "Cancels fall damage",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class NoFallModule extends Module {
    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null || player.getAbilities().flying || player.isInWater() || player.isInLava()) {
            return;
        }

        // Fall damage is applied by the server from its own fall tracking, so a
        // client-side fallDistance reset is not enough. Reporting onGround=true
        // while falling makes the server reset its tracker each tick.
        if (player.fallDistance > 2.0 && player.getDeltaMovement().y < 0) {
            player.connection.send(new ServerboundMovePlayerPacket.StatusOnly(true, player.horizontalCollision));
            player.fallDistance = 0;
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/SpeedModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "Speed",
        description = "Multiplies horizontal movement velocity",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class SpeedModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> multiplier = new Setting<>("Multiplier", Double.class, 1.5, 1.0, 3.0, 0.1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> onlyOnGround = new Setting<>("Only On Ground", Boolean.class, false);
    private final Setting<Boolean> onlyWhileSprinting = new Setting<>("Only While Sprinting", Boolean.class, false);

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }
        if (onlyOnGround.get() && !player.onGround()) {
            return;
        }
        if (onlyWhileSprinting.get() && !player.isSprinting()) {
            return;
        }

        Vec3 motion = player.getDeltaMovement();
        double factor = multiplier.get();
        player.setDeltaMovement(motion.x * factor, motion.y, motion.z * factor);
    }
}
```
