`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/SpiderModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "Spider",
        description = "Climb walls by holding into them",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class SpiderModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> climbSpeed = new Setting<>("Climb Speed", Double.class, 0.2, 0.1, 0.5, 0.05, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> holdAtTop = new Setting<>("Hold At Top", Boolean.class, true);

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        boolean pushingIntoWall = player.horizontalCollision;
        // minorHorizontalCollision covers grazing an edge, where horizontalCollision flickers.
        boolean grazing = player.minorHorizontalCollision;

        if ((pushingIntoWall || grazing) && player.input.hasForwardImpulse()) {
            Vec3 motion = player.getDeltaMovement();
            player.setDeltaMovement(motion.x, climbSpeed.get(), motion.z);
            player.fallDistance = 0;
        } else if (holdAtTop.get() && player.input.hasForwardImpulse() && !player.onGround()) {
            // Prevent dropping the moment you clear the top lip of the wall.
            Vec3 motion = player.getDeltaMovement();
            if (motion.y < 0) {
                player.setDeltaMovement(motion.x, Math.max(motion.y, -0.08), motion.z);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/SprintModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "Sprint",
        description = "Automatically keeps sprinting when moving forward",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class SprintModule extends Module {
    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        boolean canSprint = player.input.hasForwardImpulse()
                && (player.getFoodData().getFoodLevel() > 6 || player.getAbilities().flying)
                && !player.isShiftKeyDown()
                && !player.hasEffect(MobEffects.BLINDNESS)
                && !player.horizontalCollision;

        if (canSprint) {
            player.setSprinting(true);
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player != null) {
            player.setSprinting(false);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/movement/StepModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.movement;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.AttributeInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ai.attributes.Attributes;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "Step",
        description = "Walk up blocks taller than the vanilla step height",
        category = Category.MOVEMENT
)
public class StepModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> height = new Setting<>("Height", Double.class, 1.0, 0.6, 2.5, 0.1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> onlyOnGround = new Setting<>("Only On Ground", Boolean.class, false);
    private final Setting<Boolean> safe = new Setting<>("Safe", Boolean.class, true);

    private Double originalStepHeight;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        restoreStepHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        if (onlyOnGround.get() && !player.onGround()) {
            restoreStepHeight();
            return;
        }

        AttributeInstance attribute = player.getAttribute(Attributes.STEP_HEIGHT);
        if (attribute == null) {
            return;
        }

        if (originalStepHeight == null) {
            originalStepHeight = attribute.getBaseValue();
        }

        boolean boost = !safe.get() || player.onGround();
        attribute.setBaseValue(boost ? height.get() : originalStepHeight);
    }

    private void restoreStepHeight() {
        if (originalStepHeight == null) {
            return;
        }
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player != null) {
            AttributeInstance attribute = player.getAttribute(Attributes.STEP_HEIGHT);
            if (attribute != null) {
                attribute.setBaseValue(originalStepHeight);
            }
        }
        originalStepHeight = null;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/player/AntiHungerModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.player;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "AntiHunger",
        description = "Reduces the exhaustion that drains hunger, without forcing values or blocking eating",
        category = Category.PLAYER
)
public class AntiHungerModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Integer> reduction = new Setting<>("Reduction", Integer.class, 100, 0, 100, 5, () -> true);

    /**
     * Multiplier applied to exhaustion by the FoodData mixin. 1.0 = vanilla (module off),
     * 0.0 = no exhaustion at all. Eating and saturation gain are untouched.
     */
    public static float exhaustionFactor() {
        AntiHungerModule module = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(AntiHungerModule.class);
        if (module == null || !module.isEnabled()) {
            return 1.0f;
        }
        int percent = module.reduction.get();
        return Math.max(0f, 1.0f - percent / 100.0f);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/player/AutoEatModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.player;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.core.component.DataComponents;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundSetCarriedItemPacket;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.food.FoodProperties;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "AutoEat",
        description = "Eats food from the hotbar when hunger or health drops low",
        category = Category.PLAYER
)
public class AutoEatModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Integer> hungerThreshold = new Setting<>("Hunger Threshold", Integer.class, 14, 0, 20, 1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Integer> healthThreshold = new Setting<>("Health Threshold", Integer.class, 10, 0, 20, 1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> pauseOtherModulesWhileEating = new Setting<>("Pause Other Modules While Eating", Boolean.class, false);

    private int previousSlot = -1;
    private boolean eating;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        stopEating();
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        boolean hungry = player.getFoodData().getFoodLevel() < hungerThreshold.get();
        boolean hurt = player.getHealth() < healthThreshold.get();

        if (!hungry && !hurt) {
            stopEating();
            return;
        }

        Inventory inventory = player.getInventory();

        if (eating) {
            if (!player.isUsingItem()) {
                stopEating();
            }
            return;
        }

        int foodSlot = findBestFoodSlot(inventory);
        if (foodSlot < 0) {
            return;
        }

        previousSlot = inventory.getSelectedSlot();
        inventory.setSelectedSlot(foodSlot);
        player.connection.send(new ServerboundSetCarriedItemPacket(foodSlot));
        getMinecraft().gameMode.useItem(player, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
        eating = true;
    }

    private int findBestFoodSlot(Inventory inventory) {
        int bestSlot = -1;
        int bestNutrition = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
            ItemStack stack = inventory.getItem(i);
            FoodProperties food = stack.get(DataComponents.FOOD);
            if (food == null) {
                continue;
            }
            if (food.nutrition() > bestNutrition) {
                bestNutrition = food.nutrition();
                bestSlot = i;
            }
        }
        return bestSlot;
    }

    private void stopEating() {
        if (!eating) {
            return;
        }
        eating = false;

        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player != null) {
            getMinecraft().gameMode.releaseUsingItem(player);
            if (previousSlot >= 0) {
                player.getInventory().setSelectedSlot(previousSlot);
                player.connection.send(new ServerboundSetCarriedItemPacket(previousSlot));
            }
        }
        previousSlot = -1;
    }

    public boolean isPauseOtherModulesWhileEating() {
        return eating && Boolean.TRUE.equals(pauseOtherModulesWhileEating.get());
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/player/AutoRespawnModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.player;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.DeathScreen;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundClientCommandPacket;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "AutoRespawn",
        description = "Respawns automatically after dying",
        category = Category.PLAYER
)
public class AutoRespawnModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Integer> delayTicks = new Setting<>("Delay", Integer.class, 0, 0, 40, 1, () -> true);

    private int ticksDead;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        ticksDead = 0;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        boolean dead = (player != null && player.isDeadOrDying()) || getMinecraft().gui.screen() instanceof DeathScreen;

        if (!dead) {
            ticksDead = 0;
            return;
        }

        ticksDead++;
        if (ticksDead < delayTicks.get()) {
            return;
        }
        if (player != null) {
            player.connection.send(new ServerboundClientCommandPacket(ServerboundClientCommandPacket.Action.PERFORM_RESPAWN));
        }
        ticksDead = 0;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/player/AutoToolModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.player;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundSetCarriedItemPacket;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.HitResult;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "AutoTool",
        description = "Switches to the best hotbar tool before breaking a block",
        category = Category.PLAYER
)
public class AutoToolModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Boolean> preferSilkTouch = new Setting<>("Prefer Silk Touch", Boolean.class, false);
    private final Setting<Boolean> preferFortune = new Setting<>("Prefer Fortune", Boolean.class, false);
    private final Setting<Integer> avoidLowDurability = new Setting<>("Avoid Low Durability", Integer.class, 10, 0, 50, 5, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> switchBackAfter = new Setting<>("Switch Back After", Boolean.class, true);

    private int previousSlot = -1;
    private boolean switched;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        restoreSlot();
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        boolean breaking = getMinecraft().gameMode.isDestroying() || getMinecraft().options.keyAttack.isDown();
        if (!breaking) {
            restoreSlot();
            return;
        }

        HitResult hit = getMinecraft().hitResult;
        if (!(hit instanceof BlockHitResult blockHit) || hit.getType() != HitResult.Type.BLOCK) {
            restoreSlot();
            return;
        }

        BlockState state = player.level().getBlockState(blockHit.getBlockPos());
        if (state.isAir()) {
            restoreSlot();
            return;
        }

        Inventory inventory = player.getInventory();
        int currentSlot = inventory.getSelectedSlot();
        float bestSpeed = inventory.getItem(currentSlot).getDestroySpeed(state);
        int bestSlot = -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
            ItemStack stack = inventory.getItem(i);
            if (stack.isEmpty() || isLowDurability(stack)) {
                continue;
            }
            float speed = stack.getDestroySpeed(state);
            if (speed > bestSpeed) {
                bestSpeed = speed;
                bestSlot = i;
            }
        }

        if (bestSlot >= 0 && bestSlot != currentSlot) {
            if (!switched) {
                previousSlot = currentSlot;
                switched = true;
            }
            inventory.setSelectedSlot(bestSlot);
            player.connection.send(new ServerboundSetCarriedItemPacket(bestSlot));
        }
    }

    private boolean isLowDurability(ItemStack stack) {
        int percent = avoidLowDurability.get();
        if (percent <= 0 || !stack.isDamageableItem()) {
            return false;
        }
        int max = stack.getMaxDamage();
        if (max <= 0) {
            return false;
        }
        int remaining = max - stack.getDamageValue();
        return remaining * 100 / max < percent;
    }

    private void restoreSlot() {
        if (!switched) {
            return;
        }
        switched = false;

        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (switchBackAfter.get() && player != null && previousSlot >= 0) {
            player.getInventory().setSelectedSlot(previousSlot);
            player.connection.send(new ServerboundSetCarriedItemPacket(previousSlot));
        }
        previousSlot = -1;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/player/FastBreakModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.player;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.multiplayer.MultiPlayerGameMode;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "FastBreak",
        description = "Removes the pause between breaking blocks",
        category = Category.PLAYER
)
public class FastBreakModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Integer> delay = new Setting<>("Delay", Integer.class, 0, 0, 4, 1, () -> true);

    // Minecraft 26.2 ships unobfuscated, so the private field name is stable.
    private static final Field DESTROY_DELAY = resolveField();

    private static Field resolveField() {
        try {
            Field field = MultiPlayerGameMode.class.getDeclaredField("destroyDelay");
            field.setAccessible(true);
            return field;
        } catch (NoSuchFieldException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        MultiPlayerGameMode gameMode = getMinecraft().gameMode;
        if (DESTROY_DELAY == null || gameMode == null) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            int current = DESTROY_DELAY.getInt(gameMode);
            if (current > delay.get()) {
                DESTROY_DELAY.setInt(gameMode, delay.get());
            }
        } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("FastBreak could not access destroyDelay", e);
        }
    }
}

```

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.player;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "FastPlace",
        description = "Removes the delay between placing blocks and using items",
        category = Category.PLAYER
)
public class FastPlaceModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Integer> delay = new Setting<>("Delay", Integer.class, 0, 0, 4, 1, () -> true);

    // Minecraft 26.2 ships unobfuscated, so the private field name is stable.
    private static final Field RIGHT_CLICK_DELAY = resolveField();

    private static Field resolveField() {
        try {
            Field field = Minecraft.class.getDeclaredField("rightClickDelay");
            field.setAccessible(true);
            return field;
        } catch (NoSuchFieldException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        if (RIGHT_CLICK_DELAY == null) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
            int current = RIGHT_CLICK_DELAY.getInt(minecraft);
            if (current > delay.get()) {
                RIGHT_CLICK_DELAY.setInt(minecraft, delay.get());
            }
        } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("FastPlace could not access rightClickDelay", e);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/player/InventoryCleanerModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.player;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundPlayerActionPacket;
import net.minecraft.network.protocol.game.ServerboundSetCarriedItemPacket;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Inventory;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "InventoryCleaner",
        description = "Drops junk items from the hotbar",
        category = Category.PLAYER
)
public class InventoryCleanerModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<String> junkItems = new Setting<>("Junk Items", String.class, "dirt,cobblestone,rotten_flesh");
    private final Setting<Integer> keepCount = new Setting<>("Keep Count", Integer.class, 0, 0, 64, 1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Integer> delayTicks = new Setting<>("Delay", Integer.class, 5, 0, 40, 1, () -> true);

    private int ticksSinceDrop;

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        LocalPlayer player = getMinecraft().player;
        if (player == null) {
            return;
        }

        ticksSinceDrop++;
        if (ticksSinceDrop < delayTicks.get()) {
            return;
        }

        String[] junkNames = junkItems.get().split(",");
        Inventory inventory = player.getInventory();
        int selected = inventory.getSelectedSlot();

        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
            if (i == selected) {
                continue;
            }
            ItemStack stack = inventory.getItem(i);
            if (stack.isEmpty() || stack.getCount() <= keepCount.get()) {
                continue;
            }
            if (isJunk(stack, junkNames)) {
                inventory.setSelectedSlot(i);
                player.connection.send(new ServerboundSetCarriedItemPacket(i));
                player.connection.send(new ServerboundPlayerActionPacket(
                        ServerboundPlayerActionPacket.Action.DROP_ALL_ITEMS, BlockPos.ZERO, Direction.DOWN));
                inventory.setSelectedSlot(selected);
                player.connection.send(new ServerboundSetCarriedItemPacket(selected));
                ticksSinceDrop = 0;
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    private boolean isJunk(ItemStack stack, String[] junkNames) {
        String path = BuiltInRegistries.ITEM.getKey(stack.getItem()).getPath();
        for (String junkName : junkNames) {
            String trimmed = junkName.trim();
            if (!trimmed.isEmpty() && path.equals(trimmed)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/render/EspModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.render;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.ColorValue;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.Animal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.monster.Enemy;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "ESP",
        description = "Draws 2D boxes around entities through walls",
        category = Category.RENDER
)
public class EspModule extends Module {
    public enum Mode {
        BOX,
        CORNERS,
        FILLED
    }

    private final Setting<Mode> mode = new Setting<>("Mode", Mode.class, Mode.CORNERS);
    private final Setting<Boolean> targetPlayers = new Setting<>("Players", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> targetHostile = new Setting<>("Hostile", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> targetPassive = new Setting<>("Passive", Boolean.class, false);
    private final Setting<ColorValue> playerColor = new Setting<>("Player Color", ColorValue.class, new ColorValue(0xFFFF5555));
    private final Setting<ColorValue> hostileColor = new Setting<>("Hostile Color", ColorValue.class, new ColorValue(0xFFFFAA00));
    private final Setting<ColorValue> passiveColor = new Setting<>("Passive Color", ColorValue.class, new ColorValue(0xFF55FF55));
    private final Setting<Double> lineWidth = new Setting<>("Line Width", Double.class, 1.5, 0.5, 5.0, 0.5, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Integer> fillOpacity = new Setting<>("Fill Opacity", Integer.class, 40, 0, 255, 5, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> maxDistance = new Setting<>("Max Distance", Double.class, 128.0, 8.0, 512.0, 8.0, () -> true);

    public Mode getMode() {
        Mode value = mode.get();
        return value != null ? value : Mode.BOX;
    }

    public double getMaxDistance() {
        return maxDistance.get();
    }

    public int getLineWidth() {
        return Math.max(1, (int) Math.round(lineWidth.get()));
    }

    public int getFillOpacity() {
        return fillOpacity.get();
    }

    public boolean shouldRender(Entity entity) {
        if (entity instanceof Player) {
            return Boolean.TRUE.equals(targetPlayers.get());
        }
        if (entity instanceof Enemy) {
            return Boolean.TRUE.equals(targetHostile.get());
        }
        if (entity instanceof Animal) {
            return Boolean.TRUE.equals(targetPassive.get());
        }
        return false;
    }

    /** Returns the box color for an entity, or 0 if it should not be drawn. */
    public int colorFor(Entity entity) {
        if (entity instanceof Player) {
            return colorOf(playerColor);
        }
        if (entity instanceof Enemy) {
            return colorOf(hostileColor);
        }
        if (entity instanceof Animal) {
            return colorOf(passiveColor);
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private static int colorOf(Setting<ColorValue> setting) {
        ColorValue value = setting.get();
        return value != null ? value.argb() : 0xFFFFFFFF;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/render/FakeCoordsModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.render;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.hud.HudModule;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.ColorValue;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "FakeCoords",
        description = "Replaces the coordinates shown on the HUD, so screenshots and streams never leak your position",
        category = Category.RENDER
)
public class FakeCoordsModule extends Module {
    public enum Mode {
        /** Show fixed numbers you choose. */
        FIXED,
        /** Show your real position shifted by a constant offset. */
        OFFSET,
        /** Reroll plausible numbers every few seconds. */
        RANDOM,
        /** Show custom text instead of numbers. */
        TEXT
    }

    private final Setting<Mode> mode = new Setting<>("Mode", Mode.class, Mode.FIXED);

    private final Setting<Double> fixedX = new Setting<>("Fixed X", Double.class, 0.0, -100000.0, 100000.0, 100.0,
            () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> fixedY = new Setting<>("Fixed Y", Double.class, 64.0, -64.0, 320.0, 1.0,
            () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> fixedZ = new Setting<>("Fixed Z", Double.class, 0.0, -100000.0, 100000.0, 100.0,
            () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> offset = new Setting<>("Offset", Double.class, 10000.0, -100000.0, 100000.0, 500.0,
            () -> true);
    private final Setting<Integer> rerollSeconds = new Setting<>("Reroll Seconds", Integer.class, 5, 1, 60, 1,
            () -> true);

    // Text mode, with the same animation vocabulary as the watermark.
    private final Setting<String> text = new Setting<>("Text", String.class, "classified");
    private final Setting<HudModule.TextAnim> textAnim =
            new Setting<>("Text Animation", HudModule.TextAnim.class, HudModule.TextAnim.TYPEWRITER);
    private final Setting<Double> animSpeed = new Setting<>("Anim Speed", Double.class, 1.0, 0.1, 5.0, 0.1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> animDelay = new Setting<>("Anim Delay", Double.class, 1.0, 0.0, 5.0, 0.1, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Boolean> animLoop = new Setting<>("Anim Loop", Boolean.class, true);
    private final Setting<HudModule.AnimDirection> scrollDir =
            new Setting<>("Scroll Direction", HudModule.AnimDirection.class, HudModule.AnimDirection.LEFT);

    private final Setting<HudModule.ColorAnim> colorAnim =
            new Setting<>("Color Animation", HudModule.ColorAnim.class, HudModule.ColorAnim.RAINBOW);
    private final Setting<Double> colorSpeed = new Setting<>("Color Speed", Double.class, 0.5, 0.0, 3.0, 0.05,
            () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> colorSaturation = new Setting<>("Color Saturation", Double.class, 0.8, 0.0, 1.0, 0.05,
            () -> true);
    private final Setting<Double> colorBrightness = new Setting<>("Color Brightness", Double.class, 1.0, 0.2, 1.0, 0.05,
            () -> true);
    private final Setting<ColorValue> gradient1 =
            new Setting<>("Gradient Color 1", ColorValue.class, new ColorValue(0xFF55CCFF));
    private final Setting<ColorValue> gradient2 =
            new Setting<>("Gradient Color 2", ColorValue.class, new ColorValue(0xFFFF55CC));
    private final Setting<HudModule.AnimDirection> colorDir =
            new Setting<>("Color Direction", HudModule.AnimDirection.class, HudModule.AnimDirection.LEFT);

    private double randomX;
    private double randomY;
    private double randomZ;
    private long nextRerollMs;

    @Override
    protected void onEnable() {
        nextRerollMs = 0;
    }

    public Mode getMode() {
        Mode value = mode.get();
        return value != null ? value : Mode.FIXED;
    }

    public boolean isTextMode() {
        return getMode() == Mode.TEXT;
    }

    /** The line to show in place of the real coordinates. */
    public String coordinateLine(double realX, double realY, double realZ) {
        return switch (getMode()) {
            case FIXED -> format(fixedX.get(), fixedY.get(), fixedZ.get());
            case OFFSET -> format(realX + offset.get(), realY, realZ + offset.get());
            case RANDOM -> {
                reroll();
                yield format(randomX, randomY, randomZ);
            }
            case TEXT -> {
                String value = text.get();
                yield value == null || value.isEmpty() ? "classified" : value;
            }
        };
    }

    private void reroll() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (now < nextRerollMs) {
            return;
        }
        nextRerollMs = now + rerollSeconds.get() * 1000L;
        java.util.Random random = new java.util.Random();
        randomX = random.nextInt(60000) - 30000;
        randomY = 40 + random.nextInt(120);
        randomZ = random.nextInt(60000) - 30000;
    }

    private static String format(double x, double y, double z) {
        return String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.1f / %.1f / %.1f", x, y, z);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ animation accessors

    public HudModule.TextAnim getTextAnim() {
        HudModule.TextAnim value = textAnim.get();
        return value != null ? value : HudModule.TextAnim.STATIC;
    }

    public double getAnimSpeed() {
        Double value = animSpeed.get();
        return value != null ? value : 1.0;
    }

    public double getAnimDelay() {
        Double value = animDelay.get();
        return value != null ? value : 1.0;
    }

    public boolean isAnimLoop() {
        return Boolean.TRUE.equals(animLoop.get());
    }

    public HudModule.AnimDirection getScrollDir() {
        HudModule.AnimDirection value = scrollDir.get();
        return value != null ? value : HudModule.AnimDirection.LEFT;
    }

    public HudModule.ColorAnim getColorAnim() {
        HudModule.ColorAnim value = colorAnim.get();
        return value != null ? value : HudModule.ColorAnim.STATIC;
    }

    public double getColorSpeed() {
        Double value = colorSpeed.get();
        return value != null ? value : 0.5;
    }

    public float getColorSaturation() {
        Double value = colorSaturation.get();
        return (float) (value != null ? value : 0.8);
    }

    public float getColorBrightness() {
        Double value = colorBrightness.get();
        return (float) (value != null ? value : 1.0);
    }

    public int getGradient1() {
        ColorValue value = gradient1.get();
        return value != null ? value.argb() : 0xFF55CCFF;
    }

    public int getGradient2() {
        ColorValue value = gradient2.get();
        return value != null ? value.argb() : 0xFFFF55CC;
    }

    public HudModule.AnimDirection getColorDir() {
        HudModule.AnimDirection value = colorDir.get();
        return value != null ? value : HudModule.AnimDirection.LEFT;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/render/FovChangerModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.render;

import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.OptionInstance;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "FovChanger",
        description = "Overrides the field of view, including past the vanilla 110 limit",
        category = Category.RENDER
)
public class FovChangerModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Integer> fov = new Setting<>("FOV", Integer.class, 90, 30, 150, 1, () -> true);

    // The vanilla fov() OptionInstance clamps set() to 30-110, so write the private
    // value field directly (get() returns it unmodified) to allow the full range.
    private static final Field VALUE_FIELD = resolveValueField();

    private Integer originalFov;

    private static Field resolveValueField() {
        try {
            Field field = OptionInstance.class.getDeclaredField("value");
            field.setAccessible(true);
            return field;
        } catch (NoSuchFieldException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void onEnable() {
        if (getMinecraft().options != null) {
            originalFov = getMinecraft().options.fov().get();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        if (getMinecraft().options == null) {
            return;
        }
        OptionInstance<Integer> option = getMinecraft().options.fov();
        if (originalFov == null) {
            originalFov = option.get();
        }
        applyFov(option, fov.get());
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        if (originalFov == null || getMinecraft().options == null) {
            return;
        }
        applyFov(getMinecraft().options.fov(), originalFov);
        originalFov = null;
    }

    private void applyFov(OptionInstance<Integer> option, int value) {
        if (VALUE_FIELD == null) {
            option.set(value);
            return;
        }
        try {
            VALUE_FIELD.set(option, value);
        } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.warn("FovChanger could not set fov beyond the vanilla range", e);
            option.set(value);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/render/FullbrightModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.render;

import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.OptionInstance;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "Fullbright",
        description = "Overrides brightness/gamma for maximum visibility",
        category = Category.RENDER
)
public class FullbrightModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> gamma = new Setting<>("Gamma", Double.class, 10.0, 1.0, 15.0, 0.5, () -> true);

    // The gamma OptionInstance clamps set() to 0..1, so write the private value field directly
    // (get() returns it unmodified) to allow true fullbright.
    private static final Field VALUE_FIELD = resolveValueField();

    private Double originalGamma;

    private static Field resolveValueField() {
        try {
            Field field = OptionInstance.class.getDeclaredField("value");
            field.setAccessible(true);
            return field;
        } catch (NoSuchFieldException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    @Override
    protected void onEnable() {
        if (getMinecraft().options != null) {
            originalGamma = getMinecraft().options.gamma().get();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        if (getMinecraft().options == null) {
            return;
        }
        OptionInstance<Double> option = getMinecraft().options.gamma();
        if (originalGamma == null) {
            originalGamma = option.get();
        }
        applyGamma(option, gamma.get());
    }

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        if (originalGamma == null || getMinecraft().options == null) {
            return;
        }
        applyGamma(getMinecraft().options.gamma(), originalGamma);
        originalGamma = null;
    }

    private void applyGamma(OptionInstance<Double> option, double value) {
        if (VALUE_FIELD == null) {
            option.set(value);
            return;
        }
        try {
            VALUE_FIELD.set(option, value);
        } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
            Lenrete.LOGGER.warn("Fullbright could not set gamma beyond the vanilla range", e);
            option.set(value);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/module/impl/world/GhostHandModule.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.world;

import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Category;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.Module;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleMetadata;
import dev.dd.lenrete.notification.ToastManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.setting.Setting;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.MenuProvider;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.BlockHitResult;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

@ModuleMetadata(
        name = "GhostHand",
        description = "Open chests and other containers through walls by aiming at them",
        category = Category.WORLD
)
public class GhostHandModule extends Module {
    private final Setting<Double> reach = new Setting<>("Reach", Double.class, 5.0, 2.0, 20.0, 0.5, () -> true);
    private final Setting<Integer> cooldown = new Setting<>("Cooldown", Integer.class, 6, 0, 20, 1, () -> true);

    private boolean useWasDown;
    private int ticksSinceUse;

    @Override
    protected void onDisable() {
        useWasDown = false;
        ticksSinceUse = 0;
    }

    @Override
    public void onTick() {
        ticksSinceUse++;
        Minecraft minecraft = getMinecraft();
        LocalPlayer player = minecraft.player;
        if (player == null || minecraft.level == null || minecraft.gui.screen() != null) {
            useWasDown = false;
            return;
        }

        boolean useDown = minecraft.options.keyUse.isDown();

        if (useDown && !useWasDown && ticksSinceUse >= cooldown.get()) {
            if (openContainerThroughWall(minecraft, player)) {
                ticksSinceUse = 0;
            }
        }
        useWasDown = useDown;
    }

    /** Raycasts past intervening blocks to find the first container in view and interacts with it. */
    private boolean openContainerThroughWall(Minecraft minecraft, LocalPlayer player) {
        Level level = minecraft.level;
        Vec3 eye = player.getEyePosition();
        Vec3 look = player.getViewVector(1.0f);
        double maxReach = reach.get();

        BlockPos previous = null;
        // Step along the look vector; the first block position that holds a container wins,
        // even if solid blocks sit between it and the player.
        for (double d = 0.0; d <= maxReach; d += 0.2) {
            Vec3 point = eye.add(look.scale(d));
            BlockPos pos = BlockPos.containing(point);
            if (pos.equals(previous)) {
                continue;
            }
            previous = pos;

            BlockEntity blockEntity = level.getBlockEntity(pos);
            if (!(blockEntity instanceof MenuProvider)) {
                continue;
            }

            // Approach face is whichever axis the ray is travelling most against.
            Direction face = Direction.getApproximateNearest(-look.x, -look.y, -look.z);
            BlockHitResult hit = new BlockHitResult(point, face, pos, false);
            minecraft.gameMode.useItemOn(player, InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND, hit);
            player.swing(InteractionHand.MAIN_HAND);
            ToastManager.show("GhostHand: opening container");
            return true;
        }

        ToastManager.show("GhostHand: no container in view");
        return false;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/notification/ToastManager.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.notification;

import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.misc.ClickGuiModule;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.hud.HudElementRegistry;
import net.minecraft.client.DeltaTracker;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor;
import net.minecraft.resources.Identifier;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.Iterator;

public final class ToastManager {
    private static final int MAX_VISIBLE = 5;
    private static final int MARGIN = 8;
    private static final int TOAST_HEIGHT = 20;
    private static final int TOAST_SPACING = 4;
    private static final int TOAST_MAX_WIDTH = 220;
    private static final int COLOR_PANEL = 0xE0121417;
    private static final int COLOR_BORDER = 0x30FFFFFF;
    private static final int COLOR_TEXT = 0xE8EAED;

    private static final Deque<ToastEntry> ACTIVE = new ArrayDeque<>();

    private ToastManager() {
    }

    public static void init() {
        HudElementRegistry.addLast(Identifier.fromNamespaceAndPath(Lenrete.MOD_ID, "toasts"), ToastManager::render);
    }

    public static void show(String message) {
        ACTIVE.addFirst(new ToastEntry(message, System.currentTimeMillis()));
        while (ACTIVE.size() > MAX_VISIBLE) {
            ACTIVE.removeLast();
        }
    }

    private static void render(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, DeltaTracker deltaTracker) {
        if (ACTIVE.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        Minecraft client = Minecraft.getInstance();
        ClickGuiModule settings = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(ClickGuiModule.class);
        ToastPosition position = settings != null ? settings.getToastPosition() : ToastPosition.BOTTOM_RIGHT;
        int durationMs = settings != null ? settings.getToastDurationMs() : 3000;
        int fadeMs = Math.max(1, settings != null ? settings.getToastFadeMs() : 200);

        boolean right = position == ToastPosition.BOTTOM_RIGHT || position == ToastPosition.TOP_RIGHT;
        boolean bottom = position == ToastPosition.BOTTOM_RIGHT || position == ToastPosition.BOTTOM_LEFT;

        int width = g.guiWidth();
        int height = g.guiHeight();
        int y = bottom ? height - MARGIN - TOAST_HEIGHT : MARGIN;
        int step = bottom ? -(TOAST_HEIGHT + TOAST_SPACING) : (TOAST_HEIGHT + TOAST_SPACING);

        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        Iterator<ToastEntry> iterator = ACTIVE.iterator();
        int index = 0;
        while (iterator.hasNext()) {
            ToastEntry entry = iterator.next();
            long age = now - entry.createdAtMs();
            long lifetime = (long) durationMs + fadeMs;
            if (age > lifetime) {
                iterator.remove();
                continue;
            }

            double alpha;
            if (age < fadeMs) {
                alpha = age / (double) fadeMs;
            } else if (age > durationMs) {
                alpha = 1.0 - (age - durationMs) / (double) fadeMs;
            } else {
                alpha = 1.0;
            }
            alpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha));

            int textWidth = client.font.width(entry.message());
            int toastWidth = Math.min(TOAST_MAX_WIDTH, textWidth + 16);
            int x = right ? width - MARGIN - toastWidth : MARGIN;
            int rowY = y + index * step;

            fill(g, x, rowY, toastWidth, TOAST_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(COLOR_PANEL, alpha));
            outline(g, x, rowY, toastWidth, TOAST_HEIGHT, applyAlpha(COLOR_BORDER, alpha));

            g.enableScissor(x + 4, rowY, x + toastWidth - 4, rowY + TOAST_HEIGHT);
            g.text(client.font, entry.message(), x + 8, rowY + (TOAST_HEIGHT - client.font.lineHeight) / 2, applyAlpha(COLOR_TEXT, alpha));
            g.disableScissor();

            index++;
        }
    }

    private static void fill(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int x, int y, int width, int height, int color) {
        g.fill(x, y, x + width, y + height, color);
    }

    private static void outline(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int x, int y, int width, int height, int color) {
        g.horizontalLine(x, x + width - 1, y, color);
        g.horizontalLine(x, x + width - 1, y + height - 1, color);
        g.verticalLine(x, y, y + height - 1, color);
        g.verticalLine(x + width - 1, y, y + height - 1, color);
    }

    private static int applyAlpha(int color, double alpha) {
        int baseAlpha = (color >>> 24) == 0 ? 0xFF : (color >>> 24);
        int scaled = (int) Math.round(baseAlpha * alpha);
        return (scaled << 24) | (color & 0x00FFFFFF);
    }

    private record ToastEntry(String message, long createdAtMs) {
    }
}

```

```java
    BOTTOM_RIGHT,
    BOTTOM_LEFT,
    TOP_RIGHT,
    TOP_LEFT
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/render/EspRenderer.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.render;

import dev.dd.lenrete.Lenrete;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.ModuleManager;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.render.EspModule;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.render.NametagsModule;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.render.ItemEspModule;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.render.StorageEspModule;
import dev.dd.lenrete.module.impl.world.ChunkBordersModule;
import net.fabricmc.fabric.api.client.rendering.v1.hud.HudElementRegistry;
import net.minecraft.client.DeltaTracker;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor;
import net.minecraft.client.player.LocalPlayer;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.resources.Identifier;
import net.minecraft.core.Direction;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.item.ItemEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.ChunkAccess;
import net.minecraft.world.level.chunk.LevelChunk;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.ChestBlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.EnderChestBlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;

public final class EspRenderer {
    private static final EspRenderer INSTANCE = new EspRenderer();
    private static final Direction[] HORIZONTAL_DIRECTIONS = {Direction.NORTH, Direction.SOUTH, Direction.EAST, Direction.WEST};

    private EspRenderer() {
    }

    public static void init() {
        HudElementRegistry.addLast(Identifier.fromNamespaceAndPath(Lenrete.MOD_ID, "esp"), INSTANCE::render);
    }

    private void render(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, DeltaTracker deltaTracker) {
        try {
            float partialTick = deltaTracker.getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(true);
            renderOverlays(g, partialTick);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            // A single bad frame must never take the game down.
            Lenrete.LOGGER.error("ESP overlay render failed", e);
        }
    }

    private void renderOverlays(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, float partialTick) {
        Minecraft client = Minecraft.getInstance();
        LocalPlayer player = client.player;
        if (player == null || client.level == null || client.getDebugOverlay().showDebugScreen()) {
            return;
        }

        EspModule esp = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(EspModule.class);
        NametagsModule nametags = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(NametagsModule.class);
        StorageEspModule storage = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(StorageEspModule.class);
        ItemEspModule itemEsp = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(ItemEspModule.class);
        ChunkBordersModule chunkBorders = ModuleManager.getInstance().getModule(ChunkBordersModule.class);

        if (chunkBorders != null && chunkBorders.isEnabled()) {
            drawChunkBorders(g, client, player, chunkBorders);
        }

        if (storage != null && storage.isEnabled()) {
            drawStorageEsp(g, client, player, storage);
        }

        boolean espOn = esp != null && esp.isEnabled();
        boolean nametagsOn = nametags != null && nametags.isEnabled();
        boolean itemEspOn = itemEsp != null && itemEsp.isEnabled();
        if (!espOn && !nametagsOn && !itemEspOn) {
            return;
        }

        for (Entity entity : client.level.entitiesForRendering()) {
            if (itemEspOn && entity instanceof ItemEntity item) {
                double d = player.distanceTo(item);
                if (d <= itemEsp.getMaxDistance()) {
                    drawItemEsp(g, client, itemEsp, item, interpolatedPosition(item, partialTick), d);
                }
                continue;
            }

            if (!(entity instanceof LivingEntity living) || entity == player || !living.isAlive()) {
                continue;
            }

            double distance = player.distanceTo(living);

            // Interpolate the entity's rendered position with the frame's partial tick so
            // the box tracks smoothly between server ticks instead of snapping each tick.
            Vec3 renderPos = interpolatedPosition(living, partialTick);
            AABB tickBox = living.getBoundingBox();
            AABB box = tickBox.move(renderPos.subtract(living.position()));

            if (espOn && esp.shouldRender(living) && distance <= esp.getMaxDistance()) {
                Projection.ScreenBox screen = Projection.project(box);
                if (screen != null) {
                    drawEspBox(g, esp, screen, esp.colorFor(living));
                }
            }

            if (nametagsOn && nametags.shouldRender(living) && distance <= nametags.getMaxDistance()) {
                Vec3 tagPos = new Vec3(box.minX + (box.maxX - box.minX) / 2, box.maxY + 0.4, box.minZ + (box.maxZ - box.minZ) / 2);
                Projection.ScreenPoint point = Projection.projectPoint(tagPos);
                if (point != null) {
                    drawNametag(g, client, nametags, living, point, distance);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static Vec3 interpolatedPosition(Entity entity, float partialTick) {
        double x = entity.xOld + (entity.getX() - entity.xOld) * partialTick;
        double y = entity.yOld + (entity.getY() - entity.yOld) * partialTick;
        double z = entity.zOld + (entity.getZ() - entity.zOld) * partialTick;
        return new Vec3(x, y, z);
    }

    private void drawStorageEsp(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Minecraft client, LocalPlayer player, StorageEspModule module) {
        int color = module.getColor();
        double maxDistance = module.getMaxDistance();
        double maxSq = maxDistance * maxDistance;

        int chunkRadius = (int) Math.ceil(maxDistance / 16.0);
        int centerChunkX = Math.floorDiv(player.blockPosition().getX(), 16);
        int centerChunkZ = Math.floorDiv(player.blockPosition().getZ(), 16);

        // Collect matching containers first so adjacent chests (double chests) can be merged.
        java.util.List<BlockPos> containers = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        java.util.Set<Long> chestKeys = new java.util.HashSet<>();

        for (int cx = centerChunkX - chunkRadius; cx <= centerChunkX + chunkRadius; cx++) {
            for (int cz = centerChunkZ - chunkRadius; cz <= centerChunkZ + chunkRadius; cz++) {
                if (!client.level.hasChunk(cx, cz)) {
                    continue;
                }
                ChunkAccess chunk = client.level.getChunk(cx, cz);
                if (!(chunk instanceof LevelChunk levelChunk)) {
                    continue;
                }
                for (BlockEntity blockEntity : levelChunk.getBlockEntities().values()) {
                    if (!module.shouldRender(blockEntity)) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    BlockPos pos = blockEntity.getBlockPos();
                    if (player.distanceToSqr(pos.getX() + 0.5, pos.getY() + 0.5, pos.getZ() + 0.5) > maxSq) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    containers.add(pos.immutable());
                    if (isDoubleChestPart(blockEntity)) {
                        chestKeys.add(pos.asLong());
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        java.util.Set<Long> drawn = new java.util.HashSet<>();
        for (BlockPos pos : containers) {
            long key = pos.asLong();
            if (drawn.contains(key)) {
                continue;
            }
            drawn.add(key);

            AABB box = new AABB(pos);
            if (chestKeys.contains(key)) {
                // Merge with an adjacent chest half so a double chest draws as one box.
                for (net.minecraft.core.Direction dir : HORIZONTAL_DIRECTIONS) {
                    BlockPos neighbor = pos.relative(dir);
                    long neighborKey = neighbor.asLong();
                    if (chestKeys.contains(neighborKey) && !drawn.contains(neighborKey)) {
                        box = union(box, new AABB(neighbor));
                        drawn.add(neighborKey);
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            Projection.ScreenBox screen = Projection.project(box);
            if (screen != null) {
                drawStyledBox(g, screen, color, storageStyle(module), 1, module.getFillOpacity());
            }
        }
    }

    private void drawItemEsp(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Minecraft client, ItemEspModule module, ItemEntity item, Vec3 renderPos, double distance) {
        AABB box = item.getBoundingBox().move(renderPos.subtract(item.position()));
        int color = module.getColor();

        Projection.ScreenBox screen = Projection.project(box);
        if (screen != null) {
            EspModule.Mode mode = switch (module.getStyle()) {
                case CORNERS -> EspModule.Mode.CORNERS;
                case FILLED -> EspModule.Mode.FILLED;
                default -> EspModule.Mode.BOX;
            };
            drawStyledBox(g, screen, color, mode, 1, module.getFillOpacity());
        }

        String label = itemLabel(module, item.getItem(), distance);
        if (label.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        Vec3 tagPos = new Vec3(box.minX + (box.maxX - box.minX) / 2, box.maxY + 0.3, box.minZ + (box.maxZ - box.minZ) / 2);
        Projection.ScreenPoint point = Projection.projectPoint(tagPos);
        if (point == null) {
            return;
        }

        int textWidth = client.font.width(label);
        int x = clampCoord(point.x(), g.guiWidth()) - textWidth / 2;
        int y = clampCoord(point.y(), g.guiHeight()) - client.font.lineHeight - 2;
        int bg = module.getBackgroundOpacity() << 24;
        g.fill(x - 2, y - 1, x + textWidth + 2, y + client.font.lineHeight + 1, bg);
        g.text(client.font, label, x, y, color | 0xFF000000);
    }

    private static String itemLabel(ItemEspModule module, ItemStack stack, double distance) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        if (module.isShowName()) {
            sb.append(stack.getHoverName().getString());
        }
        if (module.isShowCount() && stack.getCount() > 1) {
            sb.append(" x").append(stack.getCount());
        }
        if (module.isShowEnchants() && stack.isEnchanted()) {
            sb.append(" ✦");
        }
        if (module.isShowDurability() && stack.isDamaged()) {
            sb.append(" ").append(stack.getMaxDamage() - stack.getDamageValue()).append("/").append(stack.getMaxDamage());
        }
        if (module.isShowDistance()) {
            if (sb.length() > 0) {
                sb.append(" ");
            }
            sb.append((int) distance).append("m");
        }
        return sb.toString().trim();
    }

    private static boolean isDoubleChestPart(BlockEntity blockEntity) {
        // Only ordinary/trapped chests form double chests; ender chests never do.
        return blockEntity instanceof ChestBlockEntity && !(blockEntity instanceof EnderChestBlockEntity);
    }

    private static AABB union(AABB a, AABB b) {
        return new AABB(
                Math.min(a.minX, b.minX), Math.min(a.minY, b.minY), Math.min(a.minZ, b.minZ),
                Math.max(a.maxX, b.maxX), Math.max(a.maxY, b.maxY), Math.max(a.maxZ, b.maxZ));
    }

    private void drawEspBox(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, EspModule esp, Projection.ScreenBox box, int color) {
        drawStyledBox(g, box, color, esp.getMode(), esp.getLineWidth(), esp.getFillOpacity());
    }

    private void drawStyledBox(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Projection.ScreenBox box, int color, EspModule.Mode mode, int width, int fillOpacity) {
        int x1 = clampCoord(box.minX(), g.guiWidth());
        int y1 = clampCoord(box.minY(), g.guiHeight());
        int x2 = clampCoord(box.maxX(), g.guiWidth());
        int y2 = clampCoord(box.maxY(), g.guiHeight());

        // Keep distant targets visible: enforce a minimum on-screen box size.
        int minSize = 6;
        if (x2 - x1 < minSize) {
            int cx = (x1 + x2) / 2;
            x1 = cx - minSize / 2;
            x2 = cx + minSize / 2;
        }
        if (y2 - y1 < minSize) {
            int cy = (y1 + y2) / 2;
            y1 = cy - minSize / 2;
            y2 = cy + minSize / 2;
        }
        if (x2 <= x1 || y2 <= y1) {
            return;
        }

        switch (mode) {
            case FILLED -> {
                g.fill(x1, y1, x2, y2, (fillOpacity << 24) | (color & 0x00FFFFFF));
                drawBoxOutlinePixels(g, x1, y1, x2, y2, color, width);
            }
            case CORNERS -> drawCornerBrackets(g, x1, y1, x2, y2, color, width);
            default -> drawBoxOutlinePixels(g, x1, y1, x2, y2, color, width);
        }
    }

    private void drawBoxOutlinePixels(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2, int color, int width) {
        g.fill(x1, y1, x2, y1 + width, color);
        g.fill(x1, y2 - width, x2, y2, color);
        g.fill(x1, y1, x1 + width, y2, color);
        g.fill(x2 - width, y1, x2, y2, color);
    }

    /** CS:GO-style corner brackets: short L-shaped marks at each corner instead of a full box. */
    private void drawCornerBrackets(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2, int color, int width) {
        int boxW = x2 - x1;
        int boxH = y2 - y1;
        int len = Math.max(2, Math.min(boxW, boxH) / 4);

        // Top-left.
        g.fill(x1, y1, x1 + len, y1 + width, color);
        g.fill(x1, y1, x1 + width, y1 + len, color);
        // Top-right.
        g.fill(x2 - len, y1, x2, y1 + width, color);
        g.fill(x2 - width, y1, x2, y1 + len, color);
        // Bottom-left.
        g.fill(x1, y2 - width, x1 + len, y2, color);
        g.fill(x1, y2 - len, x1 + width, y2, color);
        // Bottom-right.
        g.fill(x2 - len, y2 - width, x2, y2, color);
        g.fill(x2 - width, y2 - len, x2, y2, color);
    }

    private static EspModule.Mode storageStyle(StorageEspModule module) {
        return switch (module.getStyle()) {
            case BOX -> EspModule.Mode.BOX;
            case FILLED -> EspModule.Mode.FILLED;
            default -> EspModule.Mode.CORNERS;
        };
    }

    private void drawNametag(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Minecraft client, NametagsModule nametags, LivingEntity living, Projection.ScreenPoint point, double distance) {
        StringBuilder label = new StringBuilder(living.getName().getString());
        if (nametags.isShowHealth()) {
            label.append(" ").append((int) living.getHealth()).append("hp");
        }
        if (nametags.isShowDistance()) {
            label.append(" ").append((int) distance).append("m");
        }

        String text = label.toString();
        int textWidth = client.font.width(text);
        int x = clampCoord(point.x(), g.guiWidth()) - textWidth / 2;
        int y = clampCoord(point.y(), g.guiHeight()) - client.font.lineHeight - 2;

        int bg = (nametags.getBackgroundOpacity() << 24);
        g.fill(x - 2, y - 1, x + textWidth + 2, y + client.font.lineHeight + 1, bg);
        g.text(client.font, text, x, y, 0xFFFFFFFF);
    }

    private void drawChunkBorders(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, Minecraft client, LocalPlayer player, ChunkBordersModule module) {
        int minX = Math.floorDiv(player.blockPosition().getX(), 16) * 16;
        int minZ = Math.floorDiv(player.blockPosition().getZ(), 16) * 16;
        int maxX = minX + 16;
        int maxZ = minZ + 16;
        int color = module.getColor();

        // Limit the vertical span to a window around the player: full world-height lines
        // break whenever the camera sits between their endpoints.
        int bottom = Math.max(client.level.getMinY(), (int) player.getY() - 32);
        int top = Math.min(client.level.getMaxY(), (int) player.getY() + 32);

        double[][] corners = {{minX, minZ}, {maxX, minZ}, {maxX, maxZ}, {minX, maxZ}};

        if (module.isVerticalLines()) {
            for (double[] corner : corners) {
                drawWorldSegments(g, corner[0], bottom, corner[1], corner[0], top, corner[1], color);
            }
        }

        int y = (int) Math.floor(player.getY());
        for (int i = 0; i < corners.length; i++) {
            double[] a = corners[i];
            double[] b = corners[(i + 1) % corners.length];
            drawWorldSegments(g, a[0], y, a[1], b[0], y, b[1], color);
        }
    }

    /** Samples points along a world-space line and connects consecutive visible ones, so a line
     * that partly crosses behind the camera still renders its visible portion. */
    private void drawWorldSegments(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, double x1, double y1, double z1, double x2, double y2, double z2, int color) {
        double length = Math.sqrt(sqr(x2 - x1) + sqr(y2 - y1) + sqr(z2 - z1));
        int segments = Math.max(1, Math.min(96, (int) Math.ceil(length / 2.0)));

        Projection.ScreenPoint previous = null;
        for (int i = 0; i <= segments; i++) {
            double t = (double) i / segments;
            Projection.ScreenPoint current = Projection.projectPoint(new Vec3(
                    x1 + (x2 - x1) * t, y1 + (y2 - y1) * t, z1 + (z2 - z1) * t));
            if (previous != null && current != null) {
                drawLine(g, previous.x(), previous.y(), current.x(), current.y(), color);
            }
            previous = current;
        }
    }

    private static double sqr(double v) {
        return v * v;
    }

    private void drawLine(GuiGraphicsExtractor g, double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2, int color) {
        if (!Double.isFinite(x1) || !Double.isFinite(y1) || !Double.isFinite(x2) || !Double.isFinite(y2)) {
            return;
        }

        int width = g.guiWidth();
        int height = g.guiHeight();
        // Clip both endpoints so a point projected far off-screen (a block near the camera
        // plane blows up to huge coordinates) cannot create a multi-million-pixel line that
        // hangs the render pipeline.
        int margin = 64;
        double[] clipped = clipLine(x1, y1, x2, y2, -margin, -margin, width + margin, height + margin);
        if (clipped == null) {
            return;
        }

        double cx1 = clipped[0];
        double cy1 = clipped[1];
        double cx2 = clipped[2];
        double cy2 = clipped[3];

        double dx = cx2 - cx1;
        double dy = cy2 - cy1;
        int steps = (int) Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)));
        if (steps <= 0) {
            g.fill((int) Math.round(cx1), (int) Math.round(cy1), (int) Math.round(cx1) + 1, (int) Math.round(cy1) + 1, color);
            return;
        }
        steps = Math.min(steps, width + height + 4 * margin);

        double stepX = dx / steps;
        double stepY = dy / steps;
        for (int i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
            int px = (int) Math.round(cx1 + stepX * i);
            int py = (int) Math.round(cy1 + stepY * i);
            g.fill(px, py, px + 1, py + 1, color);
        }
    }

    private static int clampCoord(double value, int max) {
        if (!Double.isFinite(value)) {
            return value < 0 ? -64 : max + 64;
        }
        return (int) Math.round(Math.max(-64, Math.min(max + 64, value)));
    }

    /** Liang–Barsky line clip against an axis-aligned rectangle; null if fully outside. */
    private static double[] clipLine(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2, double xMin, double yMin, double xMax, double yMax) {
        double dx = x2 - x1;
        double dy = y2 - y1;
        double t0 = 0.0;
        double t1 = 1.0;
        double[] p = {-dx, dx, -dy, dy};
        double[] q = {x1 - xMin, xMax - x1, y1 - yMin, yMax - y1};

        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            if (p[i] == 0) {
                if (q[i] < 0) {
                    return null;
                }
            } else {
                double t = q[i] / p[i];
                if (p[i] < 0) {
                    if (t > t1) {
                        return null;
                    }
                    if (t > t0) {
                        t0 = t;
                    }
                } else {
                    if (t < t0) {
                        return null;
                    }
                    if (t < t1) {
                        t1 = t;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return new double[]{x1 + t0 * dx, y1 + t0 * dy, x1 + t1 * dx, y1 + t1 * dy};
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/render/Projection.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.render;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB;
import net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;
import org.joml.Vector4f;

/**
 * Projects world-space positions to 2D screen (gui-scaled) coordinates so ESP
 * overlays can be drawn on the HUD layer without touching the 3D world-render
 * pipeline.
 *
 * <p>The camera's view-rotation-projection matrix maps a camera-relative point
 * into clip space; after the perspective divide (x/w, y/w) the result is in
 * Normalized Device Coordinates (x,y in [-1,1], y pointing up). Points with a
 * non-positive w are behind the camera and must be discarded.
 */
public final class Projection {
    private Projection() {
    }

    public static ScreenBox project(AABB box) {
        double minX = Double.MAX_VALUE;
        double minY = Double.MAX_VALUE;
        double maxX = -Double.MAX_VALUE;
        double maxY = -Double.MAX_VALUE;
        boolean anyVisible = false;

        double[][] corners = {
                {box.minX, box.minY, box.minZ}, {box.maxX, box.minY, box.minZ},
                {box.minX, box.maxY, box.minZ}, {box.maxX, box.maxY, box.minZ},
                {box.minX, box.minY, box.maxZ}, {box.maxX, box.minY, box.maxZ},
                {box.minX, box.maxY, box.maxZ}, {box.maxX, box.maxY, box.maxZ}
        };

        for (double[] corner : corners) {
            ScreenPoint p = projectPoint(new Vec3(corner[0], corner[1], corner[2]));
            if (p == null) {
                continue;
            }
            minX = Math.min(minX, p.x());
            minY = Math.min(minY, p.y());
            maxX = Math.max(maxX, p.x());
            maxY = Math.max(maxY, p.y());
            anyVisible = true;
        }

        if (!anyVisible) {
            return null;
        }
        return new ScreenBox(minX, minY, maxX, maxY);
    }

    public static ScreenPoint projectPoint(Vec3 world) {
        Minecraft minecraft = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (minecraft.gameRenderer == null) {
            return null;
        }

        Vec3 cameraPos = minecraft.gameRenderer.mainCamera().position();
        Matrix4f matrix = minecraft.gameRenderer.mainCamera().getViewRotationProjectionMatrix(new Matrix4f());

        float relX = (float) (world.x - cameraPos.x);
        float relY = (float) (world.y - cameraPos.y);
        float relZ = (float) (world.z - cameraPos.z);

        Vector4f clip = matrix.transform(relX, relY, relZ, 1.0f, new Vector4f());
        if (clip.w() <= 1.0e-4f) {
            return null;
        }

        double ndcX = clip.x() / clip.w();
        double ndcY = clip.y() / clip.w();

        int width = minecraft.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth();
        int height = minecraft.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight();
        double screenX = (ndcX * 0.5 + 0.5) * width;
        double screenY = (0.5 - ndcY * 0.5) * height;
        return new ScreenPoint(screenX, screenY);
    }

    public record ScreenBox(double minX, double minY, double maxX, double maxY) {
    }

    public record ScreenPoint(double x, double y) {
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/setting/Setting.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.setting;

import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.function.BooleanSupplier;

public class Setting<T> {
    private final String name;
    private final Class<T> type;
    private final T defaultValue;
    private final T min;
    private final T max;
    private final T step;
    private final BooleanSupplier visible;
    private T value;

    public Setting(String name, Class<T> type, T defaultValue) {
        this(name, type, defaultValue, null, null, null, () -> true);
    }

    public Setting(String name, Class<T> type, T defaultValue, T min, T max, T step, BooleanSupplier visible) {
        this.name = Objects.requireNonNull(name, "name");
        this.type = Objects.requireNonNull(type, "type");
        this.defaultValue = defaultValue;
        this.value = defaultValue;
        this.min = min;
        this.max = max;
        this.step = step;
        this.visible = visible == null ? () -> true : visible;
    }

    public String getName() {
        return name;
    }

    public Class<T> getType() {
        return type;
    }

    public T get() {
        return value;
    }

    public void set(T value) {
        this.value = validate(value);
    }

    public T getDefaultValue() {
        return defaultValue;
    }

    public boolean isVisible() {
        return visible.getAsBoolean();
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({"unchecked", "rawtypes"})
    private T validate(T next) {
        if (next == null) {
            return defaultValue;
        }

        if (next instanceof Number && min instanceof Number && max instanceof Number) {
            double value = ((Number) next).doubleValue();
            double lower = ((Number) min).doubleValue();
            double upper = ((Number) max).doubleValue();
            double clamped = Math.max(lower, Math.min(upper, value));

            if (type == Integer.class) {
                return type.cast((int) Math.round(clamped));
            }
            if (type == Double.class) {
                return type.cast(clamped);
            }
        }

        if (type.isEnum() && next instanceof Enum) {
            return next;
        }

        return type.cast(next);
    }

    public T getMin() {
        return min;
    }

    public T getMax() {
        return max;
    }

    public T getStep() {
        return step;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/dd/lenrete/util/MovementKeys.java`:

```java
package dev.dd.lenrete.util;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import net.minecraft.client.KeyMapping;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.Options;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Input;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;

/** Reads the raw movement keys directly from GLFW, so movement can be polled even while a screen is open. */
public final class MovementKeys {
    private static final Field KEY_FIELD = resolveKeyField();

    private MovementKeys() {
    }

    private static Field resolveKeyField() {
        try {
            Field field = KeyMapping.class.getDeclaredField("key");
            field.setAccessible(true);
            return field;
        } catch (NoSuchFieldException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    public static Input poll(Minecraft minecraft) {
        Options options = minecraft.options;
        return new Input(
                isDown(minecraft, options.keyUp),
                isDown(minecraft, options.keyDown),
                isDown(minecraft, options.keyLeft),
                isDown(minecraft, options.keyRight),
                isDown(minecraft, options.keyJump),
                isDown(minecraft, options.keyShift),
                isDown(minecraft, options.keySprint));
    }

    public static boolean isDown(Minecraft minecraft, KeyMapping mapping) {
        int code = keyCode(mapping);
        return code != InputConstants.UNKNOWN.getValue() && InputConstants.isKeyDown(minecraft.getWindow(), code);
    }

    private static int keyCode(KeyMapping mapping) {
        if (KEY_FIELD != null) {
            try {
                InputConstants.Key key = (InputConstants.Key) KEY_FIELD.get(mapping);
                if (key != null) {
                    return key.getValue();
                }
            } catch (IllegalAccessException ignored) {
                // Fall back to the default binding below.
            }
        }
        return mapping.getDefaultKey().getValue();
    }
}

```
